    # CADCAMFUN v6 — Overview and Task Board
    
    Status: draft v1 · Language of all code, comments, docs and commit messages: **English**.
    
    ## 1. Goal
    
    Rebuild the CAD/CAM desktop application described in `README.md` from scratch as a **native desktop app** on:
    
    | Concern | Choice |
    | --- | --- |
    | Runtime / package manager / test runner | **Bun** (`bun install`, `bun test`) |
    | Application logic | **Effect v4**, pinned to exactly `4.0.0-rc.112` workspace-wide (required by `@nikcli-ai/sdk-next`; one copy only, so Layers/`Context` tags are shared) |
    | Persistence | **Drizzle ORM + SQLite** (`drizzle-orm@1.0.0-beta.22`, `bun:sqlite`) |
    | Desktop shell | **Tauri v2** (Rust shell + webview) |
    | UI | **SolidJS** + Vite + TypeScript, visually identical to the legacy React UI; Three.js for the viewport |
    | AI | **nikcli** embedded host (`@nikcli-ai/sdk-next`) as base and provider; **AI SDK v7** (`ai`, `@ai-sdk/*`) for direct/one-shot calls |
    
    The previous Next.js + Prisma + Stripe/Better Auth codebase is kept, **read-only and git-ignored**, in `.reference/`. Use it as a source of domain knowledge (types, algorithms, UI behaviour). Do not import from it and do not copy code verbatim without reviewing it; several modules contain bugs (see `04-core-cam.md`).
    
    ## 2. Decision: Tauri, not Electron
    
    The stack requires `bun:sqlite` and Effect running in-process with Drizzle. Electron's main process is Node, which has no `bun:sqlite`; we would have to swap in `better-sqlite3`/`libsql` (native rebuilds per Electron ABI). With Tauri, the application logic runs as a **Bun-compiled sidecar** (`bun build --compile`), so the exact stack above runs unchanged, and the Rust shell stays small (window, tray, menus, updater, secure storage, sidecar lifecycle). Rust 1.96 is installed; the Tauri CLI comes from `@tauri-apps/cli` (no `cargo install` needed).
    
    ## 3. Repository layout
    
    ```
    .reference/            legacy code, git-ignored, read-only
    specs/                 these documents (source of truth for scope and contracts)
    packages/
      db/                  @cadcamfun/db      Drizzle schema, migrations, Effect Database service   [DONE]
      core/                @cadcamfun/core    pure CAD geometry + CAM/G-code domain (no I/O)
      api/                 @cadcamfun/api     schema-first HttpApi definitions + shared domain schemas
      ai/                  @cadcamfun/ai      AI layer: nikcli sdk-next host + AI SDK v7 (Effect services)
      plugin-sdk/          @cadcamfun/plugin-sdk  public plugin API types + host contract
      vcs/                 @cadcamfun/vcs     git-compatible history: objects, branches, merge, diff, GitHub sync
      workflow/            @cadcamfun/workflow  Actions-style workflow schema, triggers, runner
    apps/
      sidecar/             @cadcamfun/sidecar Bun Effect server (HttpApi handlers, services, wiring)
      desktop/             @cadcamfun/desktop Vite + React UI and src-tauri/ (Rust shell)
      mobile/              @cadcamfun/mobile  iOS companion (React Native + Expo Router), thin client of a paired desktop
    ```
    
    Dependency direction (no cycles): `core` ← `api` ← `sidecar`; `db` ← `sidecar`; `ai`, `plugin-sdk` ← `sidecar`; `api` (types only) ← `desktop`. `core` and `api` must not import `db`.
    
    ## 3b. Remaining work (roadmap)
    
    **Sequence of remaining work** lives in `specs/roadmap/README.md` (RM-00–RM-11). When an agent is told to follow **the roadmap**, that folder plus `AGENTS.md` § Roadmap override the older phase lists in `20-integral-roadmap.md` / `21-integration-master-plan.md` for **order only**. Design contracts stay in `specs/02`–`27`.

Cross-WP needs go through the contracts in the specs; if a contract must change, edit the spec first and note it under "Change log" at the bottom of that spec.

    | WP14 | Multi-operation setups (many tools, many programs) | `packages/core/src/cam/program` (+ `post`, `analysis`), `packages/api/src/groups/cam-setup.ts`, `apps/sidecar/src/http/cam-setup.ts`, `apps/desktop/src/features/cam/{lib,store}/setup*` | `14-multi-op-setup.md` | WP1, WP2, WP3 | **in progress** — `cam.program` contracts, plan/generate, post tool-number override, multi-tool parsing done; desktop setup store and persistence pending |
    
    ### Parallelisation map
    
    Wave 1 (no dependencies, start together): **WP1, WP8, WP5 (skeleton with a stub sidecar)**.
    Wave 2: **WP2, WP3** (after WP1 types land), **WP6/WP7** against mocked API data.
    Wave 3: **WP4** (wires WP0 + WP2 + WP3 + WP8), then **WP9**, **WP10**.
    
    Agents in the same wave never touch the same directory, so they can run concurrently without merge conflicts. The only shared files are `package.json` workspaces, `tsconfig.base.json`, and `bun.lock`; only the integrating agent changes those, and adding a new package's own `package.json` is allowed.
    
    ## 5. Definition of done (every WP)
    
    1. `bun install` at the repo root succeeds.
    2. `bunx tsc -p <package>/tsconfig.json` exits 0 (strict, `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`).
    3. `bun test <package dir>` passes; new behaviour has tests; bug fixes have a regression test.
    4. Public surface documented in the WP's spec (update it if the surface changed).
    5. No imports from `.reference/`, no `any` in exported signatures, no `process.env` reads outside the config module.
    6. Conventions in `01-conventions.md` followed.
    
    ## 6. Scope and phasing
    
    - **Parity requirement (owner decision)**: the v6 backend exposes the **same HTTP API as the legacy app** — all 195 route files in `specs/api-inventory.md`, same paths, methods, payloads and status codes — and the UI is **visually and functionally identical to the legacy UI, re-implemented in SolidJS**. This supersedes the earlier "deferred" list below: auth, organizations, marketplace, billing, notifications, chat, MCP, ML and analytics routes are all in scope, implemented on SQLite/Drizzle. Where a route depends on a cloud service (Stripe, Polar, email, OAuth), implement it behind an Effect service with a local/no-op default and keep the HTTP contract identical.
    - **v6.0 (this plan)**: local-first desktop app — projects, 2D drawing, 3D viewport, toolpaths, G-code generation/export, materials/tools/machine configs library, cost estimates, AI assistant (bring-your-own key), plugin host, auto-update.
    - **Previously deferred (now in scope via the parity requirement above; build last, after the core flows)**: cloud sync, organizations and invitations, marketplace, Stripe billing/subscriptions, Better Auth (passkeys, 2FA, magic link), push notifications, chat. Their tables exist in `packages/db` so a later phase can reuse them, but nothing in v6.0 may depend on them.
    - Local identity in v6.0: a single local profile row in `User`; no login screen.
    
    ## 7. Open questions (decide before WP9/WP10)
    
    1. Cloud sync backend: reuse the old `cadcamfun.xyz` API, or design a new one? (Affects WP10 only.) Partly answered by `13-vcs-actions-mobile.md`: project history syncs through a per-project GitHub mirror, no custom server.
    2. Plugin sandboxing: run plugins in a Bun `Worker` (recommended) vs. separate processes.
    3. ~~Keep Stripe billing at all in v6.x, or drop licensing to a signed license-key check?~~ **Decided: Stripe.** Billing goes through the optional `PaymentProvider` (`STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`, `STRIPE_PRICE_*`); there is no license-key path.
    4. Does the nikcli host (`sdk-next`, ~20 MB, 60 deps) survive `bun build --compile` inside the sidecar, or must it run as a separate process? (Tracked in `08-ai.md` §6; decided by WP8/WP4.)
    
    ## 8. Change log
    
    - v1: initial specs.
    - v2: WP13–WP13 done; WP11/WP12 core + sidecar + API in place.
    - v3: added WP14 (multi-operation setups: one setup → many operations → many programs, with a tool table and tool changes) and its spec `14-multi-op-setup.md`.
    - v4: remaining work sequenced in `specs/roadmap/` (RM-00–RM-11); `AGENTS.md` requires following it when the roadmap is cited.
    # Conventions (apply to every WP)
    
    Reference for Effect v4 (pinned to `4.0.0-rc.112`): `packages/db/node_modules/effect/ai-docs/src/` (Bun does not hoist; any workspace package's `node_modules/effect/ai-docs/src/` works). Read the relevant folder before writing Effect code; it is the authoritative, version-matched documentation and may differ from the stable 4.0.0 docs. Do **not** use v3 idioms from memory — the API changed (see §2). Do not bump `effect` in any single package; see `08-ai.md` §6.3.
    
    ## 1. General
    
    - English only: identifiers, comments, docs, commit messages, UI strings (UI strings go through the i18n table, default locale `en`).
    - TypeScript strict. No `any` in exported signatures; prefer `unknown` + Schema decoding at boundaries.
    - ES modules, relative imports include the `.ts` extension (`allowImportingTsExtensions` is on).
    - Files `kebab-case.ts`; one concept per file; tests next to source as `*.test.ts` using `bun:test`.
    - Comments explain *why*, not *what*. Keep modules small and pure where possible.
    - Units: **millimetres and radians internally**; convert only at the UI/G-code boundary. Angles in degrees never cross a module boundary.
    - Numbers in G-code are formatted with a fixed number of decimals (default 3), never via implicit `toString`.
    
    ## 2. Effect v4 rules
    
    - Services: `class X extends Context.Service<X, Shape>()("cadcamfun/X") { static layer = ... }`. Shape is an interface of methods returning `Effect`.
    - Functions that return an Effect and benefit from tracing: `Effect.fn("Module.name")(function*(...) { ... })`; hot paths: `Effect.fnUntraced`. Do not write functions that merely wrap and return `Effect.gen`.
    - Errors that cross a process boundary (HTTP) are `Schema.TaggedError`. Internal-only errors may use `Data.TaggedError`. Never throw in Effect code; map foreign exceptions with `Effect.try`/`Effect.tryPromise` into a tagged error. Handle with `Effect.catchTag`/`Effect.catchTags`.
    - Decode all external input with `Schema.decodeUnknownEffect(schema)`. Domain models are `Schema.Class` (validated construction) or `Schema.Struct`.
    - Resources use `Effect.acquireRelease` inside a `Layer`; the Layer owns the lifecycle, callers never close handles.
    - Configuration is read once in `apps/sidecar/src/config.ts` via Effect `Config`; everything else receives config through a service.
    - Pure geometry/CAM code (`packages/core`) has **no Effect runtime dependency in hot loops**: algorithms are plain functions over readonly arrays; Effect/Schema wrap them only at the edges (validation, errors).
    - Tests: plain `bun:test`; run effects with `Effect.runPromise` and provide layers explicitly. Use `:memory:` SQLite for DB tests (see `packages/db/src/database.test.ts`).
    
    ## 3. Database rules (Drizzle)
    
    - Only `packages/db` talks to SQLite. Other packages use repository services exported from `apps/sidecar` or the `Database` service.
    - Schema changes: edit `packages/db/src/schema.ts` → `bun run db:generate` → commit the generated SQL in `packages/db/drizzle/`. Never edit generated SQL by hand.
    - Table and column names are kept identical to the legacy Prisma SQLite database.
    - All queries go through `Database.query(operation, run)` so failures become `DatabaseError`.
    
    ## 4. Git and agent workflow
    
    - Do not commit unless asked. Do not edit files outside your WP (see `00-overview.md` §4).
    - Never read, print or copy values from `.env*` files.
    - Before finishing: typecheck, test, and update the WP spec's "Status" and "Change log".
    
    ## 5. Error and logging policy
    
    - Log with `Effect.log*` and annotate with `Effect.annotateLogs`; no `console.*` outside entrypoints.
    - User-facing errors are typed, have a stable `_tag`, and a human message in English.

    $ cat apps/desktop/src/features/cam/lib/saved-program.ts apps/desktop/src/features/cam/components/SaveCamModal.tsx apps/sidecar/src/services/cam-program.ts packages/api/src/cam-program.ts
    import { Schema } from "effect"
    import { cam } from "@cadcamfun/core"
    import { CamKernelArtifact, CamKernelRequest, CamProgramDocument, type ToolpathRow } from "@cadcamfun/api"
    import type { AppState } from "../../../shared/state/context.ts"
    import { toJsonSafe } from "../../../shared/state/json-safe.ts"
    import { drawingPayload } from "../../cad/lib/projectSnapshot.ts"
    import { useCADStore } from "../../cad/store/cadStore.ts"
    import { useCamStore } from "../store/camStore.ts"
    import { useGeneratorStore } from "../store/generatorStore.ts"
    import { useVerifyStore } from "../store/verifyStore.ts"
    import { makeToolpathGenerator } from "../api/generator.ts"
    
    /** A portable program is also the JSON stored in the existing Toolpath.data column. */
    export const SavedCamProgram = CamProgramDocument
    export type SavedCamProgram = typeof SavedCamProgram.Type
    
    export const snapshotCamProgram = async (app: AppState, gcode: string): Promise<SavedCamProgram> => {
      const store = useCamStore.getState(), generator = useGeneratorStore.getState()
      const artifact = store.kernelArtifact?.nc === gcode && cam.kernel.sha256(gcode) === store.kernelArtifact.identity.nc ? store.kernelArtifact : undefined
      const request = artifact ? store.kernelRequest : undefined
      const stock = artifact ? {
        ...useCADStore.getState().workpiece,
        width: artifact.stockBounds.max.x - artifact.stockBounds.min.x, height: artifact.stockBounds.max.y - artifact.stockBounds.min.y, depth: artifact.stockBounds.max.z - artifact.stockBounds.min.z,
        x: (artifact.stockBounds.max.x + artifact.stockBounds.min.x) / 2, y: (artifact.stockBounds.max.y + artifact.stockBounds.min.y) / 2
      } : useCADStore.getState().workpiece
      const drawing = drawingPayload(app)
      if (request?.stock) drawing["stock"] = request.stock
      const snapshot = structuredClone(toJsonSafe({ settings: generator.settings, printer: generator.printer, stock, drawing, ...(request ? { kernelRequest: request } : {}) })) as Record<string, unknown>
      // Parse the captured bytes, never the delayed editor preview, which may belong to earlier NC.
      const program = artifact?.program ?? (await app.runtime.run(makeToolpathGenerator(app.client).parse(gcode))).program
      return Schema.decodeUnknownSync(SavedCamProgram)({ format: "cadcamfun-cam", version: 1, gcode, program, ...snapshot, ...(artifact ? { kernelArtifact: artifact } : {}) })
    }
    
    const record = (value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> => value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? value as Record<string, unknown> : {}
    
    /** Keep free-form metadata, but never attach earlier verification to changed program bytes. */
    export const savedProgramData = (previous: unknown, snapshot: SavedCamProgram): Record<string, unknown> => {
      const data = { ...record(previous) }
      for (const key of ["kernelArtifact", "kernelRequest", "verification", "response", "sourceRevision"]) delete data[key]
      const old = record(previous), oldArtifact = record(old["kernelArtifact"])
      if (snapshot.kernelArtifact && oldArtifact["nc"] === snapshot.gcode && typeof old["sourceRevision"] === "string") data["sourceRevision"] = old["sourceRevision"]
      return { ...data, ...Schema.encodeSync(SavedCamProgram)(snapshot) }
    }
    
    export const restoreCamProgram = async (app: AppState, row: Pick<ToolpathRow, "id" | "name" | "gcode" | "data" | "projectId" | "drawingId"> & Partial<Pick<ToolpathRow, "description" | "isPublic">>, current: () => boolean): Promise<void> => {
      const code = row.gcode ?? "", data = record(row.data)
      let snapshot: SavedCamProgram | undefined
      if (data["format"] === "cadcamfun-cam") snapshot = Schema.decodeUnknownSync(SavedCamProgram)(data)
      let artifact: cam.kernel.CamArtifact | undefined
      if (data["kernelArtifact"] !== undefined) {
        try {
          const decoded = Schema.decodeUnknownSync(CamKernelArtifact)(data["kernelArtifact"])
          if (decoded.nc === code && decoded.identity.nc === cam.kernel.sha256(code) && decoded.release !== "blocked") artifact = decoded
        } catch { /* Older stored metadata can still be opened as ordinary G-code. */ }
      }
      const program = artifact?.program ?? (snapshot?.gcode === code ? snapshot.program : (await app.runtime.run(makeToolpathGenerator(app.client).parse(code))).program)
      if (!current()) return
      const store = useCamStore.getState(), generator = useGeneratorStore.getState()
      store.clearOperationResults()
      useVerifyStore.getState().reset()
      store.setKernelArtifact(artifact)
      store.setKernelRequest(data["kernelRequest"] !== undefined ? Schema.decodeUnknownSync(CamKernelRequest)(data["kernelRequest"]) : undefined)
      store.setSavedProgram(row.id ? { id: row.id, name: row.name, projectId: row.projectId, drawingId: row.drawingId, description: row.description ?? null, isPublic: row.isPublic ?? false } : undefined)
      store.setGcode(code)
      store.setProgram(program)
      store.setActiveTab("editor")
      if (snapshot) {
        useGeneratorStore.setState({ settings: snapshot.settings, printer: snapshot.printer })
        useCADStore.getState().setWorkpiece({ ...useCADStore.getState().workpiece, ...snapshot.stock, configured: true })
        store.setWorkpiece({ x: snapshot.stock.width, y: snapshot.stock.height, z: snapshot.stock.depth })
        store.setStockAllowance(snapshot.stock.allowance)
      }
      generator.setResult(code, [])
    }
    
    export const downloadCamFile = (contents: string, filename: string, type: string): void => {
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([contents], { type }))
      const link = document.createElement("a")
      try { link.href = url; link.download = filename; document.body.appendChild(link); link.click() }
      finally { link.remove(); URL.revokeObjectURL(url) }
    }

    /** One save surface for the toolbar, ribbon and generator, backed by the existing project/library API. */
    export default function SaveCamModal(props: { readonly code: string; readonly extension: string; readonly projectOnly?: boolean; readonly onClose: () => void }): JSX.Element {
      const app = useApp(), store = useCamStore()
      const saved = store.savedProgram
      const owner = captureEditorOwner()
      const [name, setName] = createSignal(saved?.name ?? `toolpath-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`)
      const [mode, setMode] = createSignal<"existing" | "new" | "update">(saved ? "update" : owner.projectId ? "existing" : "new")
      const [projectId, setProjectId] = createSignal(owner.projectId ?? "")
      const [projectName, setProjectName] = createSignal(useDocumentStore.getState().name ?? "")
      const [showProject, setShowProject] = createSignal(props.projectOnly ?? false)
      const [format, setFormat] = createSignal("gcode")
      const [description, setDescription] = createSignal(saved?.description ?? "")
      const [organizationId, setOrganizationId] = createSignal("")
      const [isPublic, setIsPublic] = createSignal(saved?.isPublic ?? false)
      const [organizations, setOrganizations] = createSignal<ReadonlyArray<{ readonly id: string; readonly name: string }>>([])
      const [projects, setProjects] = createSignal<ReadonlyArray<{ readonly id: string; readonly name: string }>>([])
      const [loading, setLoading] = createSignal(true)
      const [busy, setBusy] = createSignal(false)
      const [error, setError] = createSignal<string>()
      const controller = new AbortController()
      let disposed = false
      // Retain the created destination on failure: a retry must not create duplicate projects.
      let createdTarget: { readonly projectId: string; readonly drawingId: string } | undefined
      onCleanup(() => { disposed = true; controller.abort() })
      onMount(() => {
        void app.runtime.run(app.client.projects.list(), { signal: controller.signal }).then(
          (rows) => { if (!disposed) setProjects(rows.map(({ id, name }) => ({ id, name }))) },
          (cause) => { if (!disposed) setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause)) }
        ).finally(() => { if (!disposed) setLoading(false) })
        void app.runtime.run(app.client.organizations.list(), { signal: controller.signal }).then(
          (rows) => { if (!disposed) setOrganizations(rows.map(({ id, name }) => ({ id, name }))) },
          (cause) => { if (!disposed) setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause)) }

      const exportToolpath = async (): Promise<void> => {

        } catch (cause) { if (!disposed) setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause)) }

            if (!createdTarget) {
              const project = await app.runtime.run(app.client.projects.create({ payload: { name: newName, description: description().trim() || null, organizationId: organizationId() || null, isPublic: isPublic() } }))
              // Remember the project immediately, including when drawing creation fails.
              createdTarget = { projectId: project.id, drawingId: "" }

          { label: t("save.elements"), value: elements.elements.length },
          { label: t("save.ncLines"), value: props.code.split("\n").length },
          { label: t("save.dataSize"), value: `${new TextEncoder().encode(props.code).length.toLocaleString()} B` },
          { label: t("save.toolpaths"), value: program.toolpaths.length },
          { label: t("save.moves"), value: program.toolpaths.reduce((sum, path) => sum + path.moves.length, 0) },
          { label: t("save.tools"), value: artifact ? artifact.tools.map((tool) => `T${tool.number} ${tool.name}`).join(", ") : [...new Set(program.toolpaths.map((path) => path.toolId))].join(", ") || t("save.none") },
          { label: t("save.operations"), value: artifact?.operations.map((operation) => operation.name).join(", ") || t("save.notSet") },
          { label: t("save.material"), value: artifact?.material.name ?? cad.workpiece.material ?? t("save.notSet") },
          { label: t("save.machine"), value: artifact?.machine.name ?? t("save.notSet") },
          { label: t("save.stock"), value: artifact ? `${artifact.stockBounds.max.x - artifact.stockBounds.min.x} × ${artifact.stockBounds.max.y - artifact.stockBounds.min.y} × ${artifact.stockBounds.max.z - artifact.stockBounds.min.z} mm` : `${cad.workpiece.width} × ${cad.workpiece.height} × ${cad.workpiece.depth} mm` },
          { label: t("save.units"), value: program.units },
          { label: t("save.workOffset"), value: program.workOffset },
          { label: t("save.verification"), value: artifact?.release ?? (store.kernelArtifact ? t("save.staleNc") : t("save.unverified")) },
          ...(artifact ? [
            { label: t("save.cycleTime"), value: `${artifact.cycleTimeSeconds} s` },
            { label: t("save.claims"), value: artifact.claims.map((claim) => `${claim.id}: ${claim.status}`).join(", ") },
            { label: t("save.issues"), value: artifact.issues.map((issue) => issue.message).join("; ") || t("save.none") },
            { label: t("save.ncIdentity"), value: artifact.identity.nc }

          ...(saved ? [{ label: t("save.savedProgram"), value: saved.id }] : [])

      return <>
        <WorkspaceExportDialog open={!showProject()} title={t("cam.save.exportTitle")} name={name()} onName={setName} format={format()} onFormat={setFormat}
          formats={[{ id: "gcode", label: `G-code (${props.extension})` }, { id: "toolpath", label: t("cam.save.formatToolpath") }]}
          projectBody={t("cam.save.projectBody")} onProject={() => setShowProject(true)} onClose={close} busy={busy()} error={error()}
          onExport={() => { if (format() === "toolpath") void exportToolpath(); else { downloadCamFile(props.code, `${filename()}${props.extension}`, "text/plain"); props.onClose() } }} />
        <WorkspaceProjectDialog open={showProject()} name={projectTitle()} onName={setProjectName} nameReadOnly={mode() !== "new" || !!createdTarget}
          description={description()} onDescription={setDescription} organizationId={organizationId()} onOrganization={setOrganizationId} isPublic={isPublic()} onPublic={setIsPublic}
          organizations={organizations()} summary={summary()} busy={busy()} error={error()} onSubmit={() => void save()}
          canSave={!!name().trim() && (mode() === "existing" ? !!projectId() && !loading() : mode() === "new" ? !!projectName().trim() : !!saved)}
          onClose={() => { if (!busy()) { if (props.projectOnly) props.onClose(); else setShowProject(false) } }}
          controls={<>
            <label class="mb-4 block space-y-1 text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300"><span>{t("cam.save.destination")}</span><select class={FIELD} name="camSaveDestination" disabled={busy()} value={mode()} onChange={(event) => setMode(event.currentTarget.value as "existing" | "new" | "update")}>
              <Show when={saved}><option value="update">{t("cam.save.update")}</option></Show><option value="existing">{t("cam.save.existing")}</option><option value="new">{t("cam.save.new")}</option>
            </select></label>

"" : projectId()} disabled={busy() || loading()} onChange={(event) => setProjectId(event.currentTarget.value)}>
              <option value="">{t(loading() ? "cam.save.loading" : "cam.save.chooseProject")}</option><For each={projects()}>{(project) => <option value={project.id}>{project.name}</option>}</For>
            </select></label></Show>
            <label class="mb-4 block space-y-1 text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300"><span>{t("cam.save.name")}</span><input class={FIELD} name="camProgramName" value={name()} disabled={busy()} onInput={(event) => setName(event.currentTarget.value)} /></label>

    import { BadRequest, CamKernelResult, CamProgramDocument, type CamKernelRequest } from "@cadcamfun/api"
    import { cam } from "@cadcamfun/core"
    import { Effect, Schema } from "effect"
    import { elementsOf } from "./ai-elements.ts"
    
    const record = (value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> => value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? value as Record<string, unknown> : {}
    
    /** Preserve the existing stored-operation revision algorithm while sharing it with HTTP saves. */
    export const drawingRevision = (loaded: { readonly id: string; readonly elements: ReadonlyArray<Record<string, unknown>>; readonly data: Record<string, unknown> }): string => {
      const canonical = (value: unknown): unknown => Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(canonical) : value !== null && typeof value === "object"
        ? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value).sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b)).map(([key, child]) => [key, canonical(child)])) : value
      return createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify(canonical({ drawingId: loaded.id, elements: loaded.elements, stock: loaded.data["stock"] ?? null }))).digest("hex")
    }
    
    /** The same request adapter serves preview generation and persistence revalidation. */
    export const runKernelRequest = Effect.fn("cam.runKernelRequest")(function*(payload: CamKernelRequest) {
      const machine = payload.machine !== undefined ? cam.kernel.findMachine(payload.machine) : undefined
      if (payload.machine !== undefined && machine === undefined) return yield* new BadRequest({ message: `Unknown machine preset '${payload.machine}'` })
      const result = yield* Effect.try({ try: () => {
        const meshes = payload.stock !== undefined ? cam.model.partMeshes(payload.elements, { tolerance: payload.tolerance ?? 0.01 }) : undefined
        const bottom = meshes !== undefined ? Math.min(...meshes.meshes.map((mesh) => cam.model.meshBounds(mesh).min[2])) : 0
        const stock = payload.stock
        return cam.kernel.runJob({
          schemaVersion: payload.schemaVersion, id: payload.id, name: payload.name, material: payload.material,
          part: { kind: "elements", elements: payload.elements }, ...(machine !== undefined ? { machine } : {}),
          ...(stock !== undefined ? { stock: { min: { x: stock.x - stock.width / 2, y: stock.y - stock.height / 2, z: bottom }, max: { x: stock.x + stock.width / 2, y: stock.y + stock.height / 2, z: bottom + stock.depth } } } : {}),
          ...(payload.origin !== undefined ? { origin: payload.origin } : {}), ...(payload.workOffset !== undefined ? { workOffset: payload.workOffset } : {}),
          quality: { ...(payload.finishAllowance !== undefined ? { finishAllowance: payload.finishAllowance } : {}), ...(payload.scallop !== undefined ? { scallop: payload.scallop } : {}), ...(payload.tolerance !== undefined ? { tolerance: payload.tolerance } : {}), ...(payload.resolution !== undefined ? { resolution: payload.resolution } : {}) }
        })

    })

    /** Validate recognized new payloads without changing the legacy free-form data contract. */
    export const prepareCamProgramData = Effect.fn("cam.prepareProgramData")(function*(input: {
      readonly data: unknown; readonly gcode: string | null | undefined; readonly previous?: unknown;

        // Existing editor/API updates may send only new NC and the unchanged old document.

    import { cam } from "@cadcamfun/core"
    import { CamKernelArtifact, CamKernelRequest } from "./cam-kernel.ts"
    
    /** Portable export and Toolpath.data use one contract; NC and IR travel together. */
    export const CamProgramDocument = Schema.Struct({
      format: Schema.Literal("cadcamfun-cam"), version: Schema.Literal(1), gcode: Schema.String,
      program: cam.ProgramSchema, settings: cam.generator.ToolpathSettingsSchema, printer: cam.generator.PrinterExtrasSchema,
      stock: cam.generator.StockSchema, drawing: Schema.Record(Schema.String, Schema.Unknown),

    $ cat specs/14-multi-op-setup.md; rg -n 'toolpath|Toolpath|operation|Operation|Change log' specs/06-desktop-ui.md specs/05-api-sidecar.md specs/02-database.md specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md; cat apps/desktop/src/features/toolpaths/screens/ToolpathDetailScreen.tsx
    # WP14 — Multi-operation setups (many tools, many programs)
    
    Status: **in progress**. Owner dirs: `packages/core/src/cam/program` (+ `post`, `analysis`), `packages/api/src/groups/cam-setup.ts`, `apps/sidecar/src/http/cam-setup.ts`, `apps/desktop/src/features/cam/{lib/setup.ts,store/setupStore.ts}`. Depends on WP2 (operations, posts, verifier) and WP3 (API contract).
    
    ## 1. The problem
    
    The v6 generator takes one operation per request (`POST /api/toolpaths/generate` → `GenerateRequest { settings, geometry }`), and the desktop multi-toolpath panel stitches the resulting texts together (`combineGCode`, `apps/desktop/src/features/cam/lib/multiToolpath.ts`). Concatenating full programs is not a program: each block carries its own header, `G21`, `M30` and `%`, no tool change is emitted, and no pocket number is assigned. The machine cannot run it.
    
    ## 2. The model
    
    ```
    Setup        = stock + machine + post config + grouping policy + ordered Operations
    Operation    = element ids + strategy/parameters + one Tool (+ optional pocket)
    ToolTable    = pocket (T1..Tn) -> Tool (library reference), derived from the operations
    Program      = one emitted .nc grouping operations; tool changes between them
    ```
    
    ```
    Operations ──► planPrograms ──► PlannedProgram[] ──► generateSetup ──► GeneratedProgram[] (text + IR)
                    (pure, no      (grouping, pockets,    (per operation: the existing
                     geometry)      ordering, numbering)     generator, then ONE emit per program)
    ```
    
    Two things the plan deliberately keeps apart:
    
    - **`single` grouping** — one program, tool changes inside it (what most machines want).
    - **`perTool` / `perOperation` grouping** — one file per tool / per operation (operator preference, machine memory limits).
    
    Both come from the same planner, so the UI can offer all of them without a second code path.
    
    ## 3. Contracts (`packages/core/src/cam/program`)
    
    ```ts
    // op.ts
    Operation = { id, name, elementIds: string[], params: ToolpathSettings, tool: Tool,
                  pocket?: int, enabled: boolean, fromPreviousStock: boolean }
    
    // setup.ts
    Setup = { id, name, machineType, workOffset, stock: Stock, post: PostConfig,
              grouping: "single" | "perTool" | "perOperation",
              retractBeforeToolChange: boolean, operations: Operation[] }
    PostConfig = { postId: "generic" | "fanuc" | "grbl", decimals, useCannedCycles, arcMode,
                   header, tolerance, lineNumbers?, stamp?, programNumberBase }
    
    // tooltable.ts
    ToolTableEntry = { pocket: number, tool: Tool, operationIds: string[] }
    buildToolTable(operations): { entries: ToolTableEntry[], warnings: string[] }
    assignPockets(operations): Map<operationId, pocket>      // explicit pocket, else first use
    
    // plan.ts
    PlannedProgram = { name, programNumber, toolNumbers: number[], operations: string[] }
    SetupPlan      = { programs: PlannedProgram[], toolTable: ToolTableEntry[],
                        toolLoads: number, toolChanges: number, warnings: string[] }
    planPrograms(setup): SetupPlan                            // pure: no geometry, no clock
    
    // programs.ts
    GeneratedProgram = { name, programNumber, gcode, program: Program, stats: GcodeStats,
                         warnings: string[], operationIds: string[], toolNumbers: number[] }
    GeneratedSetup   = { programs: GeneratedProgram[], toolTable: ToolTableEntry[],
                         totals: { programs, toolLoads, toolChanges, cutLength, rapidLength, estimatedSeconds },
                         warnings: string[] }
    generateSetup(setup, geometryOf: (op) => GeometryInput): GeneratedSetup
    ```
    
    Semantics:
    
    - **One emit per program.** Each operation is generated through the existing generator (`cam.generator.generateToolpath`), its IR moves become one `Toolpath` of a single `Program`, and that program is emitted **once** by the post — which already writes `G80`/`M5`/`M9`/`T<n> M6`/`G43 H<n>`/`M3 S…`/coolant per tool change (`post/emitter.ts`). No string concatenation anywhere.
    - **`toolId` is the Tool identity** (library id when there is one): `validate(program, machine)` already reports `MissingTool` against `machine.tools` by id. The pocket number is emission metadata, passed as the new `PostOptions.toolNumbers` override so a carousel's T numbers are honoured instead of first-use numbering.
    - **Ordering.** The setup order is the machining order. Within a `perTool` group the operations stay in setup order; `planPrograms` warns when a tool appears in non-adjacent operations (an avoidable extra tool change) and exposes `reorderByTool(operations)` for the UI to fix it.
    - **Retract before a tool change** (`retractBeforeToolChange`): raises Z to the highest Z the program reached before `M5`/`M9`/`T… M6`. Opt-in: the goldens of `04-core-cam.md` must not move.
    - **Program numbers.** `post.programNumberBase` (default 1000) + index; written into `metadata.programNumber` (read by the fanuc `O` number) and used in the program name.
    - **`fromPreviousStock`.** An operation that continues the tool before it gets that operation's real diameter as `restToolDiameter` instead of the hand-typed one, when it is a rest/rest-finishing `3d_surface` pass. It assumes the previous operation cut at the same Z levels with the same allowance (documented limit of `04-core-cam.md` §12); a height-field carryover between operations is out of scope.
    - **Validation.** `validateSetup(setup)` returns issues (duplicate pocket, an operation without elements, rpm above `tool.maxRpm`, a group with no enabled operation). It is reported, not thrown: the UI shows problems next to the offending row.
    
    ## 4. API (`packages/api`)
    
    New group `camSetup` (additive; the legacy routes of `05-api-sidecar.md` are untouched):
    
    | Route | Body | Result |
    | --- | --- | --- |
    | `POST /api/cam/setups/plan` | `{ setup }` | `SetupPlan` (dry run, no geometry needed) |
    | `POST /api/cam/setups/generate` | `{ setup }` | `GeneratedSetup` |
    | `POST /api/cam/setups/validate` | `{ setup }` | `{ issues }` |
    
    `generate` resolves `elementIds` on the caller side (the request carries the resolved elements), because the core is I/O-free.
    
    ## 5. As built
    
    - `program/{op,setup,tooltable,plan,programs}.ts`, exported as `cam.program`.
    - `PostOptions.toolNumbers?: Record<string, number>` and `PostOptions.retractBeforeToolChange?: boolean` in `post/types.ts`; `emitProgram` applies both. Defaults keep the existing output byte-identical.
    - `analyseGcode` reports one `Toolpath` per tool change (the reference interpreter now returns tool changes with their line), so a multi-tool `.nc` parsed back keeps its tools for the viewport, the verifier and the AI tools.
    - Desktop (`setupStore`) and DB persistence follow in the next steps of this WP.
    
    ## 6. Change log
    
    - v1: contracts and semantics (this document).
    specs/02-database.md:26:  - exposes `db` (Drizzle handle) and `query(operation, run)` which lifts any Drizzle call into `Effect<A, DatabaseError>`.
    specs/02-database.md:44:- Before pending migrations (or a legacy Drizzle journal upgrade) on an existing file, SQLite `VACUUM INTO` creates a standalone, WAL-consistent snapshot. New files and `:memory:` do not need backups; current databases do not create backups on startup. Names include a UTC timestamp and collision-resistant suffix; only this database's last five snapshots are retained. Backup or retention failure aborts startup as `DatabaseError` with operation `backup`.
    specs/02-database.md:45:- Migration planning/execution failures use operation `migrate`. All pending SQL and journal creation/upgrades run in one SQLite transaction; failure preserves the original logical schema, data, and journal (not byte-identical file contents). WP0 uses Drizzle's installed migration-file reader and name-based pending semantics, with an atomic runner because the installed migrator creates/upgrades its journal outside its migration transaction. Migration SQL must not contain transaction control or non-transactional operations such as `VACUUM`.
    specs/02-database.md:53:3. **Index on foreign keys**: Prisma did not create FK indexes either; add indexes for hot lookups (`Project.ownerId`, `Toolpath.projectId`, `Drawing.projectId`) in a new migration once query patterns are known.

    specs/05-api-sidecar.md:33:**Hard requirement:** every route in `specs/api-inventory.md` (195 files under `/api/*`) is implemented with the same path, methods, request/response JSON shapes and status codes as the legacy Next.js handlers in `.reference/src/pages/api`. Read each legacy handler before implementing it; the inventory lists the Prisma models each touches (same-named Drizzle tables). Routes the legacy code gated with `withApiAuth`/`withSubscription` keep that gating (v6 maps the local profile to an always-valid session; plan gating reads the local `Subscription` row). Group the routes into one `HttpApiGroup` per top-level segment (`ai`, `analytics`, `auth`, `components`, `drawings`, `library`, `machine-configs`, `marketplace`, `materials`, `mcp`, `notifications`, `organizations`, `plugins`, `production-costs`, `projects`, `toolpaths`, `tools`, `user`, …) in separate files under `packages/api/src/groups/`. Streaming routes (`/api/ai/*stream*`, `/api/mcp*`, `/api/websocket`) keep their wire format (SSE/WebSocket). Webhook and cron routes (`/api/webhooks/stripe`, `/api/marketplace/webhooks/polar`, `/api/cron/*`) are implemented with signature verification stubs behind services. Next.js-only routes (`/api/og*`, `/api/route`, `/api/graphql-playground`) are served as static/HTML equivalents.
