    specs/05-api-sidecar.md:92:## 5. Change log
    specs/05-api-sidecar.md:95:- 2026-10-14: owner-requested additive kernel integration: authenticated `POST /api/toolpaths/manufacture` takes a versioned explicit CAD snapshot with material, machine preset, stock/world origin and quality settings, returning a schema-validated kernel result with exact emitted NC, program, claims and identity. This stateless endpoint enriches the existing generator group and does not mutate saved drawings or toolpaths.
    specs/05-api-sidecar.md:101:- Toolpath verification: `POST /api/toolpaths/verify` in the `toolpathGenerator` group takes `cam.verify.VerifyRequestSchema` (`gcode` or `program`, `stock`, `tool`, `fixtures`, optional `target.solids`, `resolution`, `tolerance`) and answers `VerifyResponseSchema`. A problem with the *model* (both or neither of `gcode` / `program`, degenerate stock) is a 200 with `ok: false` and an `InvalidInput` issue; a schema-invalid body is a 400. The AI `ToolHandlers` gained `listOperations`, `updateOperation`, `suggestParameters` and `verifyOperation`, which share one loader for a stored operation (`operation()` in `services/ai.ts`). Test helpers: `makeApp().toolHandlers()` returns the real handlers over the same in-memory database.
    specs/05-api-sidecar.md:105:- AI workflow endpoints expose assistant prompts, conversation titles, design analysis, machining parameter recommendations, G-code optimization proposals and contextual design suggestions. G-code optimization is advisory and never updates a stored operation.
    specs/05-api-sidecar.md:110:Owner-requested CAM persistence alignment (2026-10-14): `CamProgramDocument` is the shared versioned portable/saved program schema, reusing core IR/settings/stock and `CamKernelRequest`/artifact. Existing `Toolpath.data` and `ToolpathVersion.data` JSON columns store it; no migration is needed. Sidecar create/update validates this recognized format and exact duplicated NC. Native evidence supplied by the desktop is accepted only after rerunning its captured kernel request and matching the artifact/NC/IR; existing server-generated originals may be retained unchanged. Edited NC loses earlier native/verification evidence. The sidecar owns source-revision attribution to a matching stored drawing; client revisions cannot certify changed geometry or stock. Native project writes require the same owner/admin/manager scope as AI writes (create/createForDrawing add `Forbidden`); legacy nonportable payload behavior is retained. Version creation requires toolpath write access. Owners include API shared schemas/groups/codegen outputs, sidecar CAM adapter/toolpath handlers, their tests, and desktop CAM/AI snapshot consumers. Atomic execution receipts remain RM-03.
    specs/05-api-sidecar.md:114:The existing drawing repository owns stock persistence. `configureStock` updates the latest drawing JSON and invalidates generated G-code, responses and verification on its operations inside one synchronous SQLite transaction. Unrelated drawing geometry, operation metadata and chain information survive. The AI handler checks project access and validates stock before invoking this method. No new storage or duplicate drawing model is introduced.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:11:The owner explicitly requests enriching the current CADCanvas, CAM components and sidecar API, preserving existing generators. This vertical owns `packages/api/src/cam-kernel.ts`, the existing `toolpathGenerator` group and generated clients; `apps/sidecar/src/http/toolpath-generator.ts`; the desktop CAM API, job builder, generator panel, i18n and matching tests; and the CAD renderer transform/cache fixes and matching tests. It adds authenticated, stateless `POST /api/toolpaths/manufacture` over an explicit CAD snapshot and the current kernel. The response carries the actual NC, IR, operations, tools, stock metrics, claims, release level and job/NC identity. No stored document is changed by generation. Existing toolpath storage remains the save interface; this integration adds no DB migration or new geometric algorithm. Generated results are published only while the source geometry, settings and editor owner remain current, and previous verification evidence is invalidated when a different program is loaded.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:13:The desktop adds a native-kernel section beside the existing operation generator. It uses selected CAD solids, material grades and machine presets; results populate the existing G-code editor and toolpath visualizer. Kernel claims remain attached to their original NC and become stale after edits. The exact CAD renderer must apply placement/gizmo transforms once, preserve face/edge picking and own disposable Three resources independently per canvas.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:15:The CAM store owns the displayed kernel artifact alongside its NC/program, so generic G-code parsing cannot erase the operation/tool structure of a matching kernel program. Its owner scope includes `apps/desktop/src/features/cam/store/camStore.ts` and `screens/CamPage.tsx` for that existing reconciliation path. Evidence remains stale after edits; loading unrelated code never certifies it.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:19:Assistant integration adds `cam_edit.manufacture` beside the read-only `cam.manufacture`: shared input parameters plus optional expected drawing revision; write authority and cancellation checked before storing an eligible artifact. The existing Toolpath row stores the original NC and `data.kernelArtifact`, with drawing/project attribution; no DB migration. The action returns the stored operation ID and a bounded summary. Desktop CAM reconciliation fetches that row, validates artifact/NC identity, and publishes its original multi-tool program/evidence without regeneration. Owners additionally include the existing WP8 CAM action/handler/prompt definitions, WP4 AI handler, WP6 CAM result decoder and matching tests. Blocked/stale/denied runs create no program row.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:52:- `packages/core/src/cam/operations/`: profile, pocket, drill, face, engraving, adaptive, surface, rest, thread and multiaxis strategies. Reuse these implementations where their capabilities and tolerance contracts are adequate.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:53:- `cam/model/model.ts`, `dexel.ts`, `mesh-queries.ts`: mesh-derived part queries and Z-dexel material intervals. Exact operations on the sampled intervals do not imply exact solid geometry. Grid resolution can grow to fit a cell budget.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:55:- `cam/ir.ts`: useful machine-neutral moves, cycles and programs, extended with rotary values. Rotary values currently use degrees. `toolpathStart` infers a position from the first destination; professional execution needs a known initial state instead.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:56:- `cam/five-axis.ts`, `indexing.ts`, `operations/multiaxis.ts`: AC/BC trunnion planning, TCP choices and sampled surface contact. Head kinematics, complete swept assembly checking and machine/controller-qualified execution are absent.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:62:Three changes earn the most Depth: unify generation behind one orchestrating Interface; make stock an operation dependency; make machine motion and final-NC verification explicit. Do not rewrite every existing algorithm first.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:71:- **ManufacturingJob**: immutable part snapshot, setups, ordered operations, tools, material parameters, machine/controller/post snapshots and quality requirements. The operation dependency graph must be acyclic and every required region must have an explicit outcome.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:73:- **OperationSpec**: discriminated strategy/parameter record, selected regions, tool assembly ID, setup ID, stock input dependency, allowance, machining direction, entry/link policy and finish requirements. Avoid an extensible `Record<string, unknown>` as the validated strategy contract.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:77:- **ArtifactIdentity**: canonical job/part/setup/operation/tool/material/machine/controller/post/algorithm digests, initial state and tolerance policy. Final evidence also binds final NC bytes and interpretation/verification versions.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:95:An empty toolpath, non-finite value, exhausted budget, unsupported region, unresolved normal or insufficient tolerance cannot be represented as a successful operation. A legitimate no-material operation needs explicit checked evidence.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:113:The feedback edge is sequential: accepted operation output stock becomes the next operation's input. Provisional stock can support planning, but the final emitted sequence must be replayed from the initial stock. If replay changes material evolution beyond the declared budget, downstream strategies are invalidated and recalculated within a bounded repair policy.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:137:`CamArtifact` contains per-operation outcomes and stock lineage, cutter-location and machine-motion references, final NC bytes/source map, interpreted trace, verification findings, achieved quality and artifact identity. `NcVerificationInput` accepts the actual NC, explicit initial state, exact setup/geometry/tool/machine/controller snapshots and verification requirements; it does not regenerate a substitute program. Imported NC can have verification evidence without an intent-comparison claim.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:139:`CamIssue` is a tagged diagnostic with stage, operation/setup IDs, optional region/segment/block/body-pair references, expected/achieved quality and a human message. Required tags include invalid input, stale geometry, unsupported capability/controller command, ambiguous initial state, unreachable pose, singularity, axis/dynamic limit, collision, gouge, remaining stock, incomplete region, tolerance not met, budget exhausted, replay mismatch and stale evidence. Effect/HTTP Adapters map these to the existing tagged-error convention.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:168:Operation B consumes the actual output stock of A, including A's entries, links and tool changes where cutting occurs. Rest operations derive their regions from this state and target allowance. Invalidate dependent states when operation order, tool shape, paths, setup or source stock changes. Pure setup transfers require a rigid stock transform and a declared re-fixturing operation; stock cannot reset to a new bounding box.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:172:Strategies generate **CutterLocationPath** intent: an explicit initial tool pose, parametric line/arc/helix or bounded spline segments, unit tool-axis direction, optional roll when the assembly requires it, feed/process intent, source region and operation IDs. Contact locations and tool-tip locations are distinct. No controller G/M words or preselected A/B/C angles belong here.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:181:- **Turning/mill-turn**: reserve discriminated operation, spindle/workholding and channel intent. Existing external-turning generation remains a limited Adapter. Full turning, live tooling, stock transfer and channel synchronization need dedicated implementation/qualification; printer generation is outside this subtractive kernel.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:183:The linking Implementation owns lead-in/out, ramps, helices, restarts, r
    ... command output truncated for persistence ...
    s, inch import, degree/radian migration and turning diameter conventions; pose round trips within allocated uncertainty. Change part, fixture, holder, work offset, gauge length, material limits, operation order, controller or NC bytes: every affected cache/evidence claim becomes stale.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:290:8. **Lifecycle and budgets**: cancel and resume at every stage; different compute quanta yield the same artifacts/findings. A stale publication or failed required operation produces no releasable program. Repeat the same input to get byte-identical deterministic NC and semantic evidence, excluding explicitly external timestamps.
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:308:## Change log
    specs/29-native-cam-kernel.md:310:- 2026-10-14: owner-requested additive CAM Save integration uses existing project/drawing/toolpath/version APIs. Portable JSON retains exact native NC and IR; edited or postprocessed NC cannot retain native release evidence. Stored programs reopen through the existing CAM/library flow. Desktop scopes and failure/version behavior are defined in spec 06; no new DB schema or atomic receipt implementation.
    /**
     * Toolpath detail (legacy `pages/toolpaths/[id].tsx`) in the CAM workspace style.
     *
     * Glass toolbar with back, the toolpath name, and the page actions (Edit / Save / Cancel, Download G-code,
     * Use in CAM, Delete). The stage has four tabs: Details (view or edit form, plus the information rail), G-code,
     * Versions (history with restore) and Comments. Writes go through the typed client; a save also records a
     * version, as the legacy page did.
     */
    import { For, Show, createEffect, createSignal, on, onMount, type JSX } from "solid-js"
    import { A, useNavigate, useParams, useSearchParams } from "@solidjs/router"
    import { Motion } from "solid-motionone"
    import { ArrowLeft, Calendar, Cpu, Download, Edit, Layers, MessageCircle, Save, Share2, Trash2 } from "lucide-solid"
    import { toast } from "../../../shared/ui/feedback.tsx"
    import { useApp } from "../../../shared/state/context.ts"
    import { useRead } from "../../../app/queries.ts"
    import { invalidatePrefix } from "../../../shared/state/query-cache.ts"
    import { Modal, OPERATION_LABEL, TYPE_LABEL, formatDate, handOffToCam, type ToolpathRow } from "../components/shared.tsx"
    import { ToolpathFormModal } from "../components/ToolpathFormModal.tsx"
    import "../../../styles/workstation.css"
    
    type Tab = "details" | "gcode" | "versions" | "comments"
    const TABS: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly id: Tab; readonly label: string }> = [
      { id: "details", label: "Details" }, { id: "gcode", label: "G-code" }, { id: "versions", label: "Versions" }, { id: "comments", label: "Comments" }
    ]
    
    const TOOLBAR_BUTTON = "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-white/40 dark:border-gray-600/40 bg-white/60 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 shadow-sm transition-all duration-200 hover:bg-white/80 dark:bg-gray-800/60 dark:text-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-700/80 disabled:opacity-50"
    const TOOLBAR_PRIMARY = "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#007ACC] px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-white shadow-md transition-all duration-200 hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50"
    const PANEL = "rounded-xl border border-white/40 bg-white/70 p-5 shadow-md backdrop-blur-sm dark:border-gray-700/50 dark:bg-gray-800/70"
    const INPUT = "w-full rounded-md border border-gray-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-gray-900 shadow-sm focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 dark:border-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-900 dark:text-white"
    const TAB_ON = "border-[#007ACC] text-[#007ACC] dark:text-blue-400"
    const TAB_OFF = "border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:border-gray-300 hover:text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-400 dark:hover:text-gray-300"
    
    interface VersionRow {
      readonly id: string
      readonly changeMessage: string | null
      readonly createdAt: string
      readonly user: { readonly name: string | null; readonly email: string | null } | null
    }
    interface CommentRow {
      readonly id: string
      readonly content: string
      readonly userId: string
      readonly createdAt: string
      readonly user: { readonly name: string | null; readonly image: string | null } | null
    }
    
    interface Draft {
      readonly name: string
      readonly description: string
      readonly type: string
      readonly operationType: string
      readonly isPublic: boolean
      readonly gcode: string
    }
    
    const draftOf = (row: ToolpathRow): Draft => ({
      name: row.name,
      description: row.description ?? "",
      type: row.type ?? "mill",
      operationType: row.operationType ?? "contour",
      isPublic: row.isPublic,
      gcode: row.gcode ?? ""
    })
    
    export function ToolpathDetailScreen(): JSX.Element {
      const app = useApp()
      const params = useParams<{ id: string }>()
      const navigate = useNavigate()
      const [urlParams] = useSearchParams()
      const run = app.runtime.run as <A>(effect: unknown) => Promise<A>
      const me = useRead((client) => client.user.profile())
    
      const [row, setRow] = createSignal<ToolpathRow>()
      const [loading, setLoading] = createSignal(true)
      const [error, setError] = createSignal<string>()
      const [busy, setBusy] = createSignal(false)
      const [editing, setEditing] = createSignal(false)
      const [draft, setDraft] = createSignal<Draft>()
      const [tab, setTab] = createSignal<Tab>("details")
      const [versions, setVersions] = createSignal<ReadonlyArray<VersionRow>>([])
      const [comments, setComments] = createSignal<ReadonlyArray<CommentRow>>([])
      const [commentText, setCommentText] = createSignal("")
      const [deleteOpen, setDeleteOpen] = createSignal(false)
      const [restoring, setRestoring] = createSignal<string>()
      const [projects, setProjects] = createSignal<ReadonlyArray<{ readonly id: string; readonly name: string }>>([])
      const [formOpen, setFormOpen] = createSignal(false)
    
      const load = async (): Promise<void> => {
        setLoading(true)
        setError(undefined)
        try {
          const found = await run<ToolpathRow>(app.client.toolpaths.get({ params: { id: params.id } }))
          setRow(found)
          if (!editing()) setDraft(draftOf(found))
        } catch (cause) {
          setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause))
        } finally {
          setLoading(false)
        }
      }
    
      const loadVersions = async (): Promise<void> => {
        try {
          setVersions(await run<ReadonlyArray<VersionRow>>(app.client.toolpathExtras.listVersions({ params: { id: params.id } })))
        } catch {
          toast.error("Failed to load version history")
        }
      }
    
      const loadComments = async (): Promise<void> => {
        try {
          setComments(await run<ReadonlyArray<CommentRow>>(app.client.toolpathExtras.listComments({ params: { id: params.id } })))
        } catch {
          toast.error("Failed to load comments")
          setComments([])
        }
      }
    
      onMount(() => {
        void load()
        void run<ReadonlyArray<{ id: string; name: string }>>(app.client.projects.list())
          .then((list) => setProjects(list.map((p) => ({ id: p.id, name: p.name }))))
          .catch(() => setProjects([]))
      })
    
      // `?download=true` comes from the list's "Download G-code" action: download once the toolpath has loaded.
      let downloaded = false
      createEffect(on(row, (current) => {
        if (current === undefined || downloaded || urlParams.download !== "true") return
        downloaded = true
        downloadGcode()
      }))
    
      createEffect(on(tab, (next) => {
        if (next === "versions" && versions().length === 0) void loadVersions()
        if (next === "comments" && comments().length === 0) void loadComments()
      }, { defer: true }))
    
      const startEdit = (): void => { const current = row(); if (current) { setDraft(draftOf(current)); setEditing(true); setTab("details") } }
      const cancelEdit = (): void => { const current = row(); if (current) setDraft(draftOf(current)); setEditing(false) }
      const patch = (next: Partial<Draft>): void => { const current = draft(); if (current) setDraft({ ...current, ...next }) }
    
      const save = async (): Promise<void> => {
        const current = row()
        const value = draft()
        if (!current || !value) return
        setBusy(true)
        try {
          const updated = await run<ToolpathRow>(app.client.toolpaths.update({
            params: { id: current.id },
            payload: { name: value.name, description: value.description === "" ? null : value.description, type: value.type, operationType: value.operationType, isPublic: value.isPublic, gcode: value.gcode }
          }))
          // A saved revision is also recorded in the version history, as the legacy page did.
          await run(app.client.toolpathExtras.createVersion({
            params: { id: current.id },
            payload: { data: updated.data ?? {}, gcode: updated.gcode ?? "", changeMessage: `Manual save on ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}` }
          }))
          setRow(updated)
          setEditing(false)
          setDraft(draftOf(updated))
          toast.success("Toolpath saved successfully")
          invalidatePrefix(app.cache, "toolpaths")
          if (tab() === "versions") void loadVersions()
        } catch (cause) {
          toast.error(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Failed to save toolpath")
        } finally {
          setBusy(false)
        }
      }
    
      const deleteToolpath = async (): Promise<void> => {
        const current = row()
        if (!current) return
        setBusy(true)
        try {
          await run(app.client.toolpaths.remove({ params: { id: current.id } }))
          toast.success("Toolpath deleted successfully")
          invalidatePrefix(app.cache, "toolpaths")
          navigate("/toolpaths")
        } catch (cause) {
          toast.error(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Failed to delete toolpath")
          setBusy(false)
        }
      }
    
      const restore = async (versionId: string): Promise<void> => {
        const current = row()
        if (!current) return
        setRestoring(versionId)
        try {
          const updated = await run<ToolpathRow>(app.client.toolpathExtras.restoreVersion({ params: { id: current.id }, payload: { versionId } }))
          setRow(updated)
          setDraft(draftOf(updated))
          toast.success("Version restored successfully")
          await loadVersions()
        } catch (cause) {
          toast.error(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Failed to restore version")
        } finally {
          setRestoring(undefined)
        }
      }
    
      const addComment = async (event: Event): Promise<void> => {
        event.preventDefault()
        const current = row()
        const text = commentText().trim()
        if (!current || text === "") return
        try {
          await run(app.client.toolpathExtras.createComment({ params: { id: current.id }, payload: { content: text } }))
          setCommentText("")
          toast.success("Comment added successfully")
          await loadComments()
        } catch (cause) {
          toast.error(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Failed to add comment")
        }
      }
    
      const removeComment = async (commentId: string): Promise<void> => {
        const current = row()
        if (!current) return
        try {
          await run(app.client.toolpathExtras.removeComment({ params: { id: current.id, commentId } }))
          toast.success("Comment deleted successfully")
          await loadComments()
        } catch (cause) {
          toast.error(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Failed to delete comment")
        }
      }
    
      const downloadGcode = (): void => {
        const current = row()
        if (!current || !current.gcode) { toast.error("No G-code available for download"); return }
        const blob = new Blob([current.gcode], { type: "text/plain" })
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
        const anchor = document.createElement("a")
        anchor.href = url
        anchor.download = `${current.name.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi, "_")}.gcode`
        document.body.appendChild(anchor)
        anchor.click()
        document.body.removeChild(anchor)
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
      }
    
      const share = (): void => {
        void navigator.clipboard?.writeText(window.location.href)
        toast.success("Link copied to clipboard")
      }
    
      const typeLabel = (value: string | null | undefined): string => TYPE_LABEL[value ?? "mill"] ?? value ?? "Unknown"
      const operationLabel = (value: string | null | undefined): string => OPERATION_LABEL[value ?? "contour"] ?? value ?? "Unknown"
    
      return (
        <Motion.div
          class="cam-workspace h-full w-full flex flex-col overflow-hidden"
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.6 }}
        >
          <div class="cam-workspace__frame flex flex-col h-full w-full">
            <Motion.div class="workstation-toolbar w-full flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2" initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ duration: 0.6, delay: 0.4 }}>
              <div class="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
                <A href="/toolpaths" class="rounded-full p-2 text-gray-600 transition-colors hover:bg-gray-200/60 dark:text-gray-300 dark:hover:bg-gray-800/60" aria-label="Back to toolpaths"><ArrowLeft size={18} aria-hidden="true" /></A>
                <div class="min-w-0">
                  <h1 class="truncate text-base font-semibold text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{row()?.name ?? "Toolpath"}</h1>
                  <Show when={row()}>{(current) => <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{typeLabel(current().type)} • {operationLabel(current().operationType)}</p>}</Show>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <Show when={!editing()} fallback={<>
                  <button type="button" class={TOOLBAR_PRIMARY} onClick={() => void save()} disabled={busy()}><Save size={16} aria-hidden="true" />Save</button>
                  <button type="button" class={TOOLBAR_BUTTON} onClick={cancelEdit} disabled={busy()}>Cancel</button>
                </>}>
                  <button type="button" class={TOOLBAR_PRIMARY} onClick={startEdit} disabled={busy() || row() === undefined}><Edit size={16} aria-hidden="true" />Edit</button>
                  <button type="button" class={TOOLBAR_BUTTON} onClick={downloadGcode} disabled={busy() || !row()?.gcode}><Download size={16} aria-hidden="true" />Download G-code</button>
                  <button type="button" class={TOOLBAR_BUTTON} onClick={() => row() && handOffToCam(row()!, navigate)} disabled={busy() || row() === undefined}><Cpu size={16} aria-hidden="true" />Use in CAM</button>
                  <button type="button" class={TOOLBAR_BUTTON} onClick={() => setFormOpen(true)} disabled={busy() || row() === undefined}>Properties</button>
                  <button type="button" class={TOOLBAR_BUTTON} onClick={() => setDeleteOpen(true)} disabled={busy() || row() === undefined}><Trash2 size={16} aria-hidden="true" />Delete</button>
                </Show>
              </div>
            </Motion.div>
    
            <div class="flex flex-1 min-h-0 flex-col overflow-y-auto p-4">
              <Show when={!loading()} fallback={<p class="p-8 text-center text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400" role="status">Loading toolpath...</p>}>
                <Show when={error() === undefined} fallback={<div class="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-800 dark:border-red-800 dark:bg-red-900/30 dark:text-red-300" role="alert">{error()}</div>}>
                  <Show when={row()}>{(current) => (
                    <>
                      <nav class="mb-4 flex gap-6 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700" aria-label="Toolpath sections">
                        <For each={TABS}>{(item) => (
                          <button type="button" aria-pressed={tab() === item.id} class={`flex items-center border-b-2 px-1 py-3 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${tab() === item.id ? TAB_ON : TAB_OFF}`} onClick={() => setTab(item.id)}>
                            {item.id === "versions" ? <Layers size={16} class="mr-2" aria-hidden="true" /> : item.id === "comments" ? <MessageCircle size={16} class="mr-2" aria-hidden="true" /> : null}
                            {item.label}
                          </button>
                        )}</For>
                      </nav>
    
                      <Show when={tab() === "details"}>
                        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-3">
                          <div class={`${PANEL} md:col-span-2`}>
                            <Show when={editing() && draft()} fallback={
                              <div class="space-y-5">
                                <h2 class="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{current().name}</h2>
                                <Show when={current().description} fallback={<p class="italic text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">No description provided</p>}>
                                  <p class="whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">{current().description}</p>
                                </Show>
                                <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                                  <div class="rounded-md bg-gray-50 p-3 dark:bg-gray-900"><h3 class="mb-1 text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Machine Type</h3><p class="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{typeLabel(current().type)}</p></div>
                                  <div class="rounded-md bg-gray-50 p-3 dark:bg-gray-900"><h3 class="mb-1 text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Operation Type</h3><p class="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{operationLabel(current().operationType)}</p></div>
                                </div>
                                <Show when={current().data && Object.keys(current().data as object).length > 0}>
                                  <div>
                                    <h3 class="mb-2 text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">Toolpath Settings</h3>
                                    <div class="max-h-80 overflow-auto rounded-md bg-gray-50 p-4 dark:bg-gray-900"><pre class="font-mono text-xs text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{JSON.stringify(current().data, null, 2)}</pre></div>
                                  </div>
                                </Show>
                              </div>
                            }>
                              {(value) => (
                                <form class="space-y-4" onSubmit={(event) => { event.preventDefault(); void save() }}>
                                  <div>
                                    <label for="detail-name" class="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">Toolpath Name</label>
                                    <input id="detail-name" name="name" type="text" required class={INPUT} value={value().name} onInput={(e) => patch({ name: e.currentTarget.value })} />
                                  </div>
                                  <div>
                                    <label for="detail-description" class="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">Description</label>
                                    <textarea id="detail-description" name="description" rows={4} class={INPUT} value={value().description} onInput={(e) => patch({ description: e.currentTarget.value })} />
                                  </div>
                                  <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                                    <div>
                                      <label for="detail-type" class="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">Machine Type</label>
                                      <select id="detail-type" name="type" class={INPUT} value={value().type} onChange={(e) => patch({ type: e.currentTarget.value })}>
                                        <option value="mill">Mill</option><option value="lathe">Lathe</option><option value="3dprinter">3D Printer</option>
                                      </select>
                                    </div>
                                    <div>
                                      <label for="detail-operation" class="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">Operation Type</label>
                                      <select id="detail-operation" name="operationType" class={INPUT} value={value().operationType} onChange={(e) => patch({ operationType: e.currentTarget.value })}>
                                        <Show when={value().type === "mill"}>
                                          <option value="contour">Contour</option><option value="pocket">Pocket</option><option value="drill">Drill</option><option value="engrave">Engrave</option><option value="profile">3D Profile</option><option value="threading">Threading</option>
                                        </Show>
                                        <Show when={value().type === "lathe"}>
                                          <option value="turning">Turning</option><option value="facing">Facing</option><option value="boring">Boring</option><option value="threading">Threading</option><option value="grooving">Grooving</option><option value="parting">Parting</option>
                                        </Show>
                                        <Show when={value().type === "3dprinter"}>
                                          <option value="standard">Standard Print</option><option value="vase">Vase Mode</option><option value="support">Support</option><option value="infill">Infill</option><option value="raft">Raft</option><option value="brim">Brim</option>
                                        </Show>
                                      </select>
                                    </div>
                                  </div>
                                  <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                                    <input id="detail-public" name="isPublic" type="checkbox" class="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-blue-600 dark:border-gray-700" checked={value().isPublic} onChange={(e) => patch({ isPublic: e.currentTarget.checked })} />
                                    <label for="detail-public" class="text-sm text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-300">Make toolpath public (visible to all users)</label>
                                  </div>
                                </form>
                              )}
                            </Show>
                          </div>
    
                          <aside class={PANEL}>
                            <h3 class="mb-4 text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">Toolpath Information</h3>
                            <dl class="space-y-3 text-sm">
                              <div class="flex justify-between"><dt class="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Created</dt><dd class="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{formatDate(current().createdAt) || "-"}</dd></div>
                              <div class="flex justify-between"><dt class="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Last Updated</dt><dd class="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{formatDate(current().updatedAt) || "-"}</dd></div>
                              <div class="flex justify-between"><dt class="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">ID</dt><dd class="font-mono text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{current().id.substring(0, 8)}...</dd></div>
                              <div class="flex justify-between"><dt class="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Visibility</dt><dd class={`font-medium ${current().isPublic ? "text-green-600 dark:text-green-400" : "text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400"}`}>{current().isPublic ? "Public" : "Private"}</dd></div>
                              <div class="flex justify-between"><dt class="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">G-code Size</dt><dd class="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{current().gcode ? `${Math.ceil(current().gcode!.length / 1024)} KB` : "No G-code"}</dd></div>
                            </dl>
                            <div class="mt-6 space-y-2">
                              <button type="button" class="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-green-600 px-4 py-2 text-white hover:bg-green-700 disabled:opacity-50" onClick={downloadGcode} disabled={!current().gcode}><Download size={16} aria-hidden="true" />Download G-code</button>
                              <button type="button" class="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-purple-600 px-4 py-2 text-white hover:bg-purple-700" onClick={() => handOffToCam(current(), navigate)}><Cpu size={16} aria-hidden="true" />Use in CAM</button>
                              <button type="button" class="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-gray-600 px-4 py-2 text-white hover:bg-gray-700" onClick={share}><Share2 size={16} aria-hidden="true" />Share Toolpath</button>
                            </div>
                          </aside>
                        </div>
                      </Show>
    
                      <Show when={tab() === "gcode"}>
                        <div class={PANEL}>
                          <div class="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
                            <h3 class="text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">G-code</h3>
                            <button type="button" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-green-600 px-3 py-1 text-sm text-white hover:bg-green-700 disabled:opacity-50" onClick={downloadGcode} disabled={!current().gcode}><Download size={14} aria-hidden="true" />Download</button>
                          </div>
                          <Show when={editing()} fallback={
                            <div class="h-[60vh] overflow-auto rounded-md border border-gray-300 bg-gray-50 dark:border-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-900">
                              <Show when={current().gcode} fallback={<div class="flex h-full items-center justify-center"><p class="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">No G-code available</p></div>}>
                                <pre class="whitespace-pre p-4 font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{current().gcode}</pre>
                              </Show>
                            </div>
                          }>
                            <textarea class="h-[60vh] w-full rounded-md border border-gray-300 bg-white p-3 font-mono text-sm text-gray-900 dark:border-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-900 dark:text-white" placeholder="; Enter G-code here" value={draft()?.gcode ?? ""} onInput={(e) => patch({ gcode: e.currentTarget.value })} />
                          </Show>
                        </div>
                      </Show>
    
                      <Show when={tab() === "versions"}>
                        <div class={PANEL}>
                          <h3 class="mb-4 text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">Version History</h3>
                          <Show when={versions().length > 0} fallback={
                            <div class="py-8 text-center"><Layers size={36} class="mx-auto mb-2 text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-600" aria-hidden="true" /><p class="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">No version history available</p></div>
                          }>
                            <div class="space-y-3">
                              <For each={versions()}>{(version) => (
                                <div class="rounded-md border border-gray-200 p-4 transition-colors hover:bg-gray-50 dark:border-gray-700 dark:hover:bg-gray-900/50">
                                  <div class="flex items-start justify-between">
                                    <div>
                                      <div class="mb-1 flex items-center text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400"><Calendar size={14} class="mr-1" aria-hidden="true" />{new Date(version.createdAt).toLocaleString()}</div>
                                      <div class="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{version.changeMessage || "No description"}</div>
                                    </div>
                                    <button type="button" class="rounded-md bg-blue-600 px-3 py-1 text-sm text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50" disabled={restoring() !== undefined} onClick={() => void restore(version.id)}>Restore</button>
                                  </div>
                                  <div class="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">Created by {version.user?.name || "Unknown user"}</div>
                                </div>
                              )}</For>
                            </div>
                          </Show>
                        </div>
                      </Show>
    
                      <Show when={tab() === "comments"}>

            </div>
          </Modal>
        </Motion.div>
      )

    /**
     * CAM workspace store (spec 06-desktop-ui.md §3: "all editor state lives in the
     * CAM store, screens are projections over the data").
     *
     * Two kinds of state live here:
     *  - **persisted**: the current toolpath (program), the post-processor choice and
     *    the workpiece; the toolbar/sidebar reflect them.

    /** One stored operation's generated program, as the page shows it. */
    export interface OperationResult {
      readonly id: string

    /**
     * The page's program and editor text for a set of operation results: every toolpath of every operation (ids prefixed with the
     * operation, so two operations' `tp-1` never collide) and every program text, each under a comment naming its operation.
     */
    export const combineOperationResults = (results: ReadonlyArray<OperationResult>): { readonly program: cam.Program; readonly gcode: string } => {
      if (results.length === 0) return { program: cam.makeProgram("untitled", [], {}), gcode: "" }
      const first = results[0]!.program
      const toolpaths = results.flatMap((r) => r.program.toolpaths.map((tp) => ({ ...tp, id: `${r.id}:${tp.id}`, toolId: r.tool ?? `${r.id}:${tp.toolId}`, name: results.length > 1 ? `${r.name} · ${tp.name}` : tp.name })))
      const program = cam.makeProgram(results.length > 1 ? `${results.length} operations` : first.name, toolpaths, { units: first.units, workOffset: first.workOffset })
      const gcode = results.length === 1 ? results[0]!.gcode : results.map((r, i) => `; ===== Operation ${i + 1}/${results.length}: ${r.name} =====\n${r.gcode.trimEnd()}`).join("\n\n")
      return { program, gcode }
    }
    
    const legacyInitial = {
      operationResults: [] as ReadonlyArray<OperationResult>,
      gcode: "",
      selectedEntities: [] as string[],
      selectedWorkpieceElementId: null as string | null,
      derivedWorkpieceDimensions: null as DerivedWorkpieceDimensions | null,
      stockAllowance: 1.0,

      isLoading: false,
      error: null as string | null,
      workpieceElements: [] as ReadonlyArray<Record<string, unknown>>,
      preserveGeometry: true
    }
    
    /** Sensible defaults for the raw piece and the program. */

    const initialProgram = cam.makeProgram("untitled", [], {})

      setRightTab: (tab) => set({ rightTab: tab }),
      setOperation: (operation) => set({ operation }),
      toggleSidebar: () => set((state) => ({ sidebarOpen: !state.sidebarOpen })),
      toggleRight: () => set((state) => ({ rightOpen: !state.rightOpen })),
      toggleFloating: () => set((state) => ({ floatingOpen: !state.floatingOpen })),
      setSelectedOperationId: (id) => set({ selectedOperationId: id }),
      setPlaying: (playing) => set({ playing }),
      setPlaybackTime: (seconds) => set({ playbackTime: seconds }),

      upsertOperationResult: (result) => set((state) => {
        const at = state.operationResults.findIndex((r) => r.id === result.id)
        const operationResults = at < 0 ? [...state.operationResults, result] : state.operationResults.map((r, i) => (i === at ? result : r))
        return { operationResults, ...combineOperationResults(operationResults) }
      }),
      removeOperationResult: (id) => set((state) => {
        if (!state.operationResults.some((r) => r.id === id)) return {}
        const operationResults = state.operationResults.filter((r) => r.id !== id)
        return { operationResults, ...combineOperationResults(operationResults) }
      }),
      clearOperationResults: () => set({ operationResults: [], ...combineOperationResults([]) }),
      toggleEntitySelection: (id) => set((state) => ({
        selectedEntities: state.selectedEntities.includes(id) ? state.selectedEntities.filter((entity) => entity !== id) : [...state.selectedEntities, id]
      })),
      clearSelectedEntities: () => set({ selectedEntities: [] }),
      setSelectedWorkpieceElementId: (id) => set((state) => ({
        selectedWorkpieceElementId: id,
        // Clearing the selection keeps nothing derived from it.
        derivedWorkpieceDimensions: id ? state.derivedWorkpieceDimensions : null
      })),
      setDerivedWorkpieceDimensions: (dimensions) => set({ derivedWorkpieceDimensions: dimensions }),
      setStockAllowance: (allowance) => set({ stockAllowance: allowance >= 0 ? allowance : 0 }),
      setIsLatheSetup: (isLathe) => set({ isLatheSetup: isLathe }),
      setLoading: (loading) => set({ isLoading: loading }),

      syncWorkpieceFromCAD: () => {
        const { elements, selectedElement } = useElementsStore.getState()
        const cadWorkpiece = useCADStore.getState().workpiece
        const mirror = (dimensions: DerivedWorkpieceDimensions) => set({ workpiece: { x: dimensions.width, y: dimensions.height, z: dimensions.depth } })
    
        // 1. The CAD workpiece already points at an element: that element is the raw piece.

        // 2. Otherwise the selected element becomes the raw piece, and the CAD store learns about it.
        if (selectedElement !== null) {
          const dimensions = cam.generator.calculateElementDimensions(selectedElement)

        // 3. Nothing selected: just expose the available elements and take the CAD workpiece size.
        set({ workpieceElements: elements, preserveGeometry: true })

        if (selectedWorkpieceElementId !== null && derivedWorkpieceDimensions !== null) {
          return { dimensions: derivedWorkpieceDimensions, elementId: selectedWorkpieceElementId }
        }

        return { dimensions: { width: cadWorkpiece.width, height: cadWorkpiece.height, depth: cadWorkpiece.depth }, elementId: null }

    /** Elements the user has selected in the CAD editor, as CAM operations see them (legacy `selectedEntities` + CAD selection). */
    export const selectedCadElementIds = (): ReadonlyArray<string> => useSelectionStore.getState().selectedElementIds
    
    /**
     * Validates a toolpath generation request against the current state. Returns
     * either the list of cuts (a toolpath), or an error message the form can show
     * inline.
     */
    export type ToolpathFailure =
      | { readonly _tag: "NoProject" }
      | { readonly _tag: "NoGeometry" }
      | { readonly _tag: "NoTool" }
      | { readonly _tag: "InvalidStock" }
      | { readonly _tag: "DecodeError"; readonly issue: string }
    
    export interface GenerateInput {
      readonly projectId: string | undefined
      readonly entities: ReadonlyArray<unknown>
      readonly workpiece: Workpiece
      readonly totalDepth: number
      readonly tool: unknown
    }
    
    /** Helper kept here so it is testable in isolation from any Solid component. */
    export const validateGenerate = (input: GenerateInput): ToolpathFailure | undefined => {
      if (input.projectId === undefined) return { _tag: "NoProject" }
      if (input.entities.length === 0) return { _tag: "NoGeometry" }
      if (input.tool === undefined) return { _tag: "NoTool" }
      const dims = [input.workpiece.x, input.workpiece.y, input.workpiece.z]
      if (dims.some((value) => !Number.isFinite(value) || value <= 0)) return { _tag: "InvalidStock" }
      if (input.totalDepth > input.workpiece.z) return { _tag: "InvalidStock" }
      return undefined
    }
    
    /** Build a typed `Tool` from a row that comes from the `tools.list` endpoint. */
    export interface ToolRow {
      readonly id: string
      readonly name: string
      readonly type: string
      readonly diameter: number
      readonly numberOfFlutes?: number
      readonly maxRPM?: number
    }
    
    /**
     * Best-effort coercion of a `tools.list` row to a `cam.Tool`. If the `type`
     * field is not a known tool kind, the row is rejected and a safe default is
     * returned so the UI keeps a working value to display.
     */
    export const toolFromRow = (row: ToolRow): cam.Tool => {
      const known: ReadonlyArray<cam.Tool["kind"]> = ["endmill", "ballnose", "drill", "vbit", "facemill"]
      const kind = (known as ReadonlyArray<string>).includes(row.type) ? (row.type as cam.Tool["kind"]) : "endmill"
      const candidate: cam.Tool = {
        id: row.id,
        name: row.name,
        kind,
        diameter: row.diameter,
        flutes: row.numberOfFlutes ?? 2,
        maxRpm: row.maxRPM ?? 18000
      }
      const decoded = Schema.decodeUnknownOption(cam.ToolSchema)(candidate)
      if (decoded._tag === "Some") return decoded.value
      // Schema rejected: keep the row id so it still shows up in selectors, but pin
      // everything else to safe defaults so the rest of the editor can rely on it.
      return {
        id: row.id,
        name: row.name,
        kind: "endmill",
        diameter: Number.isFinite(row.diameter) && row.diameter > 0 ? row.diameter : 1,
        flutes: row.numberOfFlutes && row.numberOfFlutes >= 1 ? row.numberOfFlutes : 2,
        maxRpm: row.maxRPM && row.maxRPM > 0 ? row.maxRPM : 18000
      }
    }
    
    /** Reads a JSON blob as a typed `cam.Program`. Returns `undefined` on shape mismatch. */
    export const decodeProgramOrUndefined = (value: unknown): cam.Program | undefined => {
      const decoded = Schema.decodeUnknownOption(cam.ProgramSchema)(value)
      return decoded._tag === "Some" ? decoded.value : undefined
    }

    import { createHash } from "node:crypto"
    import { BadRequest, CamKernelResult, CamProgramDocument, type CamKernelRequest } from "@cadcamfun/api"
    import { cam } from "@cadcamfun/core"
    import { Effect, Schema } from "effect"
    import { elementsOf } from "./ai-elements.ts"
    
    const record = (value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> => value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? value as Record<string, unknown> : {}
    
    /** Preserve the existing stored-operation revision algorithm while sharing it with HTTP saves. */
    export const drawingRevision = (loaded: { readonly id: string; readonly elements: ReadonlyArray<Record<string, unknown>>; readonly data: Record<string, unknown> }): string => {
      const canonical = (value: unknown): unknown => Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(canonical) : value !== null && typeof value === "object"
        ? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value).sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b)).map(([key, child]) => [key, canonical(child)])) : value
      return createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify(canonical({ drawingId: loaded.id, elements: loaded.elements, stock: loaded.data["stock"] ?? null }))).digest("hex")
    }
    
    /** The same request adapter serves preview generation and persistence revalidation. */
    export const runKernelRequest = Effect.fn("cam.runKernelRequest")(function*(payload: CamKernelRequest) {
      const machine = payload.machine !== undefined ? cam.kernel.findMachine(payload.machine) : undefined
      if (payload.machine !== undefined && machine === undefined) return yield* new BadRequest({ message: `Unknown machine preset '${payload.machine}'` })
      const result = yield* Effect.try({ try: () => {
        const meshes = payload.stock !== undefined ? cam.model.partMeshes(payload.elements, { tolerance: payload.tolerance ?? 0.01 }) : undefined
        const bottom = meshes !== undefined ? Math.min(...meshes.meshes.map((mesh) => cam.model.meshBounds(mesh).min[2])) : 0
        const stock = payload.stock
        return cam.kernel.runJob({
          schemaVersion: payload.schemaVersion, id: payload.id, name: payload.name, material: payload.material,
          part: { kind: "elements", elements: payload.elements }, ...(machine !== undefined ? { machine } : {}),
          ...(stock !== undefined ? { stock: { min: { x: stock.x - stock.width / 2, y: stock.y - stock.height / 2, z: bottom }, max: { x: stock.x + stock.width / 2, y: stock.y + stock.height / 2, z: bottom + stock.depth } } } : {}),
          ...(payload.origin !== undefined ? { origin: payload.origin } : {}), ...(payload.workOffset !== undefined ? { workOffset: payload.workOffset } : {}),
          quality: { ...(payload.finishAllowance !== undefined ? { finishAllowance: payload.finishAllowance } : {}), ...(payload.scallop !== undefined ? { scallop: payload.scallop } : {}), ...(payload.tolerance !== undefined ? { tolerance: payload.tolerance } : {}), ...(payload.resolution !== undefined ? { resolution: payload.resolution } : {}) }
        })
      }, catch: () => new BadRequest({ message: "The CAM kernel request could not be evaluated" }) })
      return yield* Schema.decodeUnknownEffect(CamKernelResult)(result).pipe(Effect.mapError(() => new BadRequest({ message: "The CAM kernel returned an invalid artifact" })))
    })
    
    const same = (a: unknown, b: unknown): boolean => cam.kernel.canonicalJson(a) === cam.kernel.canonicalJson(b)
    export const isPortableProgram = (data: unknown): boolean => record(data)["format"] === "cadcamfun-cam"
    
    /** Validate recognized new payloads without changing the legacy free-form data contract. */
    export const prepareCamProgramData = Effect.fn("cam.prepareProgramData")(function*(input: {
      readonly data: unknown; readonly gcode: string | null | undefined; readonly previous?: unknown;
      readonly drawing?: { readonly id: string; readonly data: unknown } | undefined
    }) {
      const data = { ...record(input.data) }, old = record(input.previous)
      if (!isPortableProgram(data)) {
        const artifact = record(data["kernelArtifact"])
        if (artifact["nc"] !== undefined && artifact["nc"] !== input.gcode) {
          for (const key of ["kernelArtifact", "kernelRequest", "sourceRevision", "verification", "response"]) delete data[key]
          return data
        }
        if (artifact["nc"] !== undefined) {
          if (!same(data["kernelArtifact"], old["kernelArtifact"] ?? null)) return yield* new BadRequest({ message: "New native artifacts require a portable CAM program and captured source request" })
          delete data["sourceRevision"]
          if (old["sourceRevision"] !== undefined) data["sourceRevision"] = old["sourceRevision"]
          return data
        }
        return input.data
      }
      let snapshot = yield* Schema.decodeUnknownEffect(CamProgramDocument)(data).pipe(Effect.mapError(() => new BadRequest({ message: "Invalid CADCAMFUN toolpath document" })))
      if (snapshot.gcode !== input.gcode) {
        if (input.previous === undefined || old["gcode"] !== snapshot.gcode) return yield* new BadRequest({ message: "Toolpath document and G-code must contain the same program" })
        // Existing editor/API updates may send only new NC and the unchanged old document.
        const code = input.gcode ?? ""
        const program = yield* Effect.try({ try: () => cam.generator.analyseGcode(code).program, catch: () => new BadRequest({ message: "The saved G-code could not be parsed" }) })
        delete data["kernelArtifact"]; delete data["kernelRequest"]
        snapshot = yield* Schema.decodeUnknownEffect(CamProgramDocument)({ ...data, gcode: code, program }).pipe(Effect.mapError(() => new BadRequest({ message: "Invalid updated toolpath document" })))
      }
      delete data["sourceRevision"]
      delete data["verification"]
      delete data["response"]
      if (snapshot.kernelArtifact) {
        let artifact = snapshot.kernelArtifact
        if (snapshot.kernelRequest) {
          const result = yield* runKernelRequest(snapshot.kernelRequest)
          if (result.status !== "ok" || result.value.release === "blocked" || !same(result.value, artifact) || !same(snapshot.program, artifact.program) || artifact.nc !== input.gcode) {
            return yield* new BadRequest({ message: "The saved kernel artifact does not match its source request and original NC" })
          }
          artifact = result.value
        } else if (!same(old["kernelArtifact"] ?? null, artifact) || artifact.nc !== input.gcode || !same(snapshot.program, artifact.program)) {
          // A legacy export can still be saved as ordinary NC; client-provided claims alone are not verification.
          delete data["kernelArtifact"]
        }
        if (data["kernelArtifact"] !== undefined) {
          data["kernelArtifact"] = artifact
          const drawing = input.drawing, source = snapshot.kernelRequest
          if (drawing && source) {
            const stored = record(drawing.data), elements = elementsOf(stored)
            const selected = source.elements.map((element) => elements.find((candidate) => candidate["id"] === element["id"]))
            if (selected.every((element) => element !== undefined) && same(selected, source.elements) && same(stored["stock"] ?? null, source.stock ?? null)) {
              data["sourceRevision"] = drawingRevision({ id: drawing.id, data: stored, elements })
            }
          } else if (old["sourceRevision"] !== undefined && same(old["kernelArtifact"], artifact)) data["sourceRevision"] = old["sourceRevision"]
        }
      } else {
        for (const key of ["kernelArtifact", "kernelRequest", "sourceRevision", "verification", "response"]) delete data[key]
      }
      const output = { ...data, ...Schema.encodeSync(CamProgramDocument)(snapshot) }
      if (data["kernelArtifact"] === undefined) {
        delete output["kernelArtifact"]; delete output["kernelRequest"]
        output["program"] = yield* Effect.try({ try: () => cam.generator.analyseGcode(snapshot.gcode).program, catch: () => new BadRequest({ message: "The saved G-code could not be parsed" }) })
      }
      return output
    })
    import { afterAll, afterEach, beforeAll, expect, setDefaultTimeout, test } from "bun:test"
    import { cam } from "@cadcamfun/core"
    import { Schema } from "effect"
    import { bootLiveApp } from "./helpers/live-app.ts"
    import { resetStores } from "./helpers/reset-stores.ts"
    import { useCamStore } from "../src/features/cam/store/camStore.ts"
    import { useGeneratorStore } from "../src/features/cam/store/generatorStore.ts"
    import { useElementsStore } from "../src/features/cad/store/elementsStore.ts"
    import { useDocumentStore } from "../src/features/cad/store/documentStore.ts"
    import { SavedCamProgram } from "../src/features/cam/lib/saved-program.ts"
    
    setDefaultTimeout(120_000)
    let app: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof bootLiveApp>>
    const plate = { id: "plate", type: "cube", width: 30, height: 20, depth: 5, layerId: "default" }
    let artifact: cam.kernel.CamArtifact
    beforeAll(async () => {
      app = await bootLiveApp()
      const result = await app.state.runtime.run(app.client.toolpathGenerator.manufacture({ payload: { schemaVersion: 1, id: "save-fixture", name: "Plate", material: "6061", elements: [plate] } }))
      if (result.status !== "ok") throw new Error("The save fixture did not manufacture")
      artifact = result.value
    })
    afterAll(async () => { await app.dispose() })
    afterEach(() => { resetStores(); useGeneratorStore.getState().reset() })
    
    const input = async (name: string, value: string) => {
      const field = await app.wait(() => document.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>(`input[name='${name}']`), 30_000)
      field.value = value; field.dispatchEvent(new window.Event("input", { bubbles: true }))
    }
    const click = async (selector: string) => {
      const button = await app.wait(() => document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(selector), 30_000)
      button.click()
    }
    const prepareNative = () => {
      useElementsStore.setState({ elements: [plate], selectedElement: null })
      const store = useCamStore.getState()
      store.setKernelArtifact(artifact); store.setGcode(artifact.nc); store.setProgram(artifact.program)
      store.setKernelRequest({ schemaVersion: 1, id: "save-fixture", name: "Plate", material: "6061", elements: [plate] })
      useGeneratorStore.getState().setResult(artifact.nc, [])
    }
    
    test("CAM Save exports exact NC and a portable multi-tool path, then imports the file", async () => {
      prepareNative()
      const view = app.visit("/cam")
      const downloads: Array<{ filename: string; blob: Blob }> = []
      const blobs = new Map<string, Blob>()
      const create = URL.createObjectURL, revoke = URL.revokeObjectURL, anchorClick = window.HTMLAnchorElement.prototype.click
      URL.createObjectURL = (blob) => { const id = `blob:cam-save-${blobs.size}`; blobs.set(id, blob as Blob); return id }
      URL.revokeObjectURL = () => undefined
      window.HTMLAnchorElement.prototype.click = function () { downloads.push({ filename: this.download, blob: blobs.get(this.href)! }) }
      try {
        await click("button[title='Save G-Code']")
        await input("fileName", "Plate finish")
        await click("[data-testid='workspace-export-file']")
        expect(downloads[0]!.filename).toBe("Plate finish.nc")
        expect(await downloads[0]!.blob.text()).toBe(artifact.nc)
        await click("button[title='Save G-Code']")
        await input("fileName", "Plate finish")
        const format = document.querySelector<HTMLSelectElement>("select[name='fileFormat']")!
        format.value = "toolpath"; format.dispatchEvent(new window.Event("change", { bubbles: true }))
        await click("[data-testid='workspace-export-file']")
        await app.wait(() => downloads.length === 2)
        const json = await downloads[1]!.blob.text()
        expect(downloads[1]!.filename).toBe("Plate finish.cam.json")
        const snapshot = Schema.decodeUnknownSync(SavedCamProgram)(JSON.parse(json))
        expect(snapshot.gcode).toBe(artifact.nc)
        expect(snapshot.program).toEqual(artifact.program)
        expect(snapshot.kernelArtifact?.identity).toEqual(artifact.identity)
        expect(snapshot.drawing["elements"]).toEqual([plate])
        useCamStore.getState().reset()
        const picker = await app.wait(() => document.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>("input[type='file']"))
        Object.defineProperty(picker, "files", { value: [new window.File([json], "Plate.cam.json", { type: "application/json" })], configurable: true })
        picker.dispatchEvent(new window.Event("change", { bubbles: true }))
        await app.wait(() => useCamStore.getState().gcode === artifact.nc)
        expect(useCamStore.getState().program).toEqual(artifact.program)
        expect(useCamStore.getState().kernelArtifact?.identity.nc).toBe(artifact.identity.nc)
        expect(useCamStore.getState().savedProgram).toBeUndefined()
      } finally { view.dispose(); URL.createObjectURL = create; URL.revokeObjectURL = revoke; window.HTMLAnchorElement.prototype.click = anchorClick }
    })
    
    test("CAM Save creates a private project with CAD and CAM, updates with history and reopens the stored original", async () => {
      prepareNative()
      let view = app.visit("/cam")
      try {
        await click("button[title='Save G-Code']")
        await click("[data-testid='workspace-save-as-project']")
        await input("camProgramName", "Native plate")
        await input("projectName", "Machined plate")
        await click("[data-testid='workspace-save-project']")
        const saved = await app.wait(() => useCamStore.getState().savedProgram, 30_000)
        const row = await app.state.runtime.run(app.client.toolpaths.get({ params: { id: saved.id } }))
        expect(row.name).toBe("Native plate")
        expect(row.gcode).toBe(artifact.nc)
        expect(row.drawingId).toBe(useDocumentStore.getState().drawingId)
        expect(row.data).toMatchObject({ format: "cadcamfun-cam", version: 1, kernelArtifact: { identity: artifact.identity }, program: artifact.program })
        const project = await app.sidecar.call(`/api/projects/${saved.projectId}`)
        expect(project.body.isPublic).toBe(false)
        const drawing = await app.sidecar.call(`/api/drawings/${saved.drawingId}`)
        expect(drawing.body.data.elements).toEqual([plate])
        await app.state.runtime.run(app.client.toolpaths.update({ params: { id: saved.id }, payload: { data: { ...(row.data as Record<string, unknown>), customNote: "Keep this metadata", sourceRevision: "original-revision", verification: { original: true } } } }))
        const changed = `${artifact.nc}\n; edited in CAM`
        useCamStore.getState().setGcode(changed)
        await click("button[title='Save G-Code']")
        await click("[data-testid='workspace-save-as-project']")
        expect(document.querySelector<HTMLSelectElement>("select[name='camSaveDestination']")!.value).toBe("update")
        await click("[data-testid='workspace-save-project']")
        await app.wait(() => !document.querySelector("[data-testid='workspace-project-dialog']"))
        const updated = await app.state.runtime.run(app.client.toolpaths.get({ params: { id: saved.id } }))
        expect(updated.gcode).toBe(changed)
        expect(updated.data).toMatchObject({ customNote: "Keep this metadata", gcode: changed })
        expect((updated.data as Record<string, unknown>)["kernelArtifact"]).toBeUndefined()
        expect((updated.data as Record<string, unknown>)["sourceRevision"]).toBeUndefined()
        expect((updated.data as Record<string, unknown>)["verification"]).toBeUndefined()
        const versions = await app.state.runtime.run(app.client.toolpathExtras.listVersions({ params: { id: saved.id } }))
        expect(versions).toHaveLength(1)
        expect(versions[0]!.gcode).toBe(artifact.nc)
        expect(versions[0]!.data).toMatchObject({ kernelArtifact: { identity: artifact.identity }, customNote: "Keep this metadata" })
        await app.state.runtime.run(app.client.toolpathExtras.restoreVersion({ params: { id: saved.id }, payload: { versionId: versions[0]!.id } }))
        view.dispose(); resetStores()
        view = app.visit(`/cam?loadToolpath=${saved.id}`)
        await app.wait(() => useCamStore.getState().savedProgram?.id === saved.id)
        expect(useCamStore.getState().gcode).toBe(artifact.nc)
        expect(useCamStore.getState().program).toEqual(artifact.program)
        expect(useCamStore.getState().kernelArtifact?.identity).toEqual(artifact.identity)
        await click("button[title='Save G-Code']")
        await click("[data-testid='workspace-save-as-project']")
        expect(document.querySelector<HTMLAnchorElement>("a[href='/toolpaths/" + saved.id + "']")?.textContent).toBe("Manage saved toolpath")
      } finally { view.dispose() }
    })
    
    test("generator Save uses the same project flow, without overwriting an existing CAD drawing", async () => {
      const project = await app.state.runtime.run(app.client.projects.create({ payload: { name: "Existing machining" } }))
      const drawing = await app.state.runtime.run(app.client.drawings.createForProject({ params: { id: project.id }, payload: { name: "Stored drawing", data: { elements: [{ ...plate, width: 40 }] } } }))
      useDocumentStore.getState().setDocument({ projectId: project.id, drawingId: drawing.id })
      prepareNative()
      const view = app.visit("/cam")
      try {
        const button = await app.wait(() => [...document.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>("button")].find((button) => button.textContent?.includes("Save as Toolpath")), 30_000)
        button.click()
        await app.wait(() => document.querySelector<HTMLSelectElement>("select[name='camSaveProject']")?.value === project.id)
        await app.wait(() => !document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>("[data-testid='workspace-save-project']")?.disabled)
        await click("[data-testid='workspace-save-project']")
        const saved = await app.wait(() => useCamStore.getState().savedProgram, 30_000)
        expect(saved.projectId).toBe(project.id)
        expect(saved.drawingId).toBe(drawing.id)
        const stored = await app.sidecar.call(`/api/drawings/${drawing.id}`)
        expect(stored.body.data.elements[0].width).toBe(40)
        const rows = await app.state.runtime.run(app.client.toolpaths.list({ query: { projectId: project.id } }))
        expect(rows).toHaveLength(1)
        expect(rows[0]!.gcode).toBe(artifact.nc)
        expect(rows[0]!.data).toMatchObject({ program: artifact.program })
        expect(localStorage.getItem("gcodeFromToolpathGenerator")).toBeNull()
      } finally { view.dispose() }
    })
    
    test("CAM Undo, Redo and History restore NC, native paths and settings together and discard abandoned redo", async () => {
      const { useCamHistoryStore, flushCamHistory, resetCamHistory } = await import("../src/features/cam/store/camHistoryStore.ts")
      prepareNative(); resetCamHistory()
      const view = app.visit("/cam")
      try {
        const editor = await app.wait(() => document.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>("textarea"), 30_000)
        expect(document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>("button[title='Undo']")!.disabled).toBe(true)
        const changed = `${artifact.nc}\n; manual edit`
        editor.value = changed; editor.dispatchEvent(new window.Event("input", { bubbles: true }))
        await app.wait(() => useCamHistoryStore.getState().index > 0)
        await click("button[title='Undo']")
        expect(useCamStore.getState().gcode).toBe(artifact.nc)
        expect(useCamStore.getState().program).toEqual(artifact.program)
        expect(useCamStore.getState().kernelRequest?.id).toBe("save-fixture")
        await click("button[title='Redo']")
        expect(useCamStore.getState().gcode).toBe(changed)
        const feed = useGeneratorStore.getState().settings.feedrate
        useGeneratorStore.getState().patchSettings({ feedrate: feed + 100 }); flushCamHistory()
        expect(useGeneratorStore.getState().settings.feedrate).toBe(feed + 100)
        window.dispatchEvent(new window.KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "z", ctrlKey: true, bubbles: true, cancelable: true }))
        expect(useGeneratorStore.getState().settings.feedrate).toBe(feed)
        useCamStore.getState().setGcode(`${artifact.nc}\n; different branch`); flushCamHistory()
        expect(document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>("button[title='Redo']")!.disabled).toBe(true)
        const count = useCamHistoryStore.getState().entries.length
        useCamStore.getState().setPlaybackTime(2); flushCamHistory()
        expect(useCamHistoryStore.getState().entries).toHaveLength(count)
        await click("button[title='CAM History']")
        expect(document.querySelector("[data-testid='cam-history-dialog']")?.textContent).toContain("Initial program")
        const first = document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>("[data-testid='cam-history-dialog'] ol button")!
        first.click()
        expect(useCamStore.getState().gcode).toBe(artifact.nc)
        expect(useCamStore.getState().program).toEqual(artifact.program)
        expect(useCamStore.getState().playing).toBe(false)
      } finally { view.dispose() }
    })
    
    test("CAD and CAM share export/project dialog layout and show their contents", async () => {
      prepareNative()
      let view = app.visit("/cad")
      try {
        await click("button[title='Save Project']")
        const cadExport = await app.wait(() => document.querySelector("[role='dialog'] [data-testid='workspace-export-dialog']"))
        const exportClass = cadExport.parentElement!.parentElement!.className
        await click("[data-testid='workspace-save-as-project']")
        const cadProject = await app.wait(() => document.querySelector("[data-testid='workspace-project-dialog']"))
        const projectClass = cadProject.parentElement!.parentElement!.className
        expect(cadProject.textContent).toContain("CAD drawing")
        for (const name of ["projectName", "description", "organization", "public"]) expect(cadProject.querySelector(`[name='${name}']`)).not.toBeNull()
        view.dispose(); view = app.visit("/cam")
        await click("button[title='Save G-Code']")
        const camExport = await app.wait(() => document.querySelector("[data-testid='workspace-export-dialog']"))
        expect(camExport.parentElement!.parentElement!.className).toBe(exportClass)
        await click("[data-testid='workspace-save-as-project']")
        const camProject = await app.wait(() => document.querySelector("[data-testid='workspace-project-dialog']"))
        expect(camProject.parentElement!.parentElement!.className).toBe(projectClass)
        expect(camProject.textContent).toContain("CAM program")
        expect(camProject.textContent).toContain(artifact.material.name)
        expect(camProject.textContent).toContain(artifact.identity.nc)
        for (const name of ["projectName", "description", "organization", "public"]) expect(camProject.querySelector(`[name='${name}']`)).not.toBeNull()
      } finally { view.dispose() }
    })

    import { afterAll, afterEach, beforeAll, expect, setDefaultTimeout, test } from "bun:test"
    import { cam } from "@cadcamfun/core"
    import { Schema } from "effect"
    import { bootLiveApp } from "./helpers/live-app.ts"
    import { resetStores } from "./helpers/reset-stores.ts"
    import { useCamStore } from "../src/features/cam/store/camStore.ts"
    import { useGeneratorStore } from "../src/features/cam/store/generatorStore.ts"
    import { useElementsStore } from "../src/features/cad/store/elementsStore.ts"
    import { useDocumentStore } from "../src/features/cad/store/documentStore.ts"
    import { SavedCamProgram } from "../src/features/cam/lib/saved-program.ts"
    
    setDefaultTimeout(120_000)
    let app: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof bootLiveApp>>
    const plate = { id: "plate", type: "cube", width: 30, height: 20, depth: 5, layerId: "default" }
    let artifact: cam.kernel.CamArtifact
    beforeAll(async () => {
      app = await bootLiveApp()
      const result = await app.state.runtime.run(app.client.toolpathGenerator.manufacture({ payload: { schemaVersion: 1, id: "save-fixture", name: "Plate", material: "6061", elements: [plate] } }))
      if (result.status !== "ok") throw new Error("The save fixture did not manufacture")
      artifact = result.value
    })
    afterAll(async () => { await app.dispose() })
    afterEach(() => { resetStores(); useGeneratorStore.getState().reset() })
    
    const input = async (name: string, value: string) => {
      const field = await app.wait(() => document.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>(`input[name='${name}']`), 30_000)
      field.value = value; field.dispatchEvent(new window.Event("input", { bubbles: true }))
    }
    const click = async (selector: string) => {
      const button = await app.wait(() => document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(selector), 30_000)
      button.click()
    }
    const prepareNative = () => {
      useElementsStore.setState({ elements: [plate], selectedElement: null })
      const store = useCamStore.getState()
      store.setKernelArtifact(artifact); store.setGcode(artifact.nc); store.setProgram(artifact.program)
      store.setKernelRequest({ schemaVersion: 1, id: "save-fixture", name: "Plate", material: "6061", elements: [plate] })
      useGeneratorStore.getState().setResult(artifact.nc, [])
    }
    
    test("CAM Save exports exact NC and a portable multi-tool path, then imports the file", async () => {
      prepareNative()
      const view = app.visit("/cam")
      const downloads: Array<{ filename: string; blob: Blob }> = []
      const blobs = new Map<string, Blob>()
      const create = URL.createObjectURL, revoke = URL.revokeObjectURL, anchorClick = window.HTMLAnchorElement.prototype.click
      URL.createObjectURL = (blob) => { const id = `blob:cam-save-${blobs.size}`; blobs.set(id, blob as Blob); return id }
      URL.revokeObjectURL = () => undefined
      window.HTMLAnchorElement.prototype.click = function () { downloads.push({ filename: this.download, blob: blobs.get(this.href)! }) }
      try {
        await click("button[title='Save G-Code']")
        await input("fileName", "Plate finish")
        await click("[data-testid='workspace-export-file']")
        expect(downloads[0]!.filename).toBe("Plate finish.nc")
        expect(await downloads[0]!.blob.text()).toBe(artifact.nc)
        await click("button[title='Save G-Code']")
        await input("fileName", "Plate finish")
        const format = document.querySelector<HTMLSelectElement>("select[name='fileFormat']")!
        format.value = "toolpath"; format.dispatchEvent(new window.Event("change", { bubbles: true }))
        await click("[data-testid='workspace-export-file']")
        await app.wait(() => downloads.length === 2)
        const json = await downloads[1]!.blob.text()
        expect(downloads[1]!.filename).toBe("Plate finish.cam.json")
        const snapshot = Schema.decodeUnknownSync(SavedCamProgram)(JSON.parse(json))
        expect(snapshot.gcode).toBe(artifact.nc)
        expect(snapshot.program).toEqual(artifact.program)
        expect(snapshot.kernelArtifact?.identity).toEqual(artifact.identity)
        expect(snapshot.drawing["elements"]).toEqual([plate])
        useCamStore.getState().reset()
        const picker = await app.wait(() => document.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>("input[type='file']"))
        Object.defineProperty(picker, "files", { value: [new window.File([json], "Plate.cam.json", { type: "application/json" })], configurable: true })
        picker.dispatchEvent(new window.Event("change", { bubbles: true }))
        await app.wait(() => useCamStore.getState().gcode === artifact.nc)
        expect(useCamStore.getState().program).toEqual(artifact.program)
        expect(useCamStore.getState().kernelArtifact?.identity.nc).toBe(artifact.identity.nc)
        expect(useCamStore.getState().savedProgram).toBeUndefined()
      } finally { view.dispose(); URL.createObjectURL = create; URL.revokeObjectURL = revoke; window.HTMLAnchorElement.prototype.click = anchorClick }
    })
    
    test("CAM Save creates a private project with CAD and CAM, updates with history and reopens the stored original", async () => {
      prepareNative()
      let view = app.visit("/cam")
      try {
        await click("button[title='Save G-Code']")
