    /**
     * Shows what the assistant did on a CAM operation in the CAM page.
     *
     * The tools work on stored operations in the sidecar; the page shows its own editor, program and verification. When
     * a CAM tool finishes, this reads the operation back and puts its settings in the generator, its G-code in the editor
     * (and the program in the preview), and its verification report in the Verify tab, so the user sees the same thing
     * the assistant is talking about.
     */
    import { cam as coreCam } from "@cadcamfun/core"
    import { Schema } from "effect"
    import { CamKernelArtifact, CamKernelRequest } from "@cadcamfun/api"
    import { useCADStore } from "../../cad/store/cadStore.ts"
    import type { AppState } from "../../../shared/state/context.ts"
    import { makeToolpathGenerator, type VerifyResponse } from "../../cam/api/generator.ts"
    import { useCamStore } from "../../cam/store/camStore.ts"
    import { useGeneratorStore, type ToolpathSettings } from "../../cam/store/generatorStore.ts"
    import { useVerifyStore } from "../../cam/store/verifyStore.ts"
    import { captureEditorOwner, type EditorOwner } from "./editor-sync.ts"
    
    /** The four actions whose result changes what the CAM page shows; the key is `tool.action`. */
    const CAM_ACTIONS = ["create", "update", "generate", "verify", "manufacture"] as const
    type CamAction = (typeof CAM_ACTIONS)[number]
    
    /**
     * Which CAM action a call is, from its `tool.action` key. `cam_edit.create` names the operation in its result (there was
     * no id to ask for), the other three in their input or their result — so `create` and the rest are read differently below.
     */
    const actionOfKey = (key: string): CamAction | undefined =>
      CAM_ACTIONS.find((action) => key === `cam_edit.${action}` || key.endsWith(`.${action}`) || key.endsWith(action))
    const isStockSet = (key: string): boolean => key === "cam_edit.stock_set" || key === "cam.stock_set" || key.endsWith("cam_stock")
    
    const parse = (text: string): Record<string, unknown> => {
      try {
        const value = JSON.parse(text)
        return value !== null && typeof value === "object" ? (value as Record<string, unknown>) : {}
      } catch { return {} }
    }
    
    /** The operation a CAM call was about: `create` names it in its result, the other actions in the input they were given. */
    export const operationIdOf = (key: string, input: unknown, output: string): string | undefined => {
      const result = parse(output)
      if (result["ok"] === false || typeof result["error"] === "string") return undefined
      // A receipt is evidence of an earlier commit, not a fresh operation edit to mirror from this call's input.
      if (result["_tag"] === "ActionCommitted") return undefined
      const fromInput = input !== null && typeof input === "object" ? (input as { operationId?: unknown }).operationId : undefined
      const id = actionOfKey(key) === "create" ? result["operationId"] : fromInput ?? result["operationId"]
      return typeof id === "string" && id !== "" ? id : undefined
    }
    
    interface OperationRow {
      readonly description?: string | null
      readonly isPublic?: boolean
      readonly name?: string | null
      readonly gcode: string | null
      readonly data: { readonly request?: { readonly settings?: ToolpathSettings }; readonly verification?: { readonly report?: VerifyResponse } } | null
      readonly drawingId?: string | null
      readonly projectId?: string | null
    }
    
    /** `key` is the call's action key (`cam_edit.generate`). Reads the operation back and mirrors it into the CAM stores. Best effort: a failure leaves the page as it was. */
    export async function mirrorCamResult(app: AppState, key: string, input: unknown, output: string, owner: EditorOwner = captureEditorOwner()): Promise<boolean> {
      if (!owner.current()) return false
      if (isStockSet(key)) {
        const result = parse(output)
        if (result["ok"] !== true || result["action"] !== "set") return false
        const stock = Schema.decodeUnknownSync(coreCam.generator.StockSchema)(result["stock"])
        if (!owner.current()) return false
        useCADStore.getState().setWorkpiece({ ...useCADStore.getState().workpiece, ...stock, configured: true, material: stock.material ?? "", elementId: stock.elementId })
        useCamStore.getState().setWorkpiece({ x: stock.width, y: stock.height, z: stock.depth })
        useCamStore.getState().setStockAllowance(stock.allowance)
        useCamStore.getState().setSelectedWorkpieceElementId(stock.elementId ?? null)
        // A new stock invalidates every program generated on the old one.
        useCamStore.getState().clearOperationResults()
        useGeneratorStore.getState().setResult("", [])
        useVerifyStore.getState().reset()
        return true
      }
      const kind = actionOfKey(key)
      if (kind === undefined) return false
      const id = operationIdOf(key, input, output)
      if (id === undefined || !owner.current()) return false
      try {
        const row = await app.runtime.run(app.client.toolpaths.get({ params: { id } })) as unknown as OperationRow
        if (!owner.current()) return false
        if (kind === "manufacture") {
          if (owner.projectId !== row.projectId || owner.drawingId !== row.drawingId) return false
          const data = row.data as Record<string, unknown> | null
          const artifact = Schema.decodeUnknownSync(CamKernelArtifact)(data?.["kernelArtifact"])
          if (artifact.nc !== row.gcode || coreCam.kernel.sha256(artifact.nc) !== artifact.identity.nc || artifact.release === "blocked" || !owner.current()) return false
          const cam = useCamStore.getState()
          cam.clearOperationResults()
          cam.setKernelArtifact(artifact)
          cam.setKernelRequest(data?.["kernelRequest"] !== undefined ? Schema.decodeUnknownSync(CamKernelRequest)(data["kernelRequest"]) : undefined)
          cam.setSelectedOperationId(id)
          if (row.projectId) cam.setSavedProgram({ id, name: row.name ?? "Native CAM program", projectId: row.projectId, drawingId: row.drawingId ?? null, description: row.description ?? null, isPublic: row.isPublic ?? false })
          cam.setGcode(artifact.nc)
          cam.setProgram(artifact.program)
          useGeneratorStore.getState().setResult(artifact.nc, [])
          useVerifyStore.getState().reset()
          return true
        }
        const settings = row.data?.request?.settings
        const gcode = row.gcode ?? ""
        const parsesProgram = kind !== "create" && kind !== "update" && gcode.trim() !== ""
        const parsed = parsesProgram ? await app.runtime.run(makeToolpathGenerator(app.client).parse(gcode)) : undefined
        const report = row.data?.verification?.report
        if (!owner.current()) return false
        // Set directly: the panel's own `replaceSettings` would re-apply material presets over the assistant's values.
        if (settings !== undefined) useGeneratorStore.setState({ settings })
        const cam = useCamStore.getState()
        cam.setSelectedOperationId(id)
        if (kind === "create" || kind === "update") {
          // Only this operation's program is stale; the programs of the other operations stay on the page.
          cam.removeOperationResult(id)
          useGeneratorStore.getState().setResult("", [])
          useVerifyStore.getState().reset()
          return settings !== undefined
        }
        if (gcode.trim() === "") return settings !== undefined
        const name = typeof row.name === "string" && row.name !== "" ? row.name : settings?.operationType ?? id
        const tool = settings !== undefined ? `${settings.toolType} Ø${settings.toolDiameter}` : undefined
        cam.upsertOperationResult({ id, name, gcode, program: (parsed?.program as never) ?? coreCam.makeProgram(name, [], {}), ...(tool !== undefined ? { tool } : {}) })
        useGeneratorStore.getState().setResult(gcode, [])
        if (kind === "verify" && report !== undefined) {
          useVerifyStore.getState().finish(report, gcode)
          cam.setRightTab("verify")
        }
        return true
      } catch (cause) {
        console.error("Could not mirror the CAM result into the page", cause)
        return false
      }
    }
    /**
     * The multi-toolpath model (legacy `multiToolpathStore.ts`): one toolpath per CAD element, an execution order, a
     * colour, visibility and a combined program.
     *
     * Pure reducers over a state snapshot; the Solid store (`cam/store/multiToolpathStore.ts`) wires them to signals and
     * persistence. Everything is immutable (the legacy store sorted and mutated arrays in place).
     */
    import type { ToolpathSettings } from "./elementJob.ts"
    
    export interface ToolpathPoint {
      readonly x: number
      readonly y: number
      readonly z: number
      readonly feedrate?: number
      readonly type?: string
      readonly isRapid?: boolean
      readonly isArc?: boolean
      readonly elementId?: string
    }
    
    /** One toolpath bound to one CAD element. */
    export interface ElementToolpath {
      readonly id: string
      readonly elementId: string
      readonly elementType: string
      readonly elementName: string
      readonly toolpathSettings: ToolpathSettings
      readonly gcode: string
      /** Display colour (hex). */
      readonly color: string
      readonly visible: boolean
      readonly toolpathPoints: ReadonlyArray<ToolpathPoint>
      readonly selected: boolean
      /** Execution order. */
      readonly order: number
      /** Seconds. */
      readonly estimatedTime: number
      /** Total path length, mm. */
      readonly distance: number
    }
    
    export const TOOLPATH_COLORS = [
      "#FF6B6B", "#4ECDC4", "#45B7D1", "#96CEB4", "#FECA57", "#DDA0DD", "#98D8C8", "#FFB6C1", "#FFA07A", "#87CEEB"
    ] as const
    
    export interface MultiToolpathState {
      readonly toolpaths: ReadonlyArray<ElementToolpath>
      readonly selectedToolpathIds: ReadonlyArray<string>
      readonly activeToolpathId: string | null
      /** Defaults applied to the selected toolpaths by `applyGlobalSettingsToSelected`. */
      readonly globalSettings: Partial<ToolpathSettings>
    }
    
    export const INITIAL_GLOBAL_SETTINGS: Partial<ToolpathSettings> = {
      feedrate: 800,
      plungerate: 300,
      rpm: 10000,
      depth: 5,
      stepdown: 1,
      stepover: 40,
      toolDiameter: 6,
      offset: "outside",
      direction: "climb"
    }
    
    export const initialState = (): MultiToolpathState => ({ toolpaths: [], selectedToolpathIds: [], activeToolpathId: null, globalSettings: { ...INITIAL_GLOBAL_SETTINGS } })
    
    const reindex = (toolpaths: ReadonlyArray<ElementToolpath>): ElementToolpath[] => toolpaths.map((toolpath, order) => ({ ...toolpath, order }))
    const byOrder = (toolpaths: ReadonlyArray<ElementToolpath>): ElementToolpath[] => [...toolpaths].sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order)
    
    export const newToolpathId = (): string => `toolpath-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 11)}`
    
    /** Adds an empty toolpath for an element; the colour cycles through the palette. */
    export const addToolpath = (
      state: MultiToolpathState,
      input: { readonly id?: string; readonly elementId: string; readonly elementType: string; readonly elementName: string; readonly settings: ToolpathSettings }
    ): MultiToolpathState => ({
      ...state,
      toolpaths: [
        ...state.toolpaths,
        {
          id: input.id ?? newToolpathId(),
          elementId: input.elementId,
          elementType: input.elementType,
          elementName: input.elementName,
          toolpathSettings: input.settings,
          gcode: "",
          color: TOOLPATH_COLORS[state.toolpaths.length % TOOLPATH_COLORS.length]!,
          visible: true,
          toolpathPoints: [],
          selected: false,
          order: state.toolpaths.length,
          estimatedTime: 0,
          distance: 0
        }
      ]
    })
    
    export const updateToolpath = (state: MultiToolpathState, id: string, updates: Partial<ElementToolpath>): MultiToolpathState => ({
      ...state,
      toolpaths: state.toolpaths.map(toolpath => (toolpath.id === id ? { ...toolpath, ...updates } : toolpath))
    })
    
    const without = (state: MultiToolpathState, ids: ReadonlyArray<string>): MultiToolpathState => ({
      ...state,
      toolpaths: reindex(state.toolpaths.filter(toolpath => !ids.includes(toolpath.id))),
      selectedToolpathIds: state.selectedToolpathIds.filter(id => !ids.includes(id)),
      activeToolpathId: state.activeToolpathId !== null && ids.includes(state.activeToolpathId) ? null : state.activeToolpathId
    })
    
    export const removeToolpath = (state: MultiToolpathState, id: string): MultiToolpathState => without(state, [id])
    
    export const removeToolpathsByElementId = (state: MultiToolpathState, elementId: string): MultiToolpathState =>
      without(state, state.toolpaths.filter(toolpath => toolpath.elementId === elementId).map(toolpath => toolpath.id))
    
    export const selectToolpath = (state: MultiToolpathState, id: string): MultiToolpathState => ({
      ...state,
      toolpaths: state.toolpaths.map(toolpath => (toolpath.id === id ? { ...toolpath, selected: true } : toolpath)),
      selectedToolpathIds: state.selectedToolpathIds.includes(id) ? state.selectedToolpathIds : [...state.selectedToolpathIds, id],
      activeToolpathId: id
    })
    
    export const deselectToolpath = (state: MultiToolpathState, id: string): MultiToolpathState => ({
      ...state,
      toolpaths: state.toolpaths.map(toolpath => (toolpath.id === id ? { ...toolpath, selected: false } : toolpath)),
      selectedToolpathIds: state.selectedToolpathIds.filter(selected => selected !== id),
      activeToolpathId: state.activeToolpathId === id ? null : state.activeToolpathId
    })
    
    export const selectAllToolpaths = (state: MultiToolpathState): MultiToolpathState => ({
      ...state,
      toolpaths: state.toolpaths.map(toolpath => ({ ...toolpath, selected: true })),
      selectedToolpathIds: state.toolpaths.map(toolpath => toolpath.id)
    })
    
    export const deselectAllToolpaths = (state: MultiToolpathState): MultiToolpathState => ({
      ...state,
      toolpaths: state.toolpaths.map(toolpath => ({ ...toolpath, selected: false })),
      selectedToolpathIds: [],
      activeToolpathId: null
    })
    
    export const toggleToolpathVisibility = (state: MultiToolpathState, id: string): MultiToolpathState => ({
      ...state,
      toolpaths: state.toolpaths.map(toolpath => (toolpath.id === id ? { ...toolpath, visible: !toolpath.visible } : toolpath))
    })
    
    export const setAllVisible = (state: MultiToolpathState, visible: boolean): MultiToolpathState => ({
      ...state,
      toolpaths: state.toolpaths.map(toolpath => ({ ...toolpath, visible }))
    })
    
    /** Parses G-code into points: every G0 / G1 / G2 / G3 move, with modal X / Y / Z / F carried over (legacy `parseGCodeToPoints`). */
    export const parseGCodeToPoints = (gcode: string): ToolpathPoint[] => {
      const points: ToolpathPoint[] = []
      let x = 0
      let y = 0
      let z = 0
      let f = 0
      for (const raw of gcode.split("\n")) {
        const line = raw.replace(/\(.*?\)/g, "").split(";")[0]!.trim()
        if (line.length === 0) continue
        const motion = /(?:^|\s)G0*([0-3])(?!\d)/i.exec(line)
        if (motion === null) continue
        const word = (letter: string): number | undefined => {
          const match = new RegExp(`${letter}([+-]?\\d*\\.?\\d+)`, "i").exec(line)
          return match === null ? undefined : parseFloat(match[1]!)
        }
        x = word("X") ?? x
        y = word("Y") ?? y
        z = word("Z") ?? z
        f = word("F") ?? f
        const code = Number(motion[1])
        points.push({ x, y, z, feedrate: f, type: `G${code}`, isRapid: code === 0, isArc: code === 2 || code === 3 })
      }
      return points
    }
    
    /** Path length (mm) and estimated seconds of a point list (legacy: time from the modal feedrate of each move). */
    export const measurePoints = (points: ReadonlyArray<ToolpathPoint>): { readonly distance: number; readonly time: number } => {
      let distance = 0
      let time = 0
      for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) {
        const from = points[i - 1]!
        const to = points[i]!
        const length = Math.sqrt((to.x - from.x) ** 2 + (to.y - from.y) ** 2 + (to.z - from.z) ** 2)
        distance += length
        if (to.feedrate !== undefined && to.feedrate > 0) time += (length / to.feedrate) * 60
      }
      return { distance, time }
    }
    
    /**
     * Stores generated G-code on a toolpath. Distance and time come from the points, unless the generator already
     * measured them (`measured`, the sidecar's statistics).
     */
    export const updateToolpathGCode = (
      state: MultiToolpathState,
      id: string,
      gcode: string,
      points: ReadonlyArray<ToolpathPoint>,
      measured?: { readonly distance: number; readonly time: number }
    ): MultiToolpathState => {
      const { distance, time } = measured ?? measurePoints(points)
      return {
        ...state,
        toolpaths: state.toolpaths.map(toolpath =>
          toolpath.id === id
            ? { ...toolpath, gcode, toolpathPoints: points.map(point => ({ ...point, elementId: toolpath.elementId })), estimatedTime: time, distance }
            : toolpath
        )
      }
    }
    
    export const reorderToolpaths = (state: MultiToolpathState, newOrder: ReadonlyArray<string>): MultiToolpathState => ({
      ...state,
      toolpaths: byOrder(state.toolpaths.map(toolpath => ({ ...toolpath, order: newOrder.indexOf(toolpath.id) })))
    })
    
    const swap = (state: MultiToolpathState, id: string, delta: -1 | 1): MultiToolpathState => {
      const toolpaths = [...state.toolpaths]
      const index = toolpaths.findIndex(toolpath => toolpath.id === id)
      const target = index + delta
      if (index < 0 || target < 0 || target >= toolpaths.length) return state
      ;[toolpaths[index], toolpaths[target]] = [toolpaths[target]!, toolpaths[index]!]
      return { ...state, toolpaths: reindex(toolpaths) }
    }
    
    export const moveToolpathUp = (state: MultiToolpathState, id: string): MultiToolpathState => swap(state, id, -1)
    export const moveToolpathDown = (state: MultiToolpathState, id: string): MultiToolpathState => swap(state, id, 1)
    
    export const toolpathByElementId = (state: MultiToolpathState, elementId: string): ElementToolpath | undefined =>
      state.toolpaths.find(toolpath => toolpath.elementId === elementId)
    
    export const applyGlobalSettingsToSelected = (state: MultiToolpathState): MultiToolpathState => ({
      ...state,
      toolpaths: state.toolpaths.map(toolpath =>
        state.selectedToolpathIds.includes(toolpath.id)
          ? { ...toolpath, toolpathSettings: { ...toolpath.toolpathSettings, ...state.globalSettings } as ToolpathSettings }
          : toolpath
      )
    })
    
    /** One program from every generated toolpath, in execution order (legacy `generateCombinedGCode`). */
    export const combineGCode = (toolpaths: ReadonlyArray<ElementToolpath>, stamp: string = new Date().toISOString()): string => {
      const ordered = byOrder(toolpaths)
      let text = "; Combined G-Code from Multiple Toolpaths\n"
      text += `; Generated: ${stamp}\n`
      text += `; Total toolpaths: ${ordered.length}\n\n`
      ordered.forEach((toolpath, index) => {
        if (!toolpath.gcode) return
        text += `\n; ===== Toolpath ${index + 1}: ${toolpath.elementName} =====\n`
        text += `; Element Type: ${toolpath.elementType}\n`
        text += `; Tool: Ø${toolpath.toolpathSettings.toolDiameter}mm\n`
        text += `; Color: ${toolpath.color}\n\n`
        text += toolpath.gcode
        text += "\n"
      })
      text += "\n; ===== End of Combined G-Code =====\n"
      return text
    }
    
    export const statistics = (toolpaths: ReadonlyArray<ElementToolpath>) => ({
      totalToolpaths: toolpaths.length,
      generatedToolpaths: toolpaths.filter(toolpath => toolpath.gcode).length,
      totalDistance: toolpaths.reduce((sum, toolpath) => sum + toolpath.distance, 0),
      totalTime: toolpaths.reduce((sum, toolpath) => sum + toolpath.estimatedTime, 0)
    })
    
    /** `m:ss` as the legacy panels print estimated time. */
    export const formatDuration = (seconds: number): string => `${Math.floor(seconds / 60)}:${(seconds % 60).toFixed(0).padStart(2, "0")}`
    ✓ • 0ms

    /**

    import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, test } from "bun:test"
    import { bootLiveApp } from "./helpers/live-app.ts"

      toolpathId = (await app.sidecar.call("/api/toolpaths", { method: "POST", json: { name: "Outline", projectId, type: "mill", operationType: "contour", gcode: "G0 X0" } })).body.id
    })
    afterAll(async () => { await app.dispose() })
    
    const settle = (ms = 80) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms))
    const find = <T extends Element = HTMLElement>(selector: string): Promise<T> => app.wait(() => document.querySelector<T>(selector), 30000)
    const byText = (tag: string, text: string): HTMLElement | undefined =>
      [...document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(tag)].find((el) => el.textContent?.trim() === text)

          expect(document.querySelector(".cam-workspace__stage")).not.toBeNull()
          expect(document.querySelector(".workstation-toolbar")).not.toBeNull()
          expect(await app.wait(() => byText("h3", "Outline"), 30000)).toBeDefined()
    
          const library = await app.wait(() => byText("button", "Standard Library"), 30000)
          library.click()
          await settle()
          expect(document.querySelector('[role="dialog"][aria-label="Standard Library"]')).not.toBeNull()
        } finally {
          dispose()
        }
      })
    })

          byText("button", "Comments")?.click()
          await settle()
          const textarea = await find<HTMLTextAreaElement>('textarea[placeholder="Add a comment..."]')
          textarea.value = "Check the feed rate"
          textarea.dispatchEvent(new window.Event("input", { bubbles: true }))
          await settle()
          const form = textarea.closest("form")
          form?.dispatchEvent(new window.Event("submit", { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }))
          await settle(300)
          const { body } = await app.sidecar.call(`/api/toolpaths/${toolpathId}/comments`)

          byText("button", "Versions")?.click()

          expect(byText("button", "Restore")).toBeDefined()
        } finally {
          dispose()
        }
      })
    })
    /**
     * The translation table and the `t()` function (spec 06-desktop-ui.md §1).
     *
     * `en` is the only locale in v6.0 and it is a *typed* table: `Key` comes from
     * `en.json`, so asking for a string nobody wrote is a type error rather than a
     * runtime `[missing]`. Values may carry `{placeholder}` slots that `t` fills.
     */
    import { createContext, createMemo, createSignal, useContext, type Accessor, type JSX } from "solid-js"
    import base from "./en.json" with { type: "json" }
    // One table per vertical so ports of different areas never edit the same file concurrently.
    import shell from "./en.shell.json" with { type: "json" }
    import library from "./en.library.json" with { type: "json" }
    import org from "./en.org.json" with { type: "json" }
    import cad from "./en.cad.json" with { type: "json" }
    import cam from "./en.cam.json" with { type: "json" }
    import ai from "./en.ai.json" with { type: "json" }
    import plugins from "./en.plugins.json" with { type: "json" }
    import legacy from "./en.legacy.json" with { type: "json" }
    import settings from "./en.settings.json" with { type: "json" }
    import viewport from "./en.viewport.json" with { type: "json" }
    import system from "./en.system.json" with { type: "json" }
    import pricing from "./en.pricing.json" with { type: "json" }
    import detail from "./en.detail.json" with { type: "json" }
    import companion from "./en.companion.json" with { type: "json" }
    import vcsStrings from "./en.vcs.json" with { type: "json" }
    
    const table = { ...base, ...shell, ...library, ...org, ...cad, ...cam, ...ai, ...plugins, ...legacy, ...settings, ...viewport, ...system, ...pricing, ...detail, ...companion, ...vcsStrings }
    
    /** The shipped locale, and the only one in v6.0. */
    export type Locale = "en"
    
    export const DEFAULT_LOCALE: Locale = "en"
    
    /** Flat key -> string map; values are `"text"` or `"text with {placeholders}"`. */
    export type Messages = Record<string, string>
    
    /** Every key that exists in the shipped locale. */
    export type Key = keyof typeof table
    
    /** Values a caller may substitute into a message. */
    export type Placeholders = Readonly<Record<string, string | number>>
    
    /** The shipped table, frozen so one screen cannot mutate another screen's strings. */
    export const en: Messages = Object.freeze({ ...table }) as Messages
    
    const PLACEHOLDER = /\{(\w+)\}/g
    
    /** Replaces `{name}` slots; an unknown slot is left in place so the gap stays visible. */
    export const format = (message: string, values?: Placeholders): string => {
      if (values === undefined) return message
      return message.replace(PLACEHOLDER, (match, name: string) => {
        const value = values[name]
        return value === undefined ? match : String(value)
      })
    }
    
    /** Looks a key up outside of a component (tests, log lines); unknown keys echo back. */
    export const translate = (key: Key, values?: Placeholders): string =>
      format(en[key] === undefined ? key : (en[key] as string), values)
    
    export interface I18n {
      readonly locale: Accessor<Locale>
      readonly t: (key: Key, values?: Placeholders) => string
    }
    
    const createI18n = (): I18n => {
      const [locale, setLocale] = createSignal<Locale>(DEFAULT_LOCALE)
      // A memo, not a plain read: switching locale must re-render every string.
      const messages = createMemo((): Messages => en)
      return {
        locale,
        t: (key, values) => format(messages()[key] === undefined ? key : (messages()[key] as string), values),
        setLocale
      } as I18n & { setLocale: (next: Locale) => void }
    }
    
    const I18nContext = createContext<I18n>()
    
    /**
     * The app-wide translator.
     *
     * Components render text through the *module-level* `t` so that no string is ever
     * written as a literal in JSX; the context only carries the locale, which is what
     * actually has to be reactive.
     */
    export const useI18n = (): I18n => {
      const context = useContext(I18nContext)
      if (context === undefined) throw new Error("useI18n must be called inside <I18nProvider>")
      return context
    }
    
    /** Reactive `t()` for code that has to follow a locale change. */
    export const useT = (): ((key: Key, values?: Placeholders) => string) => useI18n().t
    
    /** Imperative `t()`: the default-locale translator, safe outside a component tree. */
    export const t: (key: Key, values?: Placeholders) => string = (key, values) => translate(key, values)
    
    export interface I18nProviderProps {
      readonly children: JSX.Element
    }
    
    /** Installs the locale signal; children read text through `t`/`useT`. */
    export function I18nProvider(props: I18nProviderProps): JSX.Element {
      const i18n = createI18n()
      return I18nContext.Provider({
        value: i18n,
        get children() {
          return props.children
        }
      })
    }
    /**
     * What the explorer's per-element assignments mean for the generator (legacy `CAMElementExplorer.handleGenerateToolpath`).
     *
     * The explorer lets the user pick a tool, a material and a few parameters for each CAD element. Generating for that
     * element merges them into the job's settings exactly as the legacy handler did: the tool sets type and diameter, the
     * material scales feed and plunge by its `feedrateFactor`, the element type picks the operation (rectangle / circle
     * are pocketed, points are drilled, everything else is contoured). Pure functions over plain values, so the rules are
     * testable without the stores or the sidecar.
     */
    import { cam } from "@cadcamfun/core"
    import { toJsonSafeRecord } from "../../../shared/state/json-safe.ts"
    import { EXPLORER_MATERIALS, EXPLORER_TOOLS } from "./explorer.ts"
    import type { CadElement, Stock } from "./job.ts"
    
    export type ToolpathSettings = cam.generator.ToolpathSettings
    
    /** The parameter fields of the explorer's "Toolpath Parameters" block. */
    export interface ElementParams {
      readonly depth: number
      readonly stepover: number
      readonly feedrate: number
      readonly plungerate: number
    }
    
    export interface ElementAssignment {
      readonly toolId: string
      readonly materialId: string
      readonly params: ElementParams
    }
    
    /** Legacy `defaultToolpathParams` (the part the generator reads). */
    export const LEGACY_DEFAULTS = { tolerance: 0.01, spindleSpeed: 12000, coolant: false } as const
    
    /** Explorer material id → the generator's material type. */
    export const MATERIAL_TYPES: Readonly<Record<string, ToolpathSettings["material"]>> = {
      "wood-oak": "wood",
      "wood-maple": "wood",
      "aluminum": "aluminum",
      "acrylic": "plastic"
    }
    
    /** Operation for an element type, as the legacy handler chose it. */
    export const operationForElement = (type: string): ToolpathSettings["operationType"] =>
      type === "rectangle" || type === "circle" ? "pocket" : type === "point" ? "drill" : "contour"
    
    /** The alert the legacy explorer showed when generation was asked without both picks (or with an unknown id). */
    export const assignmentProblem = (toolId: string, materialId: string): string | undefined => {
      if (!toolId || !materialId) return "Please assign both tool and material first"
      if (EXPLORER_TOOLS[toolId] === undefined || EXPLORER_MATERIALS[materialId] === undefined) return "Invalid tool or material"
      return undefined
    }
    
    const round2 = (value: number): number => Math.round(value * 100) / 100
    
    /**
     * The job settings for one element: `base` (the generator panel's settings: machine, origin, coolant, ...) with the
     * element's tool, material and parameters laid over it. Feed and plunge are scaled by the material factor.
     */
    export const settingsForElement = (base: ToolpathSettings, element: CadElement, assignment: ElementAssignment): ToolpathSettings => {
      const tool = EXPLORER_TOOLS[assignment.toolId]
      const material = EXPLORER_MATERIALS[assignment.materialId]
      if (tool === undefined || material === undefined) throw new Error(assignmentProblem(assignment.toolId, assignment.materialId) ?? "Invalid tool or material")
      const depth = assignment.params.depth
      return {
        ...base,
        machineType: "mill",
        operationType: operationForElement(String(element["type"])),
        toolType: tool.type === "v-bit" ? "vbit" : tool.type,
        toolDiameter: tool.diameter,
        material: MATERIAL_TYPES[assignment.materialId] ?? base.material,
        depth,
        stepdown: Math.min(base.stepdown, depth),
        stepover: assignment.params.stepover,
        feedrate: round2(assignment.params.feedrate * material.feedrateFactor),
        plungerate: round2(assignment.params.plungerate * material.feedrateFactor),
        tolerance: LEGACY_DEFAULTS.tolerance,
        rpm: LEGACY_DEFAULTS.spindleSpeed,
        coolant: LEGACY_DEFAULTS.coolant
      }
    }
    
    /** A generate request that machines exactly one CAD element. */
    export const requestForElement = (input: {
      readonly element: CadElement
      readonly settings: ToolpathSettings
      readonly stock: Stock
      readonly printer?: cam.generator.PrinterExtras
    }): cam.generator.GenerateRequest => ({
      settings: input.settings,
      geometry: { ...cam.generator.DEFAULT_GEOMETRY, geometryType: "selected", selectedElements: [toJsonSafeRecord(input.element)], stock: toJsonSafeRecord(input.stock) } as never,
      printer: input.printer ?? cam.generator.DEFAULT_PRINTER_EXTRAS
    })

    /**
     * Multi-toolpath generator (legacy `MultiToolpathGenerator.tsx`): one toolpath per CAD element, an execution order,
     * and one combined program.
     *
     * Left, the element selector (create / order / remove toolpaths). Right, the full generator panel to configure the
     * element being edited. "Genera Tutti" generates every toolpath that has no G-code yet through the sidecar,
     * "Visualizza" sends the combined program to the CAM viewport and "Scarica G-Code" downloads it.
     */
    import { Show, createSignal, onMount, type JSX } from "solid-js"
    import { Box, Download, Eye, EyeOff, Layers, Play, Zap } from "lucide-solid"
    import { toast } from "../../../../shared/ui/toast.tsx"
    import { useElementsStore } from "../../../cad/store/elementsStore.ts"
    import { useSelectionStore } from "../../../cad/store/selectorStore.ts"
    import { useApp } from "../../../../shared/state/context.ts"
    import { makeToolpathGenerator } from "../../api/generator.ts"
    import { effectiveSettings, generateForElement } from "../../lib/elementGenerate.ts"
    import { assignmentProblem, type ToolpathSettings } from "../../lib/elementJob.ts"
    import { formatDuration, measurePoints, parseGCodeToPoints, statistics } from "../../lib/multiToolpath.ts"
    import { useJob } from "../../hooks/useJob.ts"
    import { useCamStore } from "../../store/camStore.ts"
    import { useGeneratorStore, type LibraryTool } from "../../store/generatorStore.ts"
    import { useMultiToolpathStore } from "../../store/multiToolpathStore.ts"
    import ToolpathGenerator from "../ToolpathGenerator.tsx"
    import { assignmentOf, materialOf, toolOf } from "./assignments.ts"
    import ElementSelector from "./ElementSelector.tsx"
    import { t } from "../../../../i18n/index.ts"
    
    export interface MultiToolpathGeneratorProps {
      /** Receives the program text sent to the viewport (the combined program, or a configured element's). */
      readonly onGCodeGenerated: (gcode: string) => void
      readonly onToolSelected?: (tool: LibraryTool) => void
    }
    
    export default function MultiToolpathGenerator(props: MultiToolpathGeneratorProps): JSX.Element {
      const app = useApp()
      const elements = useElementsStore()
      const selection = useSelectionStore()
      const camStore = useCamStore()
      const gen = useGeneratorStore()
      const job = useJob()
      const store = useMultiToolpathStore()
    
      const [configuringElementId, setConfiguringElementId] = createSignal<string | null>(null)
      const [showElementSelector, setShowElementSelector] = createSignal(true)
      const [isGeneratingAll, setIsGeneratingAll] = createSignal(false)
      // The settings new toolpaths start from: the generator panel's current ones.
      const [defaultSettings, setDefaultSettings] = createSignal<ToolpathSettings>({ ...gen.settings })
    
      onMount(() => store.updateGlobalSettings(defaultSettings()))
    
      const nameOf = (elementId: string): string => {
        const element = elements.elements.find(candidate => candidate["id"] === elementId)
        return String(element?.["name"] ?? element?.["type"] ?? elementId)
      }
    
      /** Writes the selection to both CAD stores, so the embedded generator machines this element. */
      const selectElement = (elementId: string): void => {
        selection.selectMultiple([elementId])
        elements.selectElement(elementId)
      }
    
      /** Stores generated text on the element's toolpath (adding the toolpath when the panel generated first). */
      const storeGCode = (elementId: string, gcode: string, settings: ToolpathSettings, measured?: { readonly distance: number; readonly time: number }): void => {
        const element = elements.elements.find(candidate => candidate["id"] === elementId)
        const existing = store.getToolpathByElementId(elementId)
        const id = existing?.id ?? store.addToolpath(elementId, String(element?.["type"] ?? ""), nameOf(elementId), settings)
        const points = parseGCodeToPoints(gcode)
        store.updateToolpath(id, { toolpathSettings: settings })
        store.updateToolpathGCode(id, gcode, points, measured ?? measurePoints(points))
        toast.success(`Toolpath generato per elemento ${nameOf(elementId)}`)
      }
    
      // Embedded generator panel finished for the element being configured.
      const handleGCodeGenerated = (elementId: string, gcode: string): void => storeGCode(elementId, gcode, { ...gen.settings })
    
      const handleSelectElementForConfig = (elementId: string): void => {
        setConfiguringElementId(elementId)
        selectElement(elementId)
        const toolpath = store.getToolpathByElementId(elementId)
        if (toolpath !== undefined) {
          // Load the toolpath's settings into the panel (and the defaults new toolpaths start from).
          setDefaultSettings(toolpath.toolpathSettings)
          gen.replaceSettings(toolpath.toolpathSettings)
        }
      }
    
      /** Generates one element's toolpath through the sidecar: its own tool / material win when both are assigned. */
      const generateToolpathForElement = async (elementId: string, settings: ToolpathSettings): Promise<void> => {
        const element = elements.elements.find(candidate => candidate["id"] === elementId)
        if (element === undefined) { toast.error("Elemento non trovato"); return }
        const assignment = assignmentProblem(toolOf(elementId), materialOf(elementId)) === undefined ? assignmentOf(elementId) : undefined
        const input = { element, settings: store.getToolpathByElementId(elementId)?.toolpathSettings ?? settings, sources: job.sources(), ...(assignment ? { assignment } : {}) }
        try {
          const response = await generateForElement(app, input)
          storeGCode(elementId, response.gcode, effectiveSettings(input), { distance: response.stats.cutLength + response.stats.rapidLength, time: response.stats.estimatedSeconds })
          for (const warning of response.warnings) toast(warning)
        } catch (cause) {
          toast.error(`${nameOf(elementId)}: ${cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause)}`)
        }
      }
    
      const generateAllToolpaths = async (): Promise<void> => {
        setIsGeneratingAll(true)
        try {
          for (const toolpath of [...store.toolpaths]) {
            if (!toolpath.gcode) await generateToolpathForElement(toolpath.elementId, toolpath.toolpathSettings)
          }
          toast.success("Tutti i toolpath sono stati generati")
        } catch (error) {
          toast.error("Failed to generate toolpaths")
          console.error(error)
        } finally {
          setIsGeneratingAll(false)
        }
      }
    
      const downloadCombinedGCode = (): void => {
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([store.generateCombinedGCode()], { type: "text/plain" }))
        const link = document.createElement("a")
        link.href = url
        link.download = `multi-toolpath-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.nc`
        document.body.appendChild(link)
        link.click()
        document.body.removeChild(link)
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
        toast.success(t("cam.multi.downloaded"))
      }
    
      // The combined program replaces the viewport's: G-code text for the editor, the parsed IR for the simulation.
      const sendToVisualizer = async (): Promise<void> => {
        const combined = store.generateCombinedGCode()
        camStore.setGcode(combined)
        props.onGCodeGenerated(combined)
        try {
          const parsed = await app.runtime.run(makeToolpathGenerator(app.client).parse(combined, "Multi-toolpath"))
          camStore.setProgram(parsed.program as never)
        } catch { /* the page parses the text itself when the editor receives it */ }
        toast.success("G-code inviato al visualizzatore")
      }
    
      const stats = () => statistics(store.toolpaths)
    
      return (
        <div class="flex flex-col h-full" data-testid="multi-toolpath-generator">

    [test]
    # Solid `.tsx` transform for the desktop UI tests (apps/desktop/test/helpers/solid-plugin.ts).
    preload = ["./apps/desktop/test/helpers/solid-plugin.ts"]
    import { afterAll, beforeAll, expect, test } from "bun:test"
    import { Schema } from "effect"
    import { cam } from "@cadcamfun/core"
    import { CamProgramDocument } from "@cadcamfun/api"
    import { makeApp } from "./helpers.ts"
    
    let app: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof makeApp>>
    const request = { schemaVersion: 1, id: "persisted-cam", name: "Plate", material: "6061", elements: [{ id: "plate", type: "cube", width: 30, height: 20, depth: 5, layerId: "default" }] }
    let project: string, drawing: string, document: CamProgramDocument
    beforeAll(async () => {
      app = await makeApp()
      project = (await app.call("/api/projects", { method: "POST", json: { name: "CAM persistence" } })).body.id
      drawing = (await app.call(`/api/projects/${project}/drawings`, { method: "POST", json: { name: "Plate", data: { elements: request.elements } } })).body.id
      const generated = await app.call("/api/toolpaths/manufacture", { method: "POST", json: request })
      document = Schema.decodeUnknownSync(CamProgramDocument)({ format: "cadcamfun-cam", version: 1, gcode: generated.body.value.nc, program: generated.body.value.program,
        settings: cam.generator.DEFAULT_SETTINGS, printer: cam.generator.DEFAULT_PRINTER_EXTRAS, stock: { width: 34, height: 24, depth: 6 }, drawing: { elements: request.elements }, kernelRequest: request, kernelArtifact: generated.body.value })
    })
    afterAll(async () => { await app.dispose() })
    const create = (data: unknown = document, gcode: string = document.gcode) => app.call(`/api/drawings/${drawing}/toolpaths`, { method: "POST", json: { name: "Native CAM", data, gcode, type: "mill", operationType: "kernel" } })
    
    test("the sidecar validates native save and binds current drawing evidence for AI review", async () => {
      const saved = await create({ ...document, sourceRevision: "client-forged-revision", note: "Preserved metadata" })
      expect(saved.status).toBe(201)
      expect(saved.body.gcode).toBe(document.gcode)
      expect(saved.body.data.program).toEqual(document.program)
      expect(saved.body.data.note).toBe("Preserved metadata")
      expect(saved.body.data.sourceRevision).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/)
      const handlers = await app.toolHandlers()
      const review = await handlers.reviewOperation!(project, saved.body.id)
      expect(review).toMatchObject({ status: document.kernelArtifact!.release === "verified-program" ? "pass" : document.kernelArtifact!.release, identity: { nc: document.kernelArtifact!.identity.nc } })
      expect(await handlers.operationProgram!(project, saved.body.id)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, gcode: document.gcode })
      await app.call(`/api/drawings/${drawing}`, { method: "PUT", json: { data: { elements: [{ ...request.elements[0], width: 31 }] } } })
      expect(await handlers.reviewOperation!(project, saved.body.id)).toMatchObject({ ok: false, status: "stale" })
      const historical = await create(document)
      expect(historical.status).toBe(201)
      expect(historical.body.data.sourceRevision).toBeUndefined()
      expect(await handlers.operationProgram!(project, historical.body.id)).toMatchObject({ ok: false })
      await app.call(`/api/drawings/${drawing}`, { method: "PUT", json: { data: { elements: request.elements } } })
    })
    
    test("invalid documents and altered NC, artifact or IR never become verified rows", async () => {
      const before = (await app.call(`/api/toolpaths?projectId=${project}`)).body.length
      expect((await create({ ...document, program: { ...document.program, units: "invalid" } })).status).toBe(400)
      expect((await create(document, `${document.gcode}\n; changed`)).status).toBe(400)
      expect((await create({ ...document, kernelArtifact: { ...document.kernelArtifact, release: "blocked" } })).status).toBe(400)
      expect((await create({ ...document, program: { ...document.program, toolpaths: [] } })).status).toBe(400)
      expect((await create({ kernelArtifact: document.kernelArtifact, sourceRevision: "forged" })).status).toBe(400)
      expect((await app.call(`/api/toolpaths?projectId=${project}`)).body).toHaveLength(before)
    })
    
    test("editing NC through the existing API clears obsolete native evidence and remains reopenable", async () => {
      const saved = await create()
      const code = `${document.gcode}\n; manually edited`
      const plain = { ...document, gcode: code, kernelArtifact: undefined, kernelRequest: undefined }
      const updated = await app.call(`/api/toolpaths/${saved.body.id}`, { method: "PUT", json: { gcode: code, data: plain } })
      expect(updated.status).toBe(200)
      expect(updated.body.data.kernelArtifact).toBeUndefined()
      expect(updated.body.data.kernelRequest).toBeUndefined()
      expect(updated.body.data.sourceRevision).toBeUndefined()
      expect(updated.body.data.program).toEqual(cam.generator.analyseGcode(code).program)
      const handlers = await app.toolHandlers()
      expect(await handlers.operationProgram!(project, saved.body.id)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, gcode: code })
      expect(await handlers.reviewOperation!(project, saved.body.id)).toMatchObject({ ok: false, status: "unverified" })
      const original = await create()
      const edited = await app.call(`/api/toolpaths/${original.body.id}`, { method: "PUT", json: { gcode: code } })
      expect(edited.status).toBe(200)
      const reopened = await app.call(`/api/toolpaths/${original.body.id}`)
      expect(reopened.body.gcode).toBe(code)
      expect(reopened.body.data.gcode).toBe(code)
      expect(reopened.body.data.kernelArtifact).toBeUndefined()
      expect(reopened.body.data.sourceRevision).toBeUndefined()
      expect(reopened.body.data.program).toEqual(cam.generator.analyseGcode(code).program)
      const projectProgram = await app.call("/api/toolpaths", { method: "POST", json: { name: "Project NC", projectId: project, gcode: code, data: plain } })
      expect(projectProgram.status).toBe(201)
      expect(await handlers.listOperations!(project)).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({ id: projectProgram.body.id, generated: true }))
      expect(await handlers.operationProgram!(project, projectProgram.body.id)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, gcode: code })
    })
    
    test("stock changes invalidate both the stored original and every portable NC/IR/evidence copy", async () => {
      const saved = await create()
      const handlers = await app.toolHandlers()
      const stock = { ...document.stock, width: 36 }
      expect(await handlers.stock!(project, { action: "set", stock })).toMatchObject({ ok: true })
      const invalidated = (await app.call(`/api/toolpaths/${saved.body.id}`)).body
      expect(invalidated.gcode).toBeNull()
      expect(invalidated.data.gcode).toBe("")
      expect(invalidated.data.program.toolpaths).toEqual([])
      expect(invalidated.data.stock.width).toBe(36)
      expect(invalidated.data.kernelArtifact).toBeUndefined()
      expect(invalidated.data.kernelRequest).toBeUndefined()
      expect(invalidated.data.sourceRevision).toBeUndefined()
      expect(() => Schema.decodeUnknownSync(CamProgramDocument)(invalidated.data)).not.toThrow()
    })
    110:Owner-requested CAM persistence alignment (2026-10-14): `CamProgramDocument` is the shared versioned portable/saved program schema, reusing core IR/settings/stock and `CamKernelRequest`/artifact. Existing `Toolpath.data` and `ToolpathVersion.data` JSON columns store it; no migration is needed. Sidecar create/update validates this recognized format and exact duplicated NC. Native evidence supplied by the desktop is accepted only after rerunning its captured kernel request and matching the artifact/NC/IR; existing server-generated originals may be retained unchanged. Edited NC loses earlier native/verification evidence. The sidecar owns source-revision attribution to a matching stored drawing; client revisions cannot certify changed geometry or stock. Native project writes require the same owner/admin/manager scope as AI writes (create/createForDrawing add `Forbidden`); legacy nonportable payload behavior is retained. Version creation requires toolpath write access. Owners include API shared schemas/groups/codegen outputs, sidecar CAM adapter/toolpath handlers, their tests, and desktop CAM/AI snapshot consumers. Atomic execution receipts remain RM-03.
    112:Existing transactional stock invalidation also clears native artifacts/requests/source revision and the portable document's duplicated NC/IR, updating its stock. Reopening an invalidated program must not restore obsolete stock or native claims. Saved portable programs are available to AI listing/static analysis; edited originals are explicitly unverified, and project-level programs without a drawing cannot acquire drawing verification by association.

    /**
     * The 3D viewport that plays back a `cam.Program` (spec 06-desktop-ui.md §3 tab
     * "Preview").
     *
     * It re-uses the shared `View3D` component (the same one the CAD editor uses)
     * so the visual language is identical: same camera, same lighting, same
     * gizmos. The shell hosts only the controls that are CAM-specific (playback
     * timeline, simulator overlay).
     */
    import { Show, createMemo, createSignal, type JSX } from "solid-js"
    import { Motion } from "solid-motionone"
    import { RotateCcw, Settings, X } from "lucide-solid"
    import { cam } from "@cadcamfun/core"
    import { View3D, toolpathOverlayModel, type RenderMode, type ToolpathPointKind, type ViewPreset } from "../../../viewport/view3d/index.ts"
    import { t } from "../../../i18n/index.ts"
    import type { Workpiece } from "../store/camStore.ts"
    
    export interface ToolpathVisualizerProps {
      readonly program: cam.Program
      readonly workpiece: Workpiece
      readonly stock?: cam.generator.Stock
      readonly playing: boolean
      readonly playbackTime: number
      readonly playbackRate: number
      readonly onPlaybackTimeChange: (seconds: number) => void
    }
    
    const rise = { initial: { opacity: 0, y: 20 }, animate: { opacity: 1, y: 0 } }
    
    /** Half the longest stock side, used to size the default camera distance. */
    const defaultDistance = (workpiece: Workpiece): number => {
      const longest = Math.max(workpiece.x, workpiece.y, workpiece.z)
      return Math.max(150, longest * 2.2)
    }
    
    export default function ToolpathVisualizer(props: ToolpathVisualizerProps): JSX.Element {
      const [preset, setPreset] = createSignal<ViewPreset>("perspective")
      const [mode, setMode] = createSignal<RenderMode>("shaded-edges")
      const [gridVisible, setGridVisible] = createSignal(true)
      const [axesVisible, setAxesVisible] = createSignal(true)
      const [settingsOpen, setSettingsOpen] = createSignal(false)
      const [showStats, setShowStats] = createSignal(true)
      const [showPoints, setShowPoints] = createSignal(false)
      const [showPointLabels, setShowPointLabels] = createSignal(false)
      const [pointFilter, setPointFilter] = createSignal<"all" | ToolpathPointKind>("all")
      const [pointSize, setPointSize] = createSignal(0.7)
      const [showArcCenters, setShowArcCenters] = createSignal(false)
      const [showArcRadii, setShowArcRadii] = createSignal(false)
      const [stockVisible, setStockVisible] = createSignal(true)
      const [stockOpacity, setStockOpacity] = createSignal(0.6)
      const [arcTolerance, setArcTolerance] = createSignal(0.1)
      const overlay = createMemo(() => toolpathOverlayModel(props.program))
    
      const stock = createMemo(() => ({
        origin: props.stock ? { x: props.stock.x - props.stock.width / 2, y: props.stock.y - props.stock.height / 2, z: props.stock.topZ - props.stock.depth } : { x: -props.workpiece.x / 2, y: -props.workpiece.y / 2, z: 0 },
        size: props.stock ? { x: props.stock.width, y: props.stock.height, z: props.stock.depth } : { x: props.workpiece.x, y: props.workpiece.y, z: props.workpiece.z }
      }))
    
      const hasProgram = (): boolean => props.program.toolpaths.length > 0
    
      const resetView = (): void => {
        setPreset("perspective")
        setMode("shaded-edges")
        setGridVisible(true)
        setAxesVisible(true)
        setShowPoints(false)
        setShowPointLabels(false)
        setPointFilter("all")
        setShowArcCenters(false)
        setShowArcRadii(false)
        setStockVisible(true)
        setStockOpacity(0.6)
        setArcTolerance(0.1)
        props.onPlaybackTimeChange(0)
      }
    
      const toggle = (label: string, checked: () => boolean, change: (value: boolean) => void): JSX.Element => (
        <label class="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 text-xs text-slate-200">
          <span>{label}</span>
          <input type="checkbox" class="h-4 w-4 accent-blue-500" checked={checked()} onChange={(event) => change(event.currentTarget.checked)} />
        </label>
      )
    
      return (
        <Motion.div class="h-full w-full flex flex-col" {...rise} transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}>
          <div class="flex-1 relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-white/30 dark:border-gray-600/30 bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-900 to-slate-700 shadow-xl">
            <Show
              when={hasProgram()}
              fallback={
                <div class="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-white/70 text-sm">
                  <p class="font-medium">{t("cam.preview.noToolpath")}</p>
                  <p class="mt-1 text-xs text-white/50">{props.program.toolpaths.length === 0 ? t("cam.preview.hint") : t("cam.preview.pick")}</p>
                </div>
              }
            >
              <View3D
                visionMode="cam"
                program={props.program}
                preset={preset()}
                renderMode={mode()}
    import * as THREE from "three"
    import type { Element } from "../store/elementsStore.ts"
    import type { SelectionBounds } from "../store/selectorStore.ts"
    import { createThreeObject } from "./createThreeObject.ts"
    import { initializeElementTransform, type ElementTransformFields } from "./element-transform.ts"
    import { disposeObject } from "./sceneHelpers.ts"
    
    export const componentChildren = (element: Element): Element[] => {
      if (Array.isArray(element.elements)) return element.elements
      if (element.type === "component" || element.type === "group") {
        return element.data?.elements ?? element.data?.geometry?.elements ?? []
      }
      return []
    }
    
    export interface ComponentEntry { readonly element: Element; readonly depth: number }
    export function componentEntries(elements: readonly Element[], depth = 0): ComponentEntry[] {
      return elements.flatMap((element) => [{ element, depth }, ...componentEntries(componentChildren(element), depth + 1)])
    }
    
    /** Clone the entire assembly; no instance or library record shares mutable geometry with another. */
    export function cloneComponentElement(element: Element, freshIds = false): Element {
      const copy = structuredClone(element)
      const visit = (node: Element): void => {
        if (freshIds) node.id = crypto.randomUUID()
        delete node.selected
        delete node.originalLibraryItem
        const children = componentChildren(node)
        if (children.length) {
          node.elements = children
          for (const child of children) visit(child)
        }
      }
      visit(copy)
      return copy
    }
    
    /** Translate only coordinates in this element's frame; descendants keep their local poses. */
    export function translateComponentElement(element: Element, dx: number, dy: number, dz: number): Element {
      const copy = cloneComponentElement(element)
      if (element.type === "line") {
        for (const suffix of ["1", "2"]) {
          copy[`x${suffix}`] = (element[`x${suffix}`] ?? 0) + dx
          copy[`y${suffix}`] = (element[`y${suffix}`] ?? 0) + dy
          copy[`z${suffix}`] = (element[`z${suffix}`] ?? 0) + dz
        }
      } else if (["mesh", "spline", "polyline"].includes(element.type)) {
        const key = element.type === "mesh" ? "vertices" : "points"
        if (Array.isArray(copy[key])) copy[key] = copy[key].map((point: { x: number; y: number; z?: number }) =>
          ({ ...point, x: point.x + dx, y: point.y + dy, z: (point.z ?? 0) + dz }))
      } else {
        copy.x = (element.x ?? 0) + dx
        copy.y = (element.y ?? 0) + dy
        copy.z = (element.z ?? 0) + dz
      }
      return copy
    }
    
    /** Bounds use the same geometry and transforms as the viewport, including every nested child. */
    export function componentBounds(elements: readonly Element[]): SelectionBounds | null {
      const box = new THREE.Box3()
      for (const element of elements) {
        const copy = cloneComponentElement(element)
        const object = createThreeObject(copy, { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 })
        if (!object) continue
        try {
          initializeElementTransform(object, copy as ElementTransformFields)
          box.union(new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(object))
        } finally { disposeObject(object) }
      }
      if (box.isEmpty() || ![...box.min.toArray(), ...box.max.toArray()].every(Number.isFinite)) return null
      const size = box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()), center = box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3())
      return { minX: box.min.x, maxX: box.max.x, minY: box.min.y, maxY: box.max.y, minZ: box.min.z, maxZ: box.max.z,
        width: size.x, height: size.y, depth: size.z, centerX: center.x, centerY: center.y, centerZ: center.z }
    }
    
    export interface ComponentDetails {
      readonly name: string
      readonly description?: string
      readonly layerId: string
      readonly tags?: string[]
    }
    export function createComponentAssembly(elements: readonly Element[], details: ComponentDetails): Element {
      const bounds = componentBounds(elements)
      if (!bounds) throw new Error("The selection has no usable geometry.")
      const entries = componentEntries(elements)

        id: crypto.randomUUID(), type: "component", ...details,
        x: bounds.centerX, y: bounds.centerY, z: bounds.centerZ,
        width: bounds.width, height: bounds.height, depth: bounds.depth,

    }

    /**
     * A project is more than its drawing: it holds the components the editor reuses, the toolpaths the
     * CAM workspace generates, the materials cut with and the drawings themselves (spec 03 §2).
     *
     * This module is the one place that turns the editor's stores into that complete set, so the "Save"
     * and "Save as Project" paths cannot drift apart and silently drop half the work. Layers and the
     * measuring settings travel with the drawing; the library components travel as project components.
     */
    import { toJsonSafe } from "../../../shared/state/json-safe.ts"
    import { documentSettings } from "../../../shared/state/document-settings.ts"
    import type { AppState } from "../../../shared/state/context.ts"
    import { useElementsStore } from "../store/elementsStore.ts"
    import { useLayerStore } from "../store/layerStore.ts"
    import { useLocalComponentsLibraryStore } from "../store/localComponentsLibraryStore.ts"
    
    /** How the screens run typed client calls without importing Effect types here. */
    type Run = <A>(effect: unknown) => Promise<A>
    
    /**
     * The part of the typed client this module uses, named so callers and tests can build one.
     * `AppState["client"]` satisfies it.
     */
    export interface ComponentClient {
      readonly projects: {
        readonly create: (input: { readonly payload: { name: string; description: string | null; organizationId: string | null; isPublic: boolean } }) => Promise<{ readonly id: string }>
      }
      readonly drawings: {
        readonly createForProject: (input: { readonly params: { readonly id: string }; readonly payload: { readonly name: string; readonly description: string | null; readonly data: unknown } }) => Promise<{ readonly id: string; readonly name: string }>
        readonly update: (input: { readonly params: { readonly id: string }; readonly payload: { readonly data: unknown } }) => Promise<unknown>
      }
      readonly components: {
        readonly listForProject: (input: { readonly params: { readonly id: string } }) => Promise<ReadonlyArray<{ readonly id: string; readonly name: string }>>
        readonly createForProject: (input: { readonly params: { readonly id: string }; readonly payload: { readonly name: string; readonly description: string | null; readonly type: string; readonly isPublic: boolean; readonly data: unknown } }) => Promise<{ readonly id: string }>
      }
    }
    
    /** The drawing a project stores: geometry, layers and the measuring settings, versioned. */
    export const drawingPayload = (app: Pick<AppState, "settings">): Record<string, unknown> => {
      const { elements, selectedElement } = useElementsStore.getState()
      const { layers } = useLayerStore.getState()
      // The whole payload goes through the JSON filter, not just the geometry: a setting that holds `undefined` or a class instance
      // is refused by the typed client ("Expected JSON value at ..."), and the assistant's turn would end before it started.
      return toJsonSafe({
        elements,
        layers,
        selectedElement: selectedElement?.id ?? null,
        settings: documentSettings(app),
        version: "1.0"
      }) as Record<string, unknown>
    }
    
    /** What a save actually put in the project, so the caller can report it instead of guessing. */
    export interface ProjectContents {
      readonly drawingId: string
      readonly componentIds: ReadonlyArray<string>
    }
    
    /** Creates the project's drawing; returns the new drawing id. */
    async function createDrawing(
      app: Pick<AppState, "client" | "runtime" | "settings">,
      projectId: string,
      name: string,
      run: Run
    ): Promise<string> {
      const drawing = await run<{ readonly id: string; readonly name: string }>(app.client.drawings.createForProject({
        params: { id: projectId },
        payload: { name, description: `Main drawing for project ${name}`, data: drawingPayload(app) }
      }))
      return drawing.id
    }
    
    /** Reads the component names a project already holds, so a re-save does not duplicate them. */
    async function existingNames(app: Pick<AppState, "client" | "runtime">, projectId: string, run: Run): Promise<ReadonlySet<string>> {
      try {
        const rows = await run<ReadonlyArray<{ readonly name: string }>>(app.client.components.listForProject({ params: { id: projectId } }))
        return new Set(rows.map((row) => row.name.trim().toLowerCase()))
      } catch {
        // A failed read must not block the save: the components are recreated and the server can reject duplicates.
        return new Set<string>()
      }
    }
    
    /** Creates the library components missing from the project; returns the ids of the ones it created. */
    async function writeComponents(app: Pick<AppState, "client" | "runtime">, projectId: string, run: Run, known: Set<string>): Promise<ReadonlyArray<string>> {
      const ids: string[] = []
      // Newest first, so a name collision keeps the most recent definition.
      for (const component of [...useLocalComponentsLibraryStore.getState().components].reverse()) {
        const key = component.name.trim().toLowerCase()
        if (key === "" || known.has(key)) continue
        try {
          const row = await run<{ readonly id: string }>(app.client.components.createForProject({
            params: { id: projectId },
            payload: {
              name: component.name,
              description: component.description ?? null,
              type: component.type,
              isPublic: false,
              data: {
                version: "1.0",
                type: component.type,
                elements: toJsonSafe(typeof component.data === "object" && component.data !== null && "elements" in component.data && Array.isArray(component.data.elements) ? component.data.elements : [component.data]),
                metadata: { elementType: component.meta?.elementType ?? component.type, savedFrom: "cad-editor", tags: component.tags ?? [] }
              }
            }
          }))
          known.add(key)
          ids.push(row.id)
        } catch {
          // One component the server refuses must not lose the rest of the project.
        }
      }
      return ids
    }
    
    /**
     * "Save as Project": creates the project, its drawing and the components. Toolpaths and materials
     * are server-side per project, so there is nothing to copy for them here.
     */
    export async function createProjectWithContents(
      app: Pick<AppState, "client" | "runtime" | "settings">,
      options: { readonly name: string; readonly description?: string | undefined; readonly organizationId?: string | undefined; readonly isPublic?: boolean },
      run: Run = app.runtime.run as Run
    ): Promise<ProjectContents & { readonly projectId: string }> {
      const project = await run<{ readonly id: string }>(app.client.projects.create({
        payload: { name: options.name, description: options.description ?? null, organizationId: options.organizationId ?? null, isPublic: options.isPublic ?? false }
      }))
      const drawingId = await createDrawing(app, project.id, options.name, run)
      const componentIds = await writeComponents(app, project.id, run, new Set<string>())
      return { projectId: project.id, drawingId, componentIds }
    }
    
    /**
     * "Save": updates the drawing this project already has and adds the components it is still missing.
     */
    export async function syncProjectContents(
      app: Pick<AppState, "client" | "runtime" | "settings">,
      projectId: string,
      drawingId: string,
      run: Run
    ): Promise<ProjectContents> {
      if (projectId === "") throw new Error("This document is not in a project yet; use Save as Project.")
      const known = new Set(await existingNames(app, projectId, run))
      const componentIds = await writeComponents(app, projectId, run, known)
      if (drawingId !== "") await run(app.client.drawings.update({ params: { id: drawingId }, payload: { data: drawingPayload(app) } }))
      return { drawingId, componentIds }
    }
    import * as THREE from "three"
    import { CSG } from "three-csg-ts"
    import { STLExporter } from "three/examples/jsm/exporters/STLExporter.js"
    import { OBJExporter } from "three/examples/jsm/exporters/OBJExporter.js"
    import { GLTFExporter } from "three/examples/jsm/exporters/GLTFExporter.js"
    import { useCADStore } from "../store/cadStore.ts"
    import { HISTORY_LIMIT, useElementsStore, type Element } from "../store/elementsStore.ts"
    import { createThreeObject } from "./createThreeObject.ts"
    import { initializeElementTransform, premultiplyObject, rotationOf, type ElementTransformFields } from "./element-transform.ts"
    
    export const HOVER_COLOR = 0x4a90e2
    export const SELECTED_COLOR = 0xff3366
    
    /** The X/Y/Z axis lines with their letter sprites. Child order matters: 0 = X, 1 = Y, 2 = Z (2D mode hides Z). */
    export function createCustomAxes(size: number): THREE.Group {
      const line = (points: number[], color: number) => {
        const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry()
        geometry.setAttribute("position", new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(points, 3))
        return new THREE.Line(geometry, new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color }))
      }
      const label = (text: string, position: [number, number, number], color: THREE.Color): THREE.Sprite | null => {
        const canvas = document.createElement("canvas")
        canvas.width = 64
        canvas.height = 64
        const context = canvas.getContext("2d")
        if (!context) return null
        context.fillStyle = `rgb(${color.r * 255}, ${color.g * 255}, ${color.b * 255})`
        context.font = "Bold 48px Arial"
        context.textAlign = "center"
        context.textBaseline = "middle"
        context.fillText(text, 32, 32)
        const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas)
        const sprite = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: texture }))
        sprite.position.set(...position)
        sprite.scale.set(2, 2, 2)
        return sprite
      }
    
      const group = new THREE.Group()
      group.add(line([-size * 2, 0, 0, size * 2, 0, 0], 0x0000ff))
      group.add(line([0, -size * 2, 0, 0, size * 2, 0], 0xff00ff))
      group.add(line([0, 0, -size * 2, 0, 0, size * 2], 0x00ff7f))
      for (const sprite of [
        label("X", [size + 1, 0, 0], new THREE.Color(0, 0, 1)),
        label("Y", [0, size + 1, 0], new THREE.Color(1, 0, 1)),
        label("Z", [0, 0, size + 1], new THREE.Color(0, 1, 0.5))
      ]) if (sprite) group.add(sprite)
      group.userData.isCustomAxes = true
      return group
    }
    
    export function createOriginIndicator(): THREE.Group {
      const group = new THREE.Group()
      group.userData.isOriginIndicator = true
      group.add(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(2, 16, 16), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffffff })))
      return group
    }
    
    /** Dolly the camera so the objects fill the view, keeping the current viewing direction. */
    export function fitCameraToObjects(camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera, target: THREE.Vector3, objects: readonly THREE.Object3D[]): THREE.Vector3 | null {
      if (objects.length === 0) return null
      const box = new THREE.Box3()
      for (const object of objects) box.union(new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(object))
      if (box.isEmpty()) return null
      const center = box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3())
      const size = box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3())
      const maxDim = Math.max(size.x, size.y, size.z, 0.001)
      const distance = Math.abs(maxDim / (2 * Math.tan((camera.fov * Math.PI) / 360)))
      const direction = camera.position.clone().sub(target).normalize()
      camera.position.copy(center.clone().add(direction.multiplyScalar(distance * 1.25)))
      camera.lookAt(center)
      return center
    }
    
    type Material = THREE.Material & { color?: THREE.Color; emissive?: THREE.Color; emissiveIntensity?: number; wireframe?: boolean }
    export type HighlightState = "none" | "hover" | "selected"
    
    const materialsOf = (object: THREE.Object3D): Material[] => {
      const material = (object as THREE.Mesh).material as Material | Material[] | undefined
      return material ? (Array.isArray(material) ? material : [material]) : []
    }
    
    /** Tints an element's object for hover/selection, remembering the original look so it can be restored. */
    export function applyHighlight(root: THREE.Object3D, state: HighlightState): void {
      root.traverse((object) => {
        if (object.userData.isControlPoint) return
        for (const material of materialsOf(object)) {
          const base = (material.userData.highlightBase ??= {
            color: material.color?.getHex(),
            emissive: material.emissive?.getHex(),
            emissiveIntensity: material.emissiveIntensity
          }) as { color?: number; emissive?: number; emissiveIntensity?: number }
          const tint = state === "selected" ? SELECTED_COLOR : state === "hover" ? HOVER_COLOR : undefined
          const intensity = state === "selected" ? 0.5 : 0.3
          const lit = material.emissive !== undefined && !material.wireframe && !(object instanceof THREE.Line)
          if (tint === undefined) {
            if (base.color !== undefined) material.color?.setHex(base.color)
            if (base.emissive !== undefined) material.emissive?.setHex(base.emissive)
            if (base.emissiveIntensity !== undefined) material.emissiveIntensity = base.emissiveIntensity
          } else if (lit) {
            material.emissive!.setHex(tint)
            material.emissiveIntensity = intensity
          } else if (material.color) material.color.setHex(tint)
        }
      })
    }
    
    export { applyElementTransform, type ElementTransformFields } from "./element-transform.ts"
    
    /** Builds the Three object of an element; `userData.pivot` is its centre in world space. */
    export function buildElementObject(element: Element): THREE.Object3D | null {
      const object = createThreeObject(element)
      if (!object) return null
      initializeElementTransform(object, element as ElementTransformFields)
      object.userData.isCADElement = true
      object.userData.elementId = element.id
      object.traverse((child) => {
        if (child !== object) {
          child.userData.isCADElement = true
          child.userData.elementId = element.id
          child.userData.isChild = true
        }
      })
      return object
    }

    export default function CustomElementPreview(props: CustomElementPreviewProps): JSX.Element {

        target = new THREE.Vector3(...(config?.camera?.target ?? [0, 0, 0]))

        const view = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(75, rect.width / rect.height, 0.1, 1000)

        view.lookAt(target)

        if (!gl) return
        gl.setSize(rect.width, rect.height)
        gl.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2))

        gl.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap
        container.appendChild(gl.domElement)

          const key = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1)
          key.position.set(5, 5, 5)
          key.castShadow = true
          key.shadow.mapSize.width = 1024
          key.shadow.mapSize.height = 1024
          built.add(key)
          const fill = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.3)
          fill.position.set(-5, 0, -5)
          built.add(fill)
