    344:export const ComponentDetailScreen = (): JSX.Element => <ResourceDetailScreen kind="components" />
    /**
     * Detail pages of the Resources lists (`/materials`, `/tools`, `/machine`, `/components`): materials, tools, machines and components, each opened from
     * its card. Legacy `pages/{materials,tools,machine,components}/[id].tsx`, in the CAM workspace style used by the
     * toolpaths detail: a glass toolbar with back and the page actions, a tab rail, a details panel with an edit form
     * (save / cancel), delete with confirmation, and a side information panel.
     *
     * Each page reads the typed client; the API contract types the shape of materials, tools and components, while
     * machine configurations are free-form (`config` JSON), so their fields are read defensively.
     */
    import { For, Show, createEffect, createMemo, createSignal, on, type JSX } from "solid-js"
    import { A, useNavigate, useParams } from "@solidjs/router"
    import { Motion } from "solid-motionone"
    import { ArrowLeft, Cpu, Layers, Package, Save, Settings, Trash2, Wrench, Box, Edit } from "lucide-solid"
    import { toast } from "../../../shared/ui/feedback.tsx"
    import { useApp } from "../../../shared/state/context.ts"
    import { invalidatePrefix } from "../../../shared/state/query-cache.ts"
    import { Modal } from "../../toolpaths/components/shared.tsx"
    import "../../../styles/workstation.css"
    
    type Kind = "materials" | "tools" | "machines" | "components"
    type Row = Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>
    
    const TOOLBAR_BUTTON = "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-white/40 dark:border-gray-600/40 bg-white/60 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 shadow-sm transition-all duration-200 hover:bg-white/80 dark:bg-gray-800/60 dark:text-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-700/80 disabled:opacity-50"
    const TOOLBAR_PRIMARY = "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#007ACC] px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-white shadow-md transition-all duration-200 hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50"
    const PANEL = "rounded-xl border border-white/40 bg-white/70 p-5 shadow-md backdrop-blur-sm dark:border-gray-700/50 dark:bg-gray-800/70"
    const INPUT = "w-full rounded-md border border-gray-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-gray-900 shadow-sm focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 dark:border-gray-700 dark:bg-gray-900 dark:text-white"
    const LABEL = "mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300"
    const TAB_ON = "border-[#007ACC] text-[#007ACC] dark:text-blue-400"
    const TAB_OFF = "border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:border-gray-300 hover:text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-400 dark:hover:text-gray-300"
    
    const CONFIG: Readonly<Record<Kind, { readonly title: string; readonly listPath: string; readonly icon: typeof Package; readonly plural: string }>> = {
      materials: { title: "Material", listPath: "/materials", icon: Layers, plural: "materials" },
      tools: { title: "Tool", listPath: "/tools", icon: Wrench, plural: "tools" },
      machines: { title: "Machine", listPath: "/machine", icon: Cpu, plural: "machines" },
      components: { title: "Component", listPath: "/components", icon: Box, plural: "components" }
    }
    
    const str = (value: unknown): string => (value === null || value === undefined ? "" : String(value))
    const num = (value: unknown): number => (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : Number(value) || 0)
    const formatDate = (iso: unknown): string => (typeof iso === "string" && iso !== "" ? new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString() : "")
    const asRecord = (value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> => (typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value) ? { ...(value as Record<string, unknown>) } : {})
    
    /** The payload each kind saves: the form values, numbers coerced, the rest passed through unchanged. */
    const payloadOf = (kind: Kind, form: Row): Record<string, unknown> => {
      if (kind === "materials") {
        return { name: str(form["name"]).trim(), description: str(form["description"]) === "" ? null : str(form["description"]), properties: {
          density: num(form["density"]), hardness: num(form["hardness"]), thermalConductivity: num(form["thermalConductivity"]),
          elasticModulus: num(form["elasticModulus"]), poissonRatio: num(form["poissonRatio"]), yieldStrength: num(form["yieldStrength"]),
          ultimateStrength: num(form["ultimateStrength"]), color: str(form["color"]) || "#cccccc"
        } }
      }
      if (kind === "tools") {
        return {
          name: str(form["name"]).trim(), type: str(form["type"]), diameter: num(form["diameter"]), material: str(form["material"]),
          numberOfFlutes: num(form["numberOfFlutes"]) || null, maxRPM: num(form["maxRPM"]) || null, coolantType: str(form["coolantType"]) || null,
          cuttingLength: num(form["cuttingLength"]) || null, totalLength: num(form["totalLength"]) || null, shankDiameter: num(form["shankDiameter"]) || null,
          notes: str(form["notes"]) || null
        }
      }
      if (kind === "components") {
        return { name: str(form["name"]).trim(), description: str(form["description"]) || null, type: str(form["type"]) || null, isPublic: form["isPublic"] === true }
      }
      // Machine configurations are free-form on the server: keep the name, type and the config object as given.
      return { name: str(form["name"]).trim(), type: str(form["type"]) || "mill", description: str(form["description"]) || null, config: asRecord(form["config"]), isPublic: form["isPublic"] === true }
    }
    
    /** The editable form derived from a stored row. */
    const formOf = (kind: Kind, row: Row): Row => {
      if (kind === "materials") {
        const p = asRecord(row["properties"])
        return { name: row["name"], description: row["description"] ?? "", density: p["density"] ?? 0, hardness: p["hardness"] ?? 0, thermalConductivity: p["thermalConductivity"] ?? 0,
          elasticModulus: p["elasticModulus"] ?? 0, poissonRatio: p["poissonRatio"] ?? 0, yieldStrength: p["yieldStrength"] ?? 0, ultimateStrength: p["ultimateStrength"] ?? 0, color: p["color"] ?? "#cccccc" }
      }
      if (kind === "machines") return { name: row["name"], type: row["type"], description: row["description"] ?? "", config: asRecord(row["config"]), isPublic: row["isPublic"] === true }
      return { ...row, description: row["description"] ?? row["notes"] ?? "" }
    }
    
    export interface ResourceDetailProps { readonly kind: Kind }
    
    export function ResourceDetailScreen(props: ResourceDetailProps): JSX.Element {
      const app = useApp()
      const params = useParams<{ id: string }>()
      const navigate = useNavigate()
      const run = app.runtime.run as <A>(effect: unknown) => Promise<A>
      const meta = CONFIG[props.kind]
    
      const [row, setRow] = createSignal<Row>()
      const [form, setFormSignal] = createSignal<Row>({})
      const setForm = (next: Row): void => { setFormSignal(next) }
      const [loading, setLoading] = createSignal(true)
      const [error, setError] = createSignal<string>()
      const [busy, setBusy] = createSignal(false)
      const [editing, setEditing] = createSignal(false)
      const [tab, setTab] = createSignal<"overview" | "details" | "raw">("overview")
      const [deleteOpen, setDeleteOpen] = createSignal(false)
    
      const load = async (): Promise<void> => {
        setLoading(true)
        setError(undefined)
        try {
          const response = await run<unknown>(fetchEffect(app, props.kind, params.id))
          // Machine configs answer the legacy envelope `{ success, data }`; the other kinds answer the row itself.
          const record = props.kind === "machines" ? asRecord(asRecord(response)["data"]) : asRecord(response)
          const normalized = record as Row
          setRow(normalized)
          setForm(formOf(props.kind, normalized))
        } catch (cause) {
          setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause))
        } finally {
          setLoading(false)
        }
      }
    
      createEffect(on(() => params.id, () => { void load() }))
    
      readonly stage: "prepare" | "plan" | "cutting-data" | "generate" | "post" | "replay" | "evidence"
      readonly operationId?: string
      readonly message: string
      /** NC line (1-based) of the finding, when it comes from the replay. */
      readonly line?: number
      readonly point?: Vec3
    }
    
    export type ClaimStatus = "passed" | "failed" | "unsupported" | "unresolved"
    
    export interface Claim {
      readonly id: "cutting-data" | "machine-limits" | "operations-complete" | "no-rapid-in-stock" | "holder-clearance" | "no-gouge" | "material-removed" | "nc-replay" | "machine-travel"
      readonly status: ClaimStatus
      readonly detail: string
    }
    
    export type ReleaseLevel = "blocked" | "preview" | "verified-program"
    
    export interface OperationOutcome {
      readonly id: string
      readonly name: string
      readonly strategy: Strategy
      readonly toolId: string
      readonly cut: CutClass
      readonly status: "ok" | "failed"

      readonly timeSeconds: number

    }
    
    export interface StockSummary {

      readonly finalVolume: number
      readonly partVolume: number
      /** Material above the part (plus allowance) that the program leaves, mm³ and its thickest point. */
      readonly remainingVolume: number

      readonly cellSize: number
    }
    
    export interface CamArtifact {

      readonly machine: { readonly id: string; readonly name: string; readonly post: PostId }

      readonly tools: ReadonlyArray<Cutter & { readonly number: number }>
      readonly nc: string
      readonly claims: ReadonlyArray<Claim>
      readonly issues: ReadonlyArray<CamIssue>
      readonly release: ReleaseLevel
      readonly stock: StockSummary
      readonly cycleTimeSeconds: number
      readonly identity: { readonly job: string; readonly part: string; readonly nc: string; readonly algorithm: string }

    import { cam, type Vec3 } from "@cadcamfun/core"
    
    export interface PlaybackSegment {
      readonly from: Vec3
      readonly to: Vec3
      readonly rapid: boolean
      readonly start: number
      readonly duration: number
      /** The tool cutting it (the toolpath's `toolId`), so the preview can colour each tool. */
      readonly toolId: string
    }
    export interface Playback { readonly segments: readonly PlaybackSegment[]; readonly duration: number; readonly end: Vec3 }
    
    export const buildPlayback = (program: cam.Program, rapidRate = 5000, tolerance = 0.1): Playback => {
      const segments: PlaybackSegment[] = []
      let from: Vec3 = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }, time = 0
      let toolId = ""
      const append = (to: Vec3, rapid: boolean, duration: number) => {
        segments.push({ from, to, rapid, start: time, duration, toolId }); time += duration; from = to
      }
      for (const path of program.toolpaths) {
        toolId = path.toolId
        for (const move of cam.expandMoves(path.moves, from)) {
          if (move._tag === "Comment" || move._tag === "Orient") continue
          if (move._tag === "Dwell") { append(from, false, move.seconds); continue }
          if (move._tag === "Cycle") continue
          const duration = cam.moveLength(move, from) / Math.max(1e-6, move._tag === "Rapid" ? rapidRate : move.feed) * 60
          if (move._tag !== "Arc") { append(move.to, move._tag === "Rapid", duration); continue }
          const origin = from, radius = Math.hypot(from.x - move.center.x, from.y - move.center.y)
          const sweep = cam.arcSweepBetween(from, move.to, move.center, move.clockwise)
          const angle = Math.atan2(from.y - move.center.y, from.x - move.center.x)
          const step = 2 * Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, 1 - Math.max(1e-6, tolerance) / Math.max(radius, 1e-6))))
          const count = Math.min(65536, Math.max(2, Math.ceil(sweep / Math.max(step, 1e-6))))
          for (let i = 1; i <= count; i++) {
            const t = i / count, a = angle + (move.clockwise ? -1 : 1) * sweep * t
            append(i === count ? move.to : { x: move.center.x + radius * Math.cos(a), y: move.center.y + radius * Math.sin(a), z: origin.z + (move.to.z - origin.z) * t }, false, duration / count)
          }
        }
      }
      return { segments, duration: time, end: from }
    }
    
    export const positionAt = (playback: Playback, seconds: number): Vec3 => {
      const time = Math.max(0, Math.min(playback.duration, seconds))
      let lo = 0, hi = playback.segments.length
      while (lo < hi) { const mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1; const s = playback.segments[mid]; if (s && s.start + s.duration < time) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid }
      const s = playback.segments[lo]
      if (!s) return playback.end
      const t = s.duration > 0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (time - s.start) / s.duration)) : 1
      return { x: s.from.x + (s.to.x - s.from.x) * t, y: s.from.y + (s.to.y - s.from.y) * t, z: s.from.z + (s.to.z - s.from.z) * t }
    }
    
    /** Colours of the tools in a preview, in the order the program first uses them; the first is the single-tool colour. */
    export const TOOL_COLORS: readonly number[] = [0x38bdf8, 0x22c55e, 0xf97316, 0xe879f9, 0xfacc15, 0xf43f5e, 0x2dd4bf, 0xa78bfa, 0x84cc16, 0xfb923c]
    
    /** Tool id -> preview colour, by first use in the program (the same tool keeps its colour across operations). */
    export const toolColors = (program: cam.Program): ReadonlyMap<string, number> => {
      const map = new Map<string, number>()
      for (const tp of program.toolpaths) if (!map.has(tp.toolId)) map.set(tp.toolId, TOOL_COLORS[map.size % TOOL_COLORS.length]!)
      return map
    }
    
    /** `#rrggbb` of a preview colour, for swatches in the UI. */
    export const colorHex = (color: number): string => `#${color.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}`
    /** One owned frame at a time. A moving camera requests another frame until damping settles. */
    export class FrameScheduler {
      private frame: number | undefined
      private suspended = false
      private closed = false
    
      constructor(
        private readonly draw: () => boolean,
        private readonly request: (callback: FrameRequestCallback) => number,
        private readonly cancel: (id: number) => void,
      ) {}
    
      invalidate = (): void => {
        if (this.closed || this.suspended || this.frame !== undefined) return
        this.frame = this.request(() => {
          this.frame = undefined
          if (this.closed || this.suspended) return
          if (this.draw()) this.invalidate()
        })
      }
    
      suspend(value: boolean): void {
        this.suspended = value
        if (value && this.frame !== undefined) {
          this.cancel(this.frame)
          this.frame = undefined
        }
        if (!value) this.invalidate()
      }
    
      dispose(): void {
        this.closed = true
        if (this.frame !== undefined) this.cancel(this.frame)
        this.frame = undefined
      }
    }

    ## Defining schemas and domain models
    
    All validation and domain modeling in Effect is done with `Schema`.
    
    **AVOID using predicates or manual parsing**, instead use `Schema` to parse untrusted data and validate it.
    
    For a comprehensive guide, see [SCHEMA.md](https://github.com/Effect-TS/effect/blob/main/packages/effect/SCHEMA.md). Make sure to read the guide in chunks, as it is a large document.
    /**
     * @title Schema basics
     *
     * Define `Schema.Class`s, decode unknown input into typed values, and
     * encode typed values back into their external representation.
     */
    import { Effect, Schema } from "effect"
    
    // Schema.Class defines both a runtime validator and a TypeScript class.
    // This is useful for domain models that should only be constructed from valid
    // data.
    //
    // The static `Type` and `Encoded` members are available when you need
    // the decoded or encoded TypeScript representation.
    export class User extends Schema.Class<User>("path/to/module/User")({
      id: Schema.Int,
      name: Schema.NonEmptyString,
      email: Schema.String,
      role: Schema.Literals(["admin", "member"])
    }) {}
    
    // `UserType` will be the type `User`, as schema classes use the class type as
    // the validated type.
    export type UserType = typeof User["Type"]
    
    // Access the encoded type with `typeof YourSchema["Encoded"]`.
    export type UserEncoded = typeof User["Encoded"]
    
    // Reuse parsers at the edges of your application instead of rebuilding them for
    // every request. Use the Effect-returning APIs when you are already inside
    // Effect code so validation errors remain typed in the error channel.
    export const decodeUser = Schema.decodeUnknownEffect(User)
    export const encodeUser = Schema.encodeEffect(User)
    
    export class InvalidUserPayload extends Schema.TaggedError<InvalidUserPayload>()("InvalidUserPayload", {
      message: Schema.String
    }) {}
    
    export const parseUserPayload = Effect.fn("parseUserPayload")((input: unknown) =>
      decodeUser(input).pipe(
        Effect.mapError((error) => new InvalidUserPayload({ message: error.message }))
      )
    )

    const summaryFields = (kind: Kind, row: Row): ReadonlyArray<{ readonly label: string; readonly value: string }> => {
      if (kind === "materials") {
        const p = asRecord(row["properties"])
        return [{ label: "Density", value: `${str(p["density"]) || "-"} kg/m³` }, { label: "Hardness", value: str(p["hardness"]) || "-" }, { label: "Yield strength", value: str(p["yieldStrength"]) || "-" }, { label: "Color", value: str(p["color"]) || "-" }]
      }
      if (kind === "tools") {
        return [{ label: "Type", value: str(row["type"]) || "-" }, { label: "Diameter", value: `${str(row["diameter"]) || "-"} mm` }, { label: "Material", value: str(row["material"]) || "-" }, { label: "Flutes", value: str(row["numberOfFlutes"]) || "-" }]
      }
      if (kind === "machines") {
        const c = asRecord(row["config"])
        return [{ label: "Type", value: str(row["type"]) || "-" }, { label: "Controller", value: str(c["controller"]) || "-" }, { label: "Status", value: str(c["status"]) || "-" }, { label: "Max spindle speed", value: str(c["maxSpindleSpeed"]) || "-" }]
      }
      return [{ label: "Type", value: str(row["type"]) || "-" }, { label: "Visibility", value: row["isPublic"] === true ? "Public" : "Private" }]
    }
    
    function DetailsView(props: { readonly kind: Kind; readonly row: Row | undefined }): JSX.Element {
      return <dl class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 text-sm md:grid-cols-2">
        <For each={summaryFields(props.kind, props.row ?? {})}>{(field) => (
          <div class="rounded-md bg-gray-50 p-3 dark:bg-gray-900"><dt class="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{field.label}</dt><dd class="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{field.value}</dd></div>
        )}</For>
      </dl>
    }

Saves, exports and version restores preserve all operations; server validation requires their composed NC to equal the stored NC and rebuilds their IR from each operation's NC. Changed aggregate NC or stock clears obsolete operation results. Existing version-1 documents without this field remain valid. API schema, sidecar persistence adapter and invalidation, desktop CAM stores/sync/save and resource detail components/screens/tests are in scope. Resource details show the stored component geometry in an interactive 3D preview, operation order, tools, parameters and original NC; previews never mutate the editor or substitute its current geometry.
    +

    import { brep, cam, creasedNormals, elementBrep as cadElementBrep, gearOutline, segmentsFor, textStrokes } from "@cadcamfun/core";
    import { useCADStore } from "../store/cadStore.ts";
    import { useElementsStore } from "../store/elementsStore.ts";
    import { useCustomElementsStore } from "../store/customElementsStore.ts";
    import { evaluateCustomElement } from "./customElements/evaluate.ts";
    import { customElementOf } from "../types/customElements.ts";
    import * as THREE from "three";
    import { initializeElementTransform } from "./element-transform.ts";
    
    interface SolidMesh { readonly positions: ArrayLike<number>; readonly indices: ArrayLike<number> }
    
    /** A solid mesh in scene coordinates as a Three mesh (the drawing's origin offset is added; smooth normals are not wanted on a CAD solid). */
    const solidObject = (mesh: SolidMesh, element: any, offset: { x: number; y: number; z: number }): THREE.Mesh => {
      // Smooth across the facets of a curved face, sharp along a real edge (a crease angle of 30 degrees).
      const shaded = creasedNormals({ positions: Float32Array.from(mesh.positions), indices: Uint32Array.from(mesh.indices) });
      const positions = shaded.positions;
      for (let i = 0; i < positions.length; i += 3) {
        positions[i] = positions[i]! + offset.x;
        positions[i + 1] = positions[i + 1]! + offset.y;
        positions[i + 2] = positions[i + 2]! + offset.z;
      }
      const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      geometry.setIndex(new THREE.BufferAttribute(shaded.indices, 1));
      geometry.setAttribute("position", new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
      geometry.setAttribute("normal", new THREE.BufferAttribute(shaded.normals, 3));
      const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: element.color || 0x4285f4, wireframe: element.wireframe || false });
      return new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
    };
    
    /**
     * An exact solid (`brep` element) as the CAD shows it: one mesh per face with the surface's exact normals (sharp edges stay
     * sharp), the B-rep edges as lines, and `userData.brepFace` / `brepEdge` on each piece for face and edge picking. Placed by
     * the same `elementBrep` the STEP export and the CAM use. Cached per element object.
     */
    /** The canvas owns editable transforms. Kernel geometry supplied to the factory includes placement only. */
    const withoutGizmo = (element: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> => ({
      ...element, rotation: undefined, scale: undefined, translateX: 0, translateY: 0, translateZ: 0
    });
    // Cache immutable geometry, never Three resources: two canvases must be able to highlight/dispose independently.
    const brepGeometryCache = new WeakMap<object, { readonly model: brep.Brep; readonly tessellation: ReturnType<typeof brep.tessellateBrep> }>();
    const brepObject = (element: any, offset: { x: number; y: number; z: number }): THREE.Group | null => {
      let cached = brepGeometryCache.get(element);
      if (!cached) {
        const placed = cadElementBrep(withoutGizmo(element));
        if (!placed.brep) return null;
        cached = { model: placed.brep, tessellation: brep.tessellateBrep(placed.brep, { chordTol: typeof element.tolerance === "number" ? element.tolerance : 0.01 }) };
        brepGeometryCache.set(element, cached);
      }
      const t = cached.tessellation;
      const group = new THREE.Group();
      const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: element.color || 0x8fa3b8, wireframe: element.wireframe || false, metalness: 0.25, roughness: 0.55, polygonOffset: true, polygonOffsetFactor: 1, polygonOffsetUnits: 1 });
      for (const face of t.faces) {
        const positions = new Float32Array(face.positions.length);
        for (let i = 0; i < positions.length; i += 3) {
          positions[i] = face.positions[i]! + offset.x;
    /**
     * Pieces shared by the toolpaths list and the toolpath detail page: the modal shell (same markup as the project
     * page's modals), the legacy display labels, and the CAM hand-off.
     */
    import { type JSX } from "solid-js"
    import type { ToolpathRow } from "@cadcamfun/api"
    import { Modal as UiModal } from "../../../shared/ui/modal.tsx"
    
    export type { ToolpathRow }
    
    export const TYPE_LABEL: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = { mill: "Mill", lathe: "Lathe", "3dprinter": "3D Printer" }
    
    export const OPERATION_LABEL: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = {
      contour: "Contour", pocket: "Pocket", drill: "Drill", engrave: "Engrave", profile: "3D Profile", threading: "Threading",
      turning: "Turning", facing: "Facing", boring: "Boring", grooving: "Grooving", parting: "Parting",
      standard: "Standard Print", vase: "Vase Mode", support: "Support", infill: "Infill", raft: "Raft", brim: "Brim"
    }
    
    /** Operations offered for each machine type (legacy form: the list follows the machine type). */
    export const OPERATIONS_BY_TYPE: Readonly<Record<string, ReadonlyArray<string>>> = {
      mill: ["contour", "pocket", "drill", "engrave", "profile", "threading"],
      lathe: ["turning", "facing", "boring", "threading", "grooving", "parting"],
      "3dprinter": ["standard", "vase", "support", "infill", "raft", "brim"]
    }
    
    export const formatDate = (iso: string | undefined): string => (iso === undefined || iso === "" ? "" : new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString())
    
    /** Modal in the app's markup: a scrim, a card with a title and a close button; Escape and the scrim close it. */
    export function Modal(props: { readonly open: boolean; readonly title: string; readonly onClose: () => void; readonly wide?: boolean; readonly children: JSX.Element }): JSX.Element {
      return (
        <UiModal open={props.open} title={props.title} onClose={props.onClose} size={props.wide === true ? "lg" : "md"}>
          <div class="space-y-3">{props.children}</div>
        </UiModal>
      )
    }
    
    /** Hands a toolpath to the CAM page: the program goes through local storage, the page reads `?loadToolpath=`. */
    export function handOffToCam(row: Pick<ToolpathRow, "id" | "name" | "data" | "gcode">, navigate: (to: string) => void): void {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem("toolpathToLoadInCAM", JSON.stringify({ id: row.id, name: row.name, data: row.data, gcode: row.gcode }))
      } catch { /* storage blocked: CAM opens without the program */ }
      navigate(`/cam?loadToolpath=${row.id}`)
    }
    /**
     * The Resources detail pages (materials, tools, machines, components) in the CAM workspace style: the overview
     * shows the record, Edit saves through the typed client, and the record is removed from the list.
     */
    import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, test } from "bun:test"
    import { bootLiveApp } from "./helpers/live-app.ts"
    import { resetStores } from "./helpers/reset-stores.ts"
    
    let app: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof bootLiveApp>>
    let projectId = ""
    let materialId = ""
    let toolId = ""
    let machineId = ""
    let componentId = ""
    beforeAll(async () => {
      app = await bootLiveApp()
      projectId = (await app.sidecar.call("/api/projects", { method: "POST", json: { name: "Resources" } })).body.id
      materialId = (await app.sidecar.call("/api/materials", { method: "POST", json: { name: "Brass Alloy", properties: { density: 8500, hardness: 80, color: "#d4af37" } } })).body.id
      toolId = (await app.sidecar.call("/api/tools", { method: "POST", json: { name: "Endmill 6", type: "endmill", diameter: 6, material: "carbide", numberOfFlutes: 3 } })).body.id
      machineId = (await app.sidecar.call("/api/machine-configs", { method: "POST", json: { name: "Haas VF2", type: "mill", config: { workVolume: { x: 508, y: 406, z: 635 }, maxSpindleSpeed: 12000, maxFeedRate: 10000, controller: "fanuc" } } })).body.data.id
      componentId = (await app.sidecar.call(`/api/projects/${projectId}/components`, { method: "POST", json: { name: "Bracket", type: "mechanical", data: {} } })).body.id
    })
    afterAll(async () => { await app.dispose() })
    
    // Each page mounts the whole shell (AI status, analytics, profile) before its own data, so give it room.
    const PAGE_TIMEOUT = 20000
    
    const settle = (ms = 80) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms))
    const find = <T extends Element = HTMLElement>(selector: string): Promise<T> => app.wait(() => document.querySelector<T>(selector), 30000)
    const toolbarButton = (text: string): HTMLElement | undefined =>
      [...document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(".cam-workspace button")].find((el) => el.textContent?.trim() === text)
    const byText = (tag: string, text: string): HTMLElement | undefined =>
      [...document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(tag)].find((el) => el.textContent?.trim() === text)
    
    describe("resource detail pages", () => {
      test("material shows its record in the workspace chrome", async () => {
        resetStores()
        const { dispose } = app.visit(`/materials/${materialId}`)
        try {
          await find(".cam-workspace")
          expect(await app.wait(() => byText("h1", "Brass Alloy"), 30000)).toBeDefined()
          expect(document.querySelector(".workstation-toolbar")).not.toBeNull()
          for (const label of ["Overview", "Details", "Raw data"]) expect(byText("button", label)).toBeDefined()
        } finally { dispose() }
      }, PAGE_TIMEOUT)
    
      test("tool shows diameter and flutes in the overview", async () => {
        resetStores()
        const { dispose } = app.visit(`/tools/${toolId}`)
        try {
          await find(".cam-workspace")
          expect(await app.wait(() => byText("h1", "Endmill 6"), 30000)).toBeDefined()
          expect(await app.wait(() => [...document.querySelectorAll("h3")].find((el) => el.textContent?.trim() === "Diameter") ?? undefined, 30000)).toBeDefined()
        } finally { dispose() }
      }, PAGE_TIMEOUT)
    
      test("machine reads its status and controller from the config", async () => {
        resetStores()
        const { dispose } = app.visit(`/machine/${machineId}`)
        try {
          await find(".cam-workspace")
          expect(await app.wait(() => byText("h1", "Haas VF2"), 30000)).toBeDefined()
          expect(await app.wait(() => [...document.querySelectorAll("p")].find((el) => el.textContent?.trim() === "fanuc") ?? undefined, 30000)).toBeDefined()
        } finally { dispose() }
      }, PAGE_TIMEOUT)
    
      test("component opens on its page", async () => {
        resetStores()
        const { dispose } = app.visit(`/components/${componentId}`)
        try {
          await find(".cam-workspace")
          expect(await app.wait(() => byText("h1", "Bracket"), 30000)).toBeDefined()
        } finally { dispose() }
      }, PAGE_TIMEOUT)
    
      test("Edit saves the material through the typed client", async () => {
        resetStores()
        const { dispose } = app.visit(`/materials/${materialId}`)
        try {
          await find(".cam-workspace")
          await app.wait(() => byText("h1", "Brass Alloy"), 30000)
          // The shell's menu bar also has an "Edit" button, so the page's own buttons are looked up inside the workspace.
          toolbarButton("Edit")?.click()
          await settle()
          toolbarButton("Details")?.click()
          await settle()
          const nameInput = await app.wait(() => document.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>("#res-name") ?? null, 15000).catch((cause: unknown) => {
            const buttons = [...document.querySelectorAll("button")].map((b) => b.textContent?.trim()).filter(Boolean).join(" | ")
            throw new Error(`no #res-name; buttons: ${buttons}; cause: ${String(cause).slice(0, 120)}`)
          })

    import { Show, createEffect, createSignal, onCleanup, onMount, type JSX } from "solid-js"
    import * as THREE from "three"
    import { OrbitControls } from "three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js"
    import { cam } from "@cadcamfun/core"
    import { Schema } from "effect"
    import { buildElementObject, disposeObject, fitCameraToObjects } from "../../cad/lib/sceneHelpers.ts"
    import { FrameScheduler } from "../../cad/lib/frame-scheduler.ts"
    import type { Element } from "../../cad/store/elementsStore.ts"
    import { buildPlayback, toolColors } from "../../../viewport/view3d/playback.ts"
    import { t } from "../../../i18n/index.ts"
    
    const record = (value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> => value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? value as Record<string, unknown> : {}
    const Elements = Schema.Array(Schema.Record(Schema.String, Schema.Unknown))
    
    /** Read saved documents, legacy geometry envelopes and direct component records. */
    export const resourceElements = (data: unknown): Element[] => {
      if (typeof data === "string") { try { data = JSON.parse(data) } catch { return [] } }
      const value = record(data), geometry = record(value["geometry"])
      const decoded = Schema.decodeUnknownOption(Elements)(value["elements"] ?? geometry["elements"] ?? (value["type"] ? [value] : []))
      if (decoded._tag === "None") return []
      return decoded.value.filter((element) => typeof element["type"] === "string").map((element, index) => ({
        ...element, id: typeof element["id"] === "string" ? element["id"] : `preview-${index}`,
        type: String(element["type"]), layerId: typeof element["layerId"] === "string" ? element["layerId"] : "default"
      }))
    }
    
    /** A read-only scene owns its GPU resources and never writes to the active CAD workspace. */
    export function ResourceGeometryPreview(props: {
      readonly elements: ReadonlyArray<Element>; readonly program?: cam.Program;
      readonly origin?: { readonly x: number; readonly y: number; readonly z: number }
    }): JSX.Element {
      let host: HTMLDivElement | undefined
      const [unavailable, setUnavailable] = createSignal(false)
      const [geometryError, setGeometryError] = createSignal(false)
      const [pathsVisible, setPathsVisible] = createSignal(true)
      let rebuild = () => {}, fit = () => {}
      createEffect(() => { props.elements; props.program; props.origin; pathsVisible(); rebuild() })
      onMount(() => {
        if (!host) return
        let renderer: THREE.WebGLRenderer
        try { renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true }) } catch { setUnavailable(true); return }
        renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2))
        renderer.setClearColor(0x111827)
        host.append(renderer.domElement)
        const scene = new THREE.Scene(), content = new THREE.Group()
        scene.add(content, new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xffffff, 0x334155, 2))
        const light = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 3)
        light.position.set(100, -100, 200); scene.add(light)
        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, 1, 0.01, 100000)
        camera.up.set(0, 0, 1); camera.position.set(100, -120, 100)
        const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement)
        controls.enableDamping = true
        const frames = new FrameScheduler(() => { const moving = controls.update(); renderer.render(scene, camera); return moving }, requestAnimationFrame, cancelAnimationFrame)
        controls.addEventListener("change", frames.invalidate)
        fit = () => {
          const center = fitCameraToObjects(camera, controls.target, content.children)
          if (center) controls.target.copy(center)
          controls.update(); frames.invalidate()
        }
        rebuild = () => {
          for (const child of [...content.children]) disposeObject(child)
          setGeometryError(false)
          const part = new THREE.Group(), origin = props.origin
          content.add(part)
          for (const element of props.elements) {
            try { const object = buildElementObject(structuredClone(element)); if (object) part.add(object) }
            catch { setGeometryError(true) }
          }
          if (origin) part.position.set(-origin.x, -origin.y, -origin.z)
          if (props.program && pathsVisible()) {
            const colors = toolColors(props.program), groups = new Map<string, { readonly color: number; readonly points: number[] }>()
            for (const segment of buildPlayback(props.program).segments) {
              const key = `${segment.toolId}:${segment.rapid}`
              let group = groups.get(key)
              if (!group) { group = { color: segment.rapid ? 0x94a3b8 : colors.get(segment.toolId) ?? 0x38bdf8, points: [] }; groups.set(key, group) }
              group.points.push(segment.from.x, segment.from.y, segment.from.z, segment.to.x, segment.to.y, segment.to.z)
            }
            for (const { points, color } of groups.values()) {
              const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry()
              geometry.setAttribute("position", new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(points, 3))
              content.add(new THREE.LineSegments(geometry, new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color, depthTest: false, transparent: true, opacity: 0.9 })))
            }
          }
          fit()
        }
        const resize = () => {
          const width = Math.max(1, host!.clientWidth), height = Math.max(1, host!.clientHeight)
          camera.aspect = width / height; camera.updateProjectionMatrix(); renderer.setSize(width, height); frames.invalidate()
        }
        const observer = new ResizeObserver(resize); observer.observe(host)
        const visibility = () => frames.suspend(document.hidden)
        document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", visibility)
        resize(); rebuild(); visibility()
        onCleanup(() => {
          rebuild = () => {}; fit = () => {}; observer.disconnect(); document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", visibility)
          frames.dispose(); controls.dispose(); disposeObject(content); renderer.dispose(); renderer.domElement.remove()
        })
      })
      return <div data-testid="resource-geometry-preview" class="space-y-2">
        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-sm">
          <h3 class="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{t("cam.resource.preview")}</h3>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Show when={props.program}><label class="flex items-center gap-1 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300"><input type="checkbox" checked={pathsVisible()} onChange={(event) => setPathsVisible(event.currentTarget.checked)} />{t("save.toolpaths")}</label></Show>
            <button type="button" class="rounded border border-gray-300 px-2 py-1 text-gray-700 dark:border-gray-600 dark:text-gray-200" onClick={() => fit()}>{t("cam.resource.fit")}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div ref={host} class="relative h-80 overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-gray-900" role="img" aria-label={t("cam.resource.preview")}>
          <Show when={unavailable()}><p role="status" class="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center p-4 text-sm text-gray-300">{t("cam.resource.noWebgl")}</p></Show>
        </div>
        <Show when={geometryError()}><p role="alert" class="text-sm text-amber-600">{t("cam.resource.geometryError")}</p></Show>
        <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{t("cam.resource.orbit")}</p>
      </div>
    }

    import { For, Show, createMemo, type JSX } from "solid-js"
    import { Schema } from "effect"
    import { CamKernelArtifact, CamProgramDocument, type ToolpathRow } from "@cadcamfun/api"
    import { cam } from "@cadcamfun/core"
    import { ResourceGeometryPreview, resourceElements } from "../../library/components/ResourceGeometryPreview.tsx"
    import { t } from "../../../i18n/index.ts"
    
    const record = (value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> => value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? value as Record<string, unknown> : {}
    const decode = <S extends Schema.Top>(schema: S, value: unknown): S["Type"] | undefined => {
      const result = Schema.decodeUnknownOption(schema)(value)
      return result._tag === "Some" ? result.value : undefined
    }
    
    export function ToolpathProgramDetails(props: { readonly row: ToolpathRow; readonly drawing?: unknown }): JSX.Element {
      const document = createMemo(() => decode(CamProgramDocument, props.row.data))
      const artifact = createMemo(() => {
        const value = decode(CamKernelArtifact, record(props.row.data)["kernelArtifact"])
        return value?.nc === props.row.gcode && value.identity.nc === cam.kernel.sha256(value.nc) ? value : undefined
      })
      const elements = createMemo(() => {
        const data = record(props.row.data), request = record(data["kernelRequest"])
        return resourceElements(document()?.drawing ?? (request["elements"] ? request : props.drawing ?? data))
      })
      const program = createMemo(() => artifact()?.program ?? (document()?.gcode === props.row.gcode ? document()?.program : undefined) ?? cam.generator.analyseGcode(props.row.gcode ?? "").program)
      const operations = createMemo(() => {
        const native = artifact()
        if (native) return native.operations.map((operation) => ({
          id: operation.id, name: operation.name, strategy: operation.strategy,
          tool: native.tools.find((tool) => tool.id === operation.toolId)?.name ?? operation.toolId,
          parameters: operation.parameters, warnings: operation.warnings, gcode: undefined as string | undefined,
          moves: native.program.toolpaths.filter((path) => path.id === operation.id).reduce((sum, path) => sum + path.moves.length, 0)
        }))
        if (document()?.gcode === props.row.gcode && document()?.operations?.length) return document()!.operations!.map((operation) => ({
          id: operation.id, name: operation.name, strategy: operation.settings?.operationType ?? "",
          tool: operation.tool ?? [...new Set(operation.program.toolpaths.map((path) => path.toolId))].join(", "),
          parameters: operation.settings ?? {}, warnings: [] as readonly string[], gcode: operation.gcode,
          moves: operation.program.toolpaths.reduce((sum, path) => sum + path.moves.length, 0)
        }))
        return []
      })
      const summary = createMemo(() => {
        const native = artifact(), saved = document(), settings = saved?.settings ?? record(record(props.row.data)["request"])["settings"]
        const params = record(settings), stock = saved?.stock
        return [
          { label: t("cam.resource.material"), value: native?.material.name ?? params["material"] },
          { label: t("cam.resource.machine"), value: native?.machine.name ?? props.row.type },
          { label: t("cam.resource.stock"), value: stock ? `${stock.width} × ${stock.height} × ${stock.depth} mm` : undefined },
          { label: t("cam.resource.feed"), value: params["feedrate"] === undefined ? undefined : `${params["feedrate"]} mm/min` },
          { label: t("cam.resource.rpm"), value: params["rpm"] },
          { label: t("cam.resource.depth"), value: params["depth"] === undefined ? undefined : `${params["depth"]} mm` }
        ].filter((field) => field.value !== undefined && field.value !== null)
      })
      return <section class="space-y-5" data-testid="toolpath-program-details">
        <ResourceGeometryPreview elements={elements()} program={program()} origin={artifact()?.origin} />
        <Show when={!elements().length}><p class="text-sm text-gray-500">{t("cam.resource.noGeometry")}</p></Show>
        <dl class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm"><For each={summary()}>{(field) => <div class="rounded-md bg-gray-50 p-3 dark:bg-gray-900"><dt class="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{field.label}</dt><dd class="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{String(field.value)}</dd></div>}</For></dl>
        <div>
          <h3 class="mb-3 text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{t("cam.resource.operations", { count: operations().length })}</h3>
          <Show when={operations().length} fallback={<p class="text-sm text-gray-500">{t("cam.resource.noOperations")}</p>}>
            <ol class="space-y-3" data-testid="saved-operations"><For each={operations()}>{(operation, index) => <li class="rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-4 dark:border-gray-700">
              <h4 class="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{index() + 1}. {operation.name}</h4>
              <dl class="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-1 text-xs text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">
                <Show when={operation.strategy}><div><dt class="inline">{t("cam.resource.strategy")}: </dt><dd class="inline">{operation.strategy}</dd></div></Show>
                <div><dt class="inline">{t("cam.resource.tool")}: </dt><dd class="inline">{operation.tool}</dd></div>
                <div>{t("cam.resource.moves", { count: operation.moves })}</div>
              </dl>
              <Show when={Object.keys(operation.parameters).length}><details class="mt-3 text-sm"><summary class="cursor-pointer text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400">{t("cam.resource.parameters")}</summary><pre class="mt-2 max-h-56 overflow-auto rounded bg-gray-50 p-3 text-xs text-gray-800 dark:bg-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200">{JSON.stringify(operation.parameters, null, 2)}</pre></details></Show>
              <Show when={operation.gcode}><details class="mt-2 text-sm"><summary class="cursor-pointer text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400">{t("cam.resource.operationCode")}</summary><pre class="mt-2 max-h-56 overflow-auto rounded bg-gray-50 p-3 text-xs text-gray-800 dark:bg-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200">{operation.gcode}</pre></details></Show>
              <For each={operation.warnings}>{(warning) => <p class="mt-2 text-xs text-amber-600">{warning}</p>}</For>
            </li>}</For></ol>
          </Show>
        </div>
      </section>
    }

    export const useCamHistoryStore = create<State>((set, get) => ({

    interface BooleanEntry {
      /** The exact elements the cached mesh was built from, by identity. */
      readonly operands: ReadonlyArray<any>
      readonly mesh: SolidMesh | null
    }
    
    /**
     * The solid a Boolean element stands for, cached on its operands.
     *
     * The store replaces an element object on every write, so operand *identity* is already a perfect
     * validity test — no need to stringify the operands to find out whether anything changed, which on a
     * mesh result meant materialising megabytes of `{x,y,z}` objects into a string on every rebuild. The
     * cache is keyed on the Boolean element itself and revalidated against the operands it was built
     * from, so it also holds no reference that would outlive the drawing.
     */
    const booleanCache = new WeakMap<object, BooleanEntry>()
    
    /** The solid a Boolean element stands for (cached on its operands), or null when it cannot be formed. */
    const booleanSolid = (element: any): SolidMesh | null => {
      const all = useElementsStore.getState().elements;
      const operands = dependencies(element, all)
      const hit = booleanCache.get(element)
      if (hit !== undefined && hit.operands.length === operands.length && operands.every((operand, index) => operand === hit.operands[index])) {
        return hit.mesh
      }
      let result: SolidMesh | null = null;
      try { result = cam.model.booleanElementMesh(all as any, element)?.mesh ?? null; } catch { result = null; }
      booleanCache.set(element, { operands, mesh: result })
      return result;
    };
    
    /** Whether a Boolean forms a solid; the canvas hides the operands of the ones that do and keeps them drawn for the ones that do not. */
    export const booleanFormed = (element: any): boolean => booleanSolid(element) !== null;
    
    /** The editor never draws a curve with more chords than this by default (an element can ask for more with `segments`). */
    const DISPLAY_SEGMENT_CAP = 256;
    
    /** The diameter in millimetres of a metric size such as "M8" (the fallback when it is not one). */
    const metricDiameter = (size: unknown, fallback: number): number => {
      const match = typeof size === "string" ? size.match(/M(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/i) : null;
      return match ? Number(match[1]) : fallback;
    };
    
    /**
     * Elements the builders below model with Y up (height on Y: a wall, a column, a stair, a coil, a bolt with its head on +Y).
     * The scene is Z-up, so they are stood up on their way out: height and axis on Z, the second horizontal direction on -Y.
     */
    const Y_UP_BUILT = new Set(["wall", "floor", "roof", "window", "door", "stair", "column", "spring", "screw", "bolt", "rivet"]);
    
    export const createThreeObject = (element: any, originOffset = useCADStore.getState().originOffset): THREE.Object3D | null => {
      const object = buildObject(element, originOffset);
      if (object === null || !Y_UP_BUILT.has(element.type)) return object;
      object.rotation.set(Math.PI / 2, 0, 0);
      // A wall's z is its base (0 = standing on the ground), so moving it by d moves it by d; everything else is placed by x, y, z.
      const z = element.type === "wall" ? (element.z || 0) + (element.height || 30) / 2 : element.z || 0;
      object.position.set((element.x || 0) + originOffset.x, (element.y || 0) + originOffset.y, z + originOffset.z);
      return object;
    };
    
    const buildObject = (element: any, originOffset: { x: number; y: number; z: number }): THREE.Object3D | null => {
        const { tolerance: documentTolerance } = useCADStore.getState();
        // Round shapes: the element's own `segments`, else enough for its tolerance (its own, else the drawing's), never coarser than 32.
        const seg = (radius: number): number =>
          typeof element.segments === "number" ? element.segments
            : Math.max(32, segmentsFor(Number(radius) || 0, typeof element.tolerance === "number" ? element.tolerance : documentTolerance, DISPLAY_SEGMENT_CAP));

      root.traverse((object) => {

      for (const material of materials) {

    }

    import { render } from "solid-js/web"
    import App from "./app/App.tsx"
    import "./styles/globals.css"
    import "./styles/ui.css"
    import "./features/dashboard/styles/dashboard.css"
    import "./features/marketplace/styles/marketplace.css"
    import "./features/library/styles/library.css"
    import "./styles/misc.css"
    import "./styles/professional.css"
    import "./styles/glass.css"
    import { ToastViewport } from "./shared/ui/feedback.tsx"
    import { Toaster } from "./shared/ui/toast.tsx"
    
    // In the desktop app the webview's console and uncaught errors go to the shell log (next to the sidecar's), where support finds them.
    // (`attachConsole` of the log plugin goes the other way, Rust to console, so the calls are forwarded here.)
    if ("__TAURI_INTERNALS__" in window) {
      void import("@tauri-apps/plugin-log").then(({ debug, error, info, warn }) => {
        const forward = (method: "debug" | "info" | "log" | "warn" | "error", write: (message: string) => Promise<void>) => {
          const original = console[method].bind(console)
          console[method] = (...args: unknown[]) => {
            original(...args)
            void write(args.map((a) => (typeof a === "string" ? a : a instanceof Error ? a.message : JSON.stringify(a))).join(" ")).catch(() => undefined)
          }
        }
        forward("debug", debug); forward("log", info); forward("info", info); forward("warn", warn); forward("error", error)
        window.addEventListener("error", (event) => { void error(`webview error: ${event.message} (${event.filename}:${event.lineno})`) })
        window.addEventListener("unhandledrejection", (event) => { void error(`webview unhandled rejection: ${String(event.reason)}`) })
      }).catch(() => undefined)
    }
    
    render(() => (
      <>
        <App />
        <ToastViewport />
        {/* Mounted once at the root so `toast.tsx` messages raised outside the
            CAD/CAM pages (assistant panel, AI settings) are visible too. */}
        <Toaster />
      </>
    ), document.getElementById("root")!)

    +test("a composite CAM resource saves and reopens every operation and displays its saved component", async () => {
    +  useElementsStore.setState({ elements: [plate], selectedElement: null })
    +  const store = useCamStore.getState()
    +  const codes = ["G21\nG0 X0 Y0 Z5\nG1 Z-1 F300\nG1 X10 F800", "G21\nG0 X10 Y0 Z5\nG1 Z-2 F200\nG1 Y10 F600"]
    +  for (const [index, gcode] of codes.entries()) store.upsertOperationResult({ id: `operation-${index}`, name: index ? "Finish contour" : "Rough pocket", gcode, program: cam.generator.analyseGcode(gcode).program, settings: { ...cam.generator.DEFAULT_SETTINGS, toolDiameter: index ? 3 : 6 }, tool: index ? "endmill Ø3" : "endmill Ø6" })
    +  const snapshot = await snapshotCamProgram(app.state, useCamStore.getState().gcode)
    +  expect(snapshot.operations).toHaveLength(2)
    +  const project = await app.state.runtime.run(app.client.projects.create({ payload: { name: "Composite resource" } }))
    +  const row = await app.state.runtime.run(app.client.toolpaths.create({ payload: { name: "Pocket and contour", projectId: project.id, operationType: "multi-operation", gcode: snapshot.gcode, data: savedProgramData(undefined, snapshot) } }))
    +  store.reset()
    +  await restoreCamProgram(app.state, row, () => true)
    +  expect(useCamStore.getState().operationResults.map((operation) => operation.name)).toEqual(["Rough pocket", "Finish contour"])
    +  expect(useCamStore.getState().operationResults.map((operation) => operation.gcode)).toEqual(codes)
    +  expect(useCamStore.getState().program.toolpaths).toHaveLength(snapshot.program.toolpaths.length)
    +  useElementsStore.setState({ elements: [{ ...plate, width: 999 }], selectedElement: null })
    +  const view = app.visit(`/toolpaths/${row.id}`)
    +  try {
    +    const details = await app.wait(() => document.querySelector("[data-testid='toolpath-program-details']"), 30_000)
    +    expect(details.querySelector("[data-testid='resource-geometry-preview']")).not.toBeNull()
    +    const items = details.querySelectorAll("[data-testid='saved-operations'] > li")
    +    expect(items).toHaveLength(2)
    +    expect(items[0]!.textContent).toContain("Rough pocket")
    +    expect(items[1]!.textContent).toContain("endmill Ø3")
    +    expect(details.textContent).not.toContain("no saved component geometry")
    +    expect(useElementsStore.getState().elements[0]!["width"]).toBe(999)
    +  } finally { view.dispose() }
    +  const edited = await snapshotCamProgram(app.state, `${snapshot.gcode}\n; changed`)
    +  expect(edited.operations).toBeUndefined()
    +  expect(savedProgramData(row.data, edited)["operations"]).toBeUndefined()
    +})

    +test("composite resources preserve ordered operations through versions and discard them after NC edits", async () => {
    +  const operations = [
    +    { id: "rough", name: "Rough pocket", tool: "endmill Ø6", settings: { ...cam.generator.DEFAULT_SETTINGS, operationType: "pocket" as const }, gcode: "G21\nG0 X0 Y0 Z5\nG1 Z-1 F300\nG1 X10 F800", program: cam.makeProgram("forged", [], {}) },
    +    { id: "finish", name: "Finish contour", tool: "endmill Ø3", settings: { ...cam.generator.DEFAULT_SETTINGS, toolDiameter: 3 }, gcode: "G21\nG0 X10 Y0 Z5\nG1 Z-2 F200\nG1 Y10 F600", program: cam.makeProgram("forged", [], {}) }
    +  ]
    +  const combined = combineCamOperations(operations)
    +  const data = { ...document, ...combined, operations, kernelRequest: undefined, kernelArtifact: undefined }
    +  const saved = await create(data, combined.gcode)
    +  expect(saved.status).toBe(201)
    +  expect(saved.body.data.operations.map((operation: { id: string }) => operation.id)).toEqual(["rough", "finish"])
    +  expect(saved.body.data.operations[0].settings.operationType).toBe("pocket")
    +  expect(saved.body.data.operations[0].program).toEqual(cam.generator.analyseGcode(operations[0]!.gcode).program)
    +  expect(saved.body.data.program.toolpaths.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2)
    +  expect((await create({ ...data, operations: [...operations].reverse() }, combined.gcode)).status).toBe(400)
    +  const version = await app.call(`/api/toolpaths/${saved.body.id}/versions`, { method: "POST", json: { data: saved.body.data, gcode: saved.body.gcode, changeMessage: "Composite original" } })
    +  expect(version.status).toBe(201)
    +  const edited = await app.call(`/api/toolpaths/${saved.body.id}`, { method: "PUT", json: { gcode: `${combined.gcode}\n; edited` } })
    +  expect(edited.status).toBe(200)
    +  expect(edited.body.data.operations).toBeUndefined()
    +  const restored = await app.call(`/api/toolpaths/${saved.body.id}/versions/restore`, { method: "POST", json: { versionId: version.body.id } })
    +  expect(restored.status).toBe(200)
    +  expect(restored.body.data.operations).toHaveLength(2)
    +  const handlers = await app.toolHandlers()
    +  await handlers.stock!(project, { action: "set", stock: { ...document.stock, width: 36 } })
    +  expect((await app.call(`/api/toolpaths/${saved.body.id}`)).body.data.operations).toBeUndefined()
    +})

    /**
     * Non-destructive Boolean elements (`boolean-union`, `boolean-subtract`, `boolean-intersect`, whose `operands` are the ids of other
     * elements) as a real solid: every operand meshed the way the CAM sees it (`element-mesh.ts`), combined with the mesh Boolean of
     * `cad/csg.ts`. The viewport draws this instead of a placeholder, and hides the operands a Boolean consumes, as every CAD does.
     *
     * Semantics match the part model: a union is every operand united, an intersection is every operand intersected, a subtraction is the
     * first operand minus all the others. An operand that is itself a Boolean is resolved first; a loop or a depth over 16 is an error
     * reported in `warnings`, not an infinite recursion. The result is a mesh (see `csg.ts` for what that does and does not mean).
     */
    import { booleanMesh, type BooleanOp } from "../../cad/csg.ts"
    import type { Mesh } from "../../cad/solid.ts"
    import { elementMesh, placeElementMesh, type Element } from "./element-mesh.ts"
    
    const OPERATIONS: Readonly<Record<string, BooleanOp>> = { "boolean-union": "union", "boolean-subtract": "subtract", "boolean-intersect": "intersect" }
    
    export const isBooleanElement = (e: Element): boolean => typeof e["type"] === "string" && e["type"] in OPERATIONS
    
    const operandIds = (e: Element): string[] => (Array.isArray(e["operands"]) ? (e["operands"] as unknown[]).filter((x): x is string => typeof x === "string") : [])
    
    /** Ids of the elements the visible Booleans consume (they are not drawn or machined on their own). */
    export const consumedOperands = (elements: ReadonlyArray<Element>): Set<string> => {
      const out = new Set<string>()
      for (const e of elements) if (isBooleanElement(e) && e["visible"] !== false) for (const id of operandIds(e)) out.add(id)
      return out
    }
    
    export interface BooleanResult {
      readonly mesh: Mesh
      readonly warnings: ReadonlyArray<string>
    }
    
    const narrow = (m: { readonly positions: ArrayLike<number>; readonly indices: ArrayLike<number> }): Mesh => ({ positions: Float32Array.from(m.positions), indices: Uint32Array.from(m.indices) })
    
    /** The solid a Boolean element stands for, or `undefined` when it cannot be formed (and then `warnings` says why through `explain`). */
    export const booleanElementMesh = (elements: ReadonlyArray<Element>, target: Element, tolerance = 0.01): BooleanResult | undefined => {
      const byId = new Map<string, Element>()
      for (const e of elements) if (typeof e["id"] === "string") byId.set(e["id"], e)
      const warnings: string[] = []
      const solve = (e: Element, trail: ReadonlyArray<string>): Mesh | undefined => {
        const op = OPERATIONS[String(e["type"])]
        if (op === undefined) {
          const m = elementMesh(e, tolerance)
          if (m === undefined) warnings.push(`${String(e["type"])}${typeof e["name"] === "string" ? ` "${e["name"]}"` : ""} is not a solid`)
          return m === undefined ? undefined : narrow(m)
        }
        const id = typeof e["id"] === "string" ? e["id"] : ""
        if (trail.includes(id) || trail.length > 16) { warnings.push("Booleans refer to each other in a loop, or are nested too deep"); return undefined }
        const operands = operandIds(e).map((o) => byId.get(o))
        if (operands.some((o) => o === undefined)) warnings.push("A Boolean refers to an element that is not in the drawing")
        const meshes = operands.flatMap((o) => (o === undefined ? [] : [solve(o, [...trail, id])])).filter((m): m is Mesh => m !== undefined)
        if (meshes.length < 2) { warnings.push(`${String(e["type"])} needs two operands that are solids`); return undefined }
        return meshes.slice(1).reduce((acc, m) => booleanMesh(acc, m, op), meshes[0]!)
      }
      const mesh = solve(target, [])
      if (mesh === undefined) return undefined
      // The Boolean element itself can be moved, turned and scaled with the gizmo like any other: its transform acts on the result.
      const placed = placeElementMesh(target, { positions: Float64Array.from(mesh.positions), indices: mesh.indices })
      return { mesh: narrow(placed), warnings }
    }
    /**
     * Where the sidecar lives: from the Tauri shell in the desktop app, or Vite env vars
     * in plain-browser dev (spec 06-desktop-ui.md §1).
     *
     * This module only *resolves* the endpoint. Building the client, checking `/health`
     * and timing out is `state/client.ts`'s job, so the boot sequence stays testable.
     */
    export interface Endpoint {
      readonly baseUrl: string
      readonly token: string
    }
    
    /** True inside the Tauri webview, where `invoke` is the only way to reach the sidecar. */
    export const isTauri = (): boolean =>
      typeof window !== "undefined" && "__TAURI_INTERNALS__" in window
    
    let cached: Promise<Endpoint> | undefined
    
    export function getEndpoint(): Promise<Endpoint> {
      cached ??= (async () => {
        if (isTauri()) {
          const { invoke } = await import("@tauri-apps/api/core")
          return invoke<Endpoint>("get_sidecar_endpoint")
        }
        const baseUrl = import.meta.env.VITE_SIDECAR_URL as string | undefined
        const token = import.meta.env.VITE_SIDECAR_TOKEN as string | undefined
        if (!baseUrl || !token) throw new Error("Set VITE_SIDECAR_URL and VITE_SIDECAR_TOKEN to run outside Tauri")
        return { baseUrl, token }
      })()
      const pending = cached
      void pending.catch(() => { if (cached === pending) cached = undefined })
      return pending
    }
    
    /** Re-resolve the endpoint (the shell restarts the sidecar on a new port after a crash). */
    export const resetEndpoint = (): void => {
      cached = undefined
    }
    
    /** Overrides the endpoint, for tests and for the browser fallback. */
    export const setEndpoint = (endpoint: Endpoint): void => {
      cached = Promise.resolve(endpoint)
    }
    
    /**
     * `fetch` for `/api/*` paths, as the legacy UI called them (`fetch("/api/projects")`), with the base URL and
     * Bearer token added. Same method/body/JSON conventions as the legacy Next.js API.
     */
    export async function apiFetch(path: string, init: RequestInit = {}): Promise<Response> {
      const { baseUrl, token } = await getEndpoint()
      const headers = new Headers(init.headers)
      headers.set("authorization", `Bearer ${token}`)
      if (init.body && typeof init.body === "string" && !headers.has("content-type")) headers.set("content-type", "application/json")
      return fetch(`${baseUrl}${path}`, { ...init, headers })
    }
    
    export class ApiError extends Error {
      constructor(readonly status: number, message: string) {
        super(message)
      }
    }
    
    /** JSON helper that throws `ApiError` carrying the legacy `{ message }` body on non-2xx. */
    export async function apiJson<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
      const res = await apiFetch(path, init)
      const text = await res.text()
      const body = text ? JSON.parse(text) : undefined
      if (!res.ok) throw new ApiError(res.status, body?.message ?? res.statusText)
      return body as T
    }

    // Use the real generated client over an in-memory HTTP transport: payloads and
    // responses undergo the same schema decoding as the sidecar, without casts.
    /**
     * Decodes a request body into a value: strings are JSON, bytes are decoded as UTF-8,
     * and a stream (what `fetch` hands a `Request` body) is drained. Anything else is
     * treated as an empty payload, matching what an endpoint with no fields would send.
     */
    const decodeBody = async (body: unknown): Promise<unknown> => {
      try {
        if (typeof body === "string") return body === "" ? {} : JSON.parse(body)
        if (body instanceof Uint8Array) return body.length === 0 ? {} : JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(body))
        if (ArrayBuffer.isView(body) || body instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
          const bytes = body instanceof ArrayBuffer ? new Uint8Array(body) : new Uint8Array(body.buffer, body.byteOffset, body.byteLength)
          return bytes.length === 0 ? {} : JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes))
        }
        if (body instanceof ReadableStream) {
          const text = await new Response(body).text()
          return text === "" ? {} : JSON.parse(text)
        }
        if (typeof body === "object" && body !== null) return body
      } catch {
        return {}
      }
      return {}
    }
    
    /**
     * The generator routes are pure functions of the request, so the browser preview runs the very same
     * `cam.generator` code the sidecar does and answers with the contract Schemas' encoding.
     */
    const generatorRoute = (kind: string, body: unknown): { readonly status: number; readonly body: unknown } => {
      const g = cam.generator
      try {
        switch (kind) {
          case "generate": {
            const outcome = g.generateToolpath(Schema.decodeUnknownSync(g.GenerateRequestSchema)(body))
            if (outcome.error !== undefined) return { status: 400, body: { message: outcome.error } }
            return { status: 200, body: Schema.encodeSync(g.GenerateResponseSchema)(outcome.response) }
          }
          case "suggest": {
            const request = Schema.decodeUnknownSync(g.SuggestRequestSchema)(body)
            return { status: 200, body: Schema.encodeSync(g.SuggestResponseSchema)(g.suggestFor(request.settings, request.geometry as cam.generator.GeometryInput)) }
          }
          default: {
            const request = Schema.decodeUnknownSync(g.ParseRequestSchema)(body)
            const analysis = g.analyseGcode(request.gcode, request.name)
            return { status: 200, body: Schema.encodeSync(g.ParseResponseSchema)({ warnings: analysis.warnings, stats: analysis.stats, program: analysis.program }) }
          }
        }
      } catch (cause) {
        return { status: 400, body: { message: cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause) } }
      }
    }

        materials: [...(options.seed?.materials ?? [{ ...common(), id: "aluminium", name: "Aluminium 6061", description: null, properties: { density: 2700, costPerUnit: 4.2 } }])],

    ## Computer Use
    
    Control native apps and browsers on the user’s computer by reading or operating UI. Prefer purpose-built connectors, APIs, or CLIs when available.
    
    - Use `cua_repl` (JavaScript) for all UI actions.
    - Do not use other technologies besides `cua_repl` for computer interactions, unless specifically requested by the user (e.g. AppleScript, `osascript`, JXA, System Events, CGEvent synthesis).
    - Prefer a dedicated plugin or skill when it can complete the task; use Computer Use for interactions that are not exposed through a more specific interface.
    - `cua_repl` state is persistent across calls
    - If you create a tab or get an app, the initial UI state is automatically included in the tool result.
    
    ## API
